| Disclosure Agreement (SDA) provisioning | §2.3 (Disclosing), §10.4 | App creates DA JSON in `v22Provisionals.disclosureAgreements` on accept of a request | SDP provisions the DA bilaterally; both parties' QS receive identical signed copies; Platform records mutual consent; sCAKs issued for the grantee | SDP |
| Evaluation Agreement provisioning | §2.3 (Agreeing), §10.5 | App creates EA JSON in `v22Provisionals.evaluationAgreements` paired with the DA | SDP provisions the EA bilaterally; consent records on-chain | SDP |
| SDA Cryptographic Access Keys (sCAKs) | (not modeled) | Not present in the prototype | SDP issues, rotates, and revokes sCAKs as needed to enforce access rights granted via the DA. Key lifecycle is invisible to users — issued, rotated, and revoked under the inherent authorization of the user on the Platform | SDP |
| Provisional → active transition | §11 (implicit), §7.4 | App flips `_showAsProvisional` flag in client state on accept | SDP transitions the DA/EA from `pending` to `active` lifecycle status; Platform notifies both parties | SDP |
| DA amendment | §11.2 | App writes `amendments[]` entry on the DA artifact in `v22Provisionals`; fires `v22-da-amendment` notification client-side (Phase 11E.2) | SDP records amendment as a lifecycle event on the existing DA; both parties' copies update via Platform push; sCAKs re-issued as scope changes | SDP |
| EA amendment | §11.2 (Amend EA modal #108 pending) | Not yet implemented in V2.2 | Same pattern as DA amendment | SDP |
| Decline retention | §11.4 | App annotates the provisional DA/EA pair with `_declineMeta` and retains in `v22Provisionals` until user dismisses | SDP transitions the DA/EA to a `declined` lifecycle status; decline reason + declining party recorded on-chain. Dismissal becomes a per-user UI preference (hide-from-view), not data deletion | SDP (state); App (UI preference) |
| Agreement expiry | §11.5 | App does not currently enforce expiry; `expires` field is informational | SDP runs lifecycle transitions on a clock; `expires` triggers automatic state change to `expired`; auto-renewal (if enabled) handled by SDP | SDP |
| Agreement revocation | §11.5a (Phase 9D) | App annotates DA/EA with `_revokedMeta` and maintains `revocationRecords` audit ledger in `v22Provisionals`; cascade to paired EA + dependent Eval Results computed client-side; dismissal annotates `_dismissedRevoked` | SDP transitions the DA/EA to a `revoked` lifecycle status; cascade rules executed Platform-side; revocation record signed and on-chain; dismissal is per-user UI preference | SDP |
| Proof-of-Evaluation DA non-revocability | §11.5a | App suppresses Revoke action labels on Proof-of-Eval DA rows; defensive guard in handler | SDP enforces non-revocability as a lifecycle constraint; App cannot construct a revocation request for these | SDP |
| Permission Consumption | (not modeled) | Not present in the prototype | SDP tracks when a grantee actually exercises their granted access (e.g., fetching in-scope evidence, running an evaluation) for audit, compensation, and usage-limit enforcement | SDP |

### 18.4 Evaluation and parsing execution

| Concern | Spec section | Prototype behavior | Production behavior | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Parse execution | §2.3 (Parsing), §11.8 | App runs `makeParseRunArtifacts(...)` client-side; AI fields pre-populated from seeded `aiValue` + `aiConfidence` | PEP runs the parse against the Asset's actual contents using the chosen Parse Template; produces structured key-value pairs; DPP registers the resulting Parse Result | PEP (compute); DPP (record) |
| Evaluation execution | §2.3 (Evaluating), §11.3 | App runs `makeEvaluationRunArtifacts(...)` client-side; AI assessments pre-populated | REP runs the evaluation against the Claim's referenced Assets using the chosen Requirements Set; produces SAT/UNSAT/MISSING/N/A per row; integrates automated + human-reviewed steps | REP (compute); DPP (record) |
| Proof-of-Evaluation (POE) artifact | §11.3, §10.6 | The prototype's Evaluation Result is a plain JSON in `v22Provisionals.evaluationResults` | The Evaluation Result *is* the POE artifact — a cryptographically signed, on-chain proof of the evaluation having been conducted, establishing the credibility of claims | REP (POE production); DPP (POE registration) |
| Human-edited indicators | §10.6 (per-row `_aiOriginalValue`) | App tracks per-row AI-original-value snapshot in client state and persists to the submitted artifact | REP returns AI-original values as part of the result payload; human-edited delta is recorded as part of the Eval Result artifact | REP / DPP |
| Supersede chain | §11.3 | App computes supersede locally: previous result's `status` flipped to `superseded`, `supersededBy` populated | SDP/REP manage the supersede chain as part of the Eval Result lifecycle | SDP / REP |
| Self-evaluation | §11.6 | App emits internal proof-of-eval DA + ownership DA via `makeEvaluationRunArtifacts`; both grantor==grantee | Same REP execution path as inter-party evaluation; internal agreements may be implicit (not separate SDP artifacts) since no cross-party consent is required — detail TBD | REP; SDP (internal agreement modeling TBD) |
| Duplicate detection | §11.3 | App detects duplicate Req Set + evidence combo client-side and blocks submission | DPP enforces duplicate detection at registration; App may pre-check for UX but is not authoritative | DPP (authoritative); App (pre-check UX) |

### 18.5 Access control and view derivation

| Concern | Spec section | Prototype behavior | Production behavior | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| View building | §6, §6.5 | App's `buildViewForActor` filters the shared dataset client-side based on actor identity, agreements, and ownership | App-side view builder *mirrors* SDP-side authorization. SDP is authoritative for what each actor is permitted to see; the App's filter is a UX layer producing views consistent with SDP-issued authorization scopes | SDP (authority); App (UX rendering) |
| Cross-canvas pull-in rules | §6.5 | App computes pull-ins client-side from active DAs/EAs in the shared dataset | SDP issues per-actor view scopes; App renders within those scopes | SDP (scope issuance); App (rendering) |
| Counterparty data visibility | §6.4 | App filters out non-disclosed Assets, EAs, Eval Results client-side | SDP enforces data segregation at the access layer; the App never receives data the actor isn't authorized to see | SDP |
| Internal Actor → Claim suppression | §6.5, §11 (Phase 9A.5) | App-side `deriveAgreementEdges` suppresses redundant ownership edges for visual cleanliness | UX-only concern; no production equivalent. Ownership remains recorded in DPP regardless of edge rendering | App (UX only) |

### 18.6 Notifications and inter-party signaling

| Concern | Spec section | Prototype behavior | Production behavior | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Notification dispatch | §7.4 | App pushes notifications via React state on every reciprocal action | Platform emits events on lifecycle transitions (SDP, DPP, REP, COP); production notification system subscribes and routes per-actor | Platform (eventing); App (UI rendering) |
| Notification types | §7.4, §11.5a | Multiple V2.2 types wired client-side (`v22-request`, `v22-accept`, `v22-decline`, `v22-da-amendment`, `v22-ea-amendment`, `v22-eval-completed`, `v22-da-revoked`, `v22-ea-revoked`, `v22-transfer-*`) | Each maps to a Platform lifecycle event emitted by the owning protocol; notification payload derived from the on-chain state transition | Platform (by protocol) |
| Reciprocal notification convention | §11 (Phase 9A.5 convention in `CLAUDE.md`) | App must explicitly fire reciprocal notifications on every party-to-party action; audit tracked as backlog #81 | Platform eventing automatically reaches both parties on any bilateral state transition; the convention dissolves | Platform |

### 18.7 Cryptographic primitives

| Concern | Spec section | Prototype behavior | Production behavior | Authority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Signatures on artifacts | §10 (opening) | None — schemas are mocked, no signatures present | All artifacts cryptographically signed by the issuing actor's DID; signatures verified at registration | DPP |
| Merkle proofs / on-chain anchoring | §10 (opening) | None | DPP anchors artifact hashes on-chain; Merkle proofs available for any artifact's lineage | DPP |
| Cryptographic Asset Keys (CAKs) | (not modeled) | Not present in the prototype | CAKs govern the data asset; public CAKs are part of a raw registered data object's identity (alongside PIN and DOT) | DPP |

### 18.8 Out-of-scope for the prototype

The following systems exist in production but are not modeled in the prototype:

- **Credit / billing system.** Prototype tracks credits in a single client-side counter (`activeRole.credits`) and charges synthetic amounts on Asset registration + Claim creation (§2.3). Production billing is a separate Platform service.
- **Identity and onboarding.** Prototype starts every demo with three actors already in place. Production includes actor onboarding, DID issuance, KYC where applicable, QS provisioning.
- **AI Shopper backend.** §9 — mock implementation only. Production requires a real LLM-backed agent, indexing of the Public Directory, and a search API.
- **Public Directory at scale.** §8 — placeholder visualization with mocked supplier clusters. Production requires a real catalog indexing system, search infrastructure, and per-Claim discoverability metadata.
- **Product Story Protocol (PSP).** Out of scope. PSP orchestrates the interpretation of a product's associations to PCN-registered authors and objects for product storytelling. The prototype's Claim construction is a simpler cousin of PSP-enabled functionality.
- **Audit log / events log.** Backlog #30 contemplates this; not in V2.2.
- **Network resilience and offline behavior.** Prototype assumes synchronous in-memory state. Production must handle partial failures, network partitions, and eventual consistency between actor App instances and Platform.

---

*End of spec. Claude Code: begin Phase 1 when Andrew approves. Work to `xhigh` effort, surface ambiguities, perform a structured review against the phase's acceptance criteria before declaring phase completion.*

---

## Changelog — Spec updates during migration

Each entry names the section updated, the phase that surfaced the deviation, and a one-line summary. The implementation is the source of truth for shipped reality; these entries record where the Round 11 baseline has been corrected.

- **§3.7 / §8 — Phase 19.1 — Per-chain slot allocation (post-pass).** Phase 19 banded ERs/PoEs relative to each chain's anchor but placed anchor Claims independently, so a second Claim could land in a prior chain's eval band (Bob's VReg Claim at `symmetricRowY(1)=+300` collided with the PRM chain's eval row at +300). Phase 19.1 makes the evaluation chain the layout unit: a new module-scoped `assignChainSlots(view)` runs as a **post-pass** at the end of `buildV22Canvas` (after all column placement) and overrides Y on chain-related nodes only. One chain per visible Claim; each chain's `dataNodes` (Claim + source Assets + counterparty `granteeAssetId` Assets) land at a symmetric slot `Y = slotIdx × SLOT_HEIGHT` (`SLOT_HEIGHT = 600`, new constant) and its `evalNodes` (Parse Results + ERs targeting the Claim + PoEs wrapping them) at `slotY + EVAL_BAND_OFFSET`. Slots distribute by Claim `createdDate` (oldest at center, alternating outward); shared endpoints resolve to the first chain by sort order (`Map.set` no-op if present), with diagonal edges into later slots. `SLOT_HEIGHT (600) > EVAL_BAND_OFFSET (300)` guarantees one slot's eval row never collides with the next slot's data row. **Field-name fixes vs the prompt:** the EA grantee asset is the flat `ea.granteeAssetId` (not `ea.grantee.assetId`), and the Claim creation date is the top-level `claim.createdDate` (not `claim.terms.createdDate`); both verified against `makeClaim` / `makeEvaluationAgreement` and probed live (the createdDate sort produced a meaningful oldest-at-center order). **Note:** every visible Claim becomes a chain (not just Claims with ERs), so catalog-heavy views (e.g. Dave) spread Claims to the 600 slot pitch rather than the 300 `ROW_STEP` pitch — a uniform consequence of the slot model; the prompt's QA #6 "Dave byte-unchanged" premise was incorrect (Dave has a proof-only-pulled ER chain, and slot allocation applies to all his Claims). Data-probe-verified: Bob PRM slot 0 (Claim Y=0, ER+PoE Y=300, Avionics Y=0) + VReg slot +1 (Claim Y=600, ER Y=900) — the Phase 19 collision is gone; Alice 8 Claims at distinct slots with each Claim's evals on its eval row; Carol 2 chains in separate slots. Footer rolls forward to v0.19.1.
- **§3.7 / §8 — Phase 19 — Node mapping cleanup: type-aware Y band for evaluation chains.** Evaluation-chain artifacts (Eval Results + PoEs) now render in a vertical band `EVAL_BAND_OFFSET = 300` below the central data row, instead of piling onto Y≈0 alongside the Assets/Claims they connect to. The four-node chain Asset → Eval Result → PoE → Counterparty Claim reads as a "hump" — Asset/Claim stay on the data row, ER+PoE sit 300 below their anchor Claim, so disclosure edges run diagonally (down then up) instead of overlapping horizontally through Y=0. **Single positioning rule in `v2_2Data.js`'s `buildV22Canvas`** chain-Y assignment: Pass 1 (anchor-Y inherit) and Pass 2 (collision search) base their Y at `anchorY + EVAL_BAND_OFFSET`; the collision search within the band biases away from center (steps further below first, only reaching back toward center when no slot is free) so additional chains stack below rather than crisscrossing the data row. The Phase 11D.4.1 proof-only-pulled ER offset (`sourceClaim.y + 300`) is now sourced from the same constant, and every independent-Y eval-chain fallback (the ER defensive fallback + the three PoE fallbacks that previously could land at/near Y=0) carries the band offset too. PoE happy-path placement is unchanged in mechanism — it inherits the wrapped Eval Result's (now banded) Y automatically. No rendering changes (V2Canvas renders whatever Y it's given); seeded scenes AND newly-created Eval Results from `Run Evaluation` pick up the band automatically. **Note:** the band is relative to each chain's anchor Claim — a chain anchored to a Claim at Y=−300 lands its ER at Y=0 (absolute), which is correctly 300 below its own anchor and sits in a different X column from the data-row nodes (no edge overlap). Footer rolls forward to v0.19.0.
- **§8.2.2 / §8.2.4 / §8 — Phase 18.3.1.2 — Umbrella outline wraps directed-DA Claims regardless of public status.** Design refinement to the Directory cluster's umbrella outline (surfaced as an 18.3.1 QA finding): Bob has Alice→Bob directed DAs + paired EAs on PRM Assembly + Voltage Regulator IC, but those Claims sat *outside* the umbrella outline on Bob's view of Alice's cluster because they're also publicly disclosed (so they bucket into `cluster.publicClaims` and were skipped from `cluster.umbrellaClaims` → outline never included them). **New behavior:** the outline wraps every Claim the active viewer has a directed DA on (public+directed included). Dot color is unchanged — public Claims stay indigo, directed-only Claims stay amber; §8.2.2's public-takes-precedence rule governs dot *color*, not outline *membership*. **Implementation:** `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` (`v2_2Data.js`) now builds a `cluster.umbrellaPerimeterClaimIds` Set per cluster — every directed-DA subject id joins it (the `publicClaimIds.has(id) continue` short-circuit that excluded public+directed Claims from `umbrellaClaims` for color now runs *after* the perimeter `.add(id)`). DirectoryLayer threads the Set into each `clusterSpec` and the outline computation filters `placedDots` by `perimeterIds.has(d.claim.id)` instead of `d.kind === 'umbrella'` (with the amber-only behavior retained as a defensive fallback when the Set is absent). The own-cluster spec + RFP-only-cluster init both carry an empty Set (shape parity → no outline). Outline visual style (amber stroke/fill, Minkowski-capsule geometry) unchanged. **Verified by data probe:** Bob's view of Alice's cluster → perimeter = 7 (PRM + Vreg public+directed + 5 Phase 17.4 umbrella-seed directed-only; EMI Shield public-only correctly excluded); PRM + Vreg still in `publicClaims` (indigo). Carol's view → perimeter = 5 (PRM + EMI public+directed + 3 directed-only). 96/98 mock clusters on Bob's view have an empty perimeter → no outline. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.3.1.2.
- **§8.14 / §8 — Phase 18.3.1.1 — Directory action-bar: hide Issue Badge, surface Reject Solicitation.** Two surgical fixes closing 18.3.1 QA #8 findings on the Directory Claim-card action bar. **(Fix 1 — `AssetNode.jsx` CLAIM branch.)** The Issue Badge `buttons.push` was unconditional for non-owners, so it surfaced on every Directory Claim card and no-op'd on click (Issue Badge dispatches through `onV22CardAction` → `onClaimCardAction` for Directory cards, but that handler isn't wired for `'issueBadge'` — no anchored Asset / parent-canvas position to attach a badge to). Gated behind a new `isDirectoryMountedClaim` check (`!!(node._directoryWarmPathRequestCandidate || node._directoryExistingEa || node._directoryRequestEaCandidate || node._solicitationContext)`) — the Directory adapter applies these stamps, the parent-canvas adapter does not — so Issue Badge stays on parent-canvas non-owner Claims (Phase 14.2 wiring intact) but disappears on Directory cards. **(Fix 2 — Directory card adapter + dispatch.)** 18.3.1 Deviation #2 stamped `_solicitationContext` at the parent-canvas adapter (`v22DataWithReveal`) + the Directory *panel* mount's `syntheticNode`, but NOT the Directory *card* synthetic node (where `_directoryWarmPathRequestCandidate` etc. land in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`), so the V22ActionBar Reject icon was never reached on a solicited Claim's dot. Added a parallel `_solicitationContext` stamp at the DirectoryLayer card adapter — inlined (not imported from V2App: wrong dependency direction) with `solicitations` in its array form (`.find` rather than the Map `.get` used by `computeSolicitationContext`), same predicate otherwise (active solicitation DA via the `_solicitationId` stamp targeting the active actor, linked solicitation still `'pending'`). **Necessary completion (beyond the prompt's literal scope):** the Directory card's action bar dispatches through V2App's `onClaimCardAction` handler (not the parent-canvas `onV22CardAction` switch), which had no `rejectSolicitation` branch — so a `rejectSolicitation` case was added there too (opens the existing `SolicitationRejectModal` via the card node's `_solicitationContext`), otherwise the newly-surfaced Reject button would itself be a dead affordance. Reject parity restored at the Directory card surface (parent-canvas card + Detail Panel footer were already correct in 18.3.1). Footer rolls forward to v0.18.3.1.1.
- **§8.14 / §8 — Phase 18.3.1 — Bob-side solicitation UI: clickable rows, context-aware Claim panel, Reject cascade.** Six surface-level parts, all reusing Phase 18.3 primitives (no new architecture). **(A)** `SolicitationCard` redesigned — the front "actor pill + party + 'solicited'" row + the dead `ActorPill` component are removed; the title becomes the Claim name with a "from {owner}" subtitle; `MessageBlock` drops its indigo `borderLeft`; and the card body (title/subtitle/message/dates) is wrapped in a clickable `<div>` (cursor pointer + 4%-indigo hover tint) wired for both viewer roles. The accepted-state pointer + action bar stay as siblings outside the clickable region so their own clicks aren't captured. **(B)** New V2App `handleSelectSolicitation(solicitation)` routes to the offered Claim via the existing `routeToPublishedClaim(claimId)` machinery (Phase 18.2.3; name is a holdover — the Claim is directed-disclosed via the solicitation DA, not "published"). Threaded through `RfpDetailPanel` (`onSelectSolicitation`) to both SolicitationCard mounts. **(C)** New module-scoped `computeSolicitationContext(claim, activeParty, das, solicitationsMap)` returns `{ solicitationId, rfpId }` when an active solicitation-DA (identified by the `_solicitationId` stamp) targets the viewer AND the linked solicitation is still `pending`; null otherwise. The `_solicitationContext` stamp is applied at both Claim-node mount paths — `v22DataWithReveal` (parent canvas, added to the `anyDecoration` gate + the per-node early-return guard + the spread + the memo deps via `v22Solicitations`) and the Directory mount's `syntheticNode`. **(D)** V22ClaimPanel footer gains a "Reject Solicitation" `FooterButton` (gated on `node._solicitationContext` + `onRejectSolicitation`), additive to the existing non-owner branches (warm-path Request EA still renders via `hasWarmPathAction`); wired on both V22NodeDetailPanel mounts to open the existing `SolicitationRejectModal`. **(E)** V22ActionBar CLAIM branch gains a `✕` Reject icon (standalone `if`, additive with the `▷` Request EA branch) + a new `rejectSolicitation` dispatch case in V2App's `onV22CardAction` switch — universal footer↔action-bar parity. **(F)** `v22-rfp-solicitation-received` notification clicks now route to the offered Claim (Directory → Claim panel) instead of the RFP marker; the four other RFP notification types (rejected/accepted/closed/reopened) stay routing to the RFP card. Reject reuses Phase 18.3's `handleRejectSolicitation` cascade-revoke; Request EA on a solicited Claim reuses the Phase 11C warm-path (a solicitation DA satisfies the `_hasActiveDaWithoutEa` predicate by structural identity). See new §8.9.6.4. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.3.1.
- **§8.13 / §8 — Phase 18.3.0.2 — Self-eval orphan check: replace `evaluatorParty` signal with synthetic-EA id prefix.** Phase 18.3.0.1's self-eval orphan bypass was applied correctly but used an ineffective signal: `er.evaluatorParty === claim.owner`. `evaluatorParty` is passed *into* `makeEvaluationRunArtifacts` (V2App:1956) as a config field, but the factory stores it as `owner`/`evaluator` on the output Eval Result artifact — it is **not** preserved as `er.evaluatorParty`. There are no `er.evaluatorParty` read sites anywhere in V2.2 (only `node.evaluatorParty`, an adapter-computed property on the synthetic card node). So `er.evaluatorParty === undefined`, the bypass returned false for every self-eval result, and the predicate fell through to the EA lookup — which (correctly per architecture) never finds the synthetic, unpersisted self-eval EA → false-orphan. **Fix:** switch the self-eval signal to the synthetic-EA id prefix `er.evaluationAgreementId.startsWith('self-eval-')`. The synthetic self-eval EA's id is built at V2App:1913 as `` `self-eval-${claimId}` `` and is the sole site in the codebase emitting that prefix (verified by grep). The check operates on the same `evaluationAgreementId` field the EA lookup below already reads, so the field is guaranteed populated on every Eval Result reaching this branch. Strict relaxation — every previously-orphaned cross-party result stays orphaned; only the self-eval case is bypassed. Side-benefit: with `isOrphaned = false` on self-eval results, the V22NodeDetailPanel footer falls through past the Dismiss-only early return into the Re-Run + Create PoE branch, restoring footer↔action-bar parity (no V22NodeDetailPanel edit needed). Footer rolls forward to v0.18.3.0.2.
- **§8.13 / §8 — Phase 18.3.0.1 — Solicitation modal polish + self-eval orphan fix.** Five items from 18.3 QA. **(1)** `SolicitationCreateModal`'s title: `Solicit with my Claim — Re: {rfp.name}` → `Solicit '{rfp.name}' with my Claim`. **(2)** `ModalShared`'s `ModalHeader` "Step N of M" pill gains `whiteSpace: 'nowrap'` + `flexShrink: 0` so it never wraps when a long title squeezes the header flex row (propagates harmlessly to every modal using the shared header). **(3)** Submit-lag observation filed as backlog #206 — no code. **(4)** the Step 1 Claim-picker row subtitle now shows three counts — `{N} referenced asset{s} • {M} parse result{s} • {K} PoE{s}` — from the `parseResultsByClaim`/`poesByClaim` props (Phase 18.3 Edit C2), so the user can see which Claims support Selective (needs parse results) or Proof-Only (needs PoEs) before Step 2; zero counts are shown, not suppressed. **(5)** the V2App `isOrphaned` predicate gains a self-evaluation bypass: a self-eval EA is synthetic and never persisted to `v22Provisionals.evaluationAgreements`, so the EA-lookup falsely flagged self-eval Eval Results orphaned (short-circuiting the Detail Panel footer to Dismiss-only, breaking footer↔action-bar parity). Fix: when `er.evaluatorParty === claim.owner` (the self-evaluation signal, stamped by `makeEvaluationRunArtifacts` as `ea.grantee.party`), the result is never orphaned — a strict relaxation that restores Re-Run + Create PoE to the footer for self-eval results. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.3.0.1.
- **§8.13 / §8 — Phase 18.3 — Solicitations carry a Disclosure Agreement (architecture + creation flow).** **(Part A — data model.)** `makeRfpSolicitation` gains a `disclosureAgreementId` link-back field. The view-builder *override* (directed-DA wins over public-DA for a Claim) and the *cluster inclusion* (the offered Claim appears in the solicitor's cluster on the owner's Directory) are delivered by **existing infrastructure** — a solicitation DA is structurally a directed claim DA, identical to a Phase 16.0/17.4 umbrella DA, so `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole`'s umbrella pass already pulls it in (cluster upserted if needed) and `buildViewForActor`'s party-scoping already excludes the conflicting public DA. No separate directed-DA passes were added (they'd be redundant + risk duplicate cluster dots); a documenting comment marks the umbrella pass. **(Part B — modal.)** `SolicitationCreateModal` is restructured from a single-step Claim picker into a 4-step flow (Required Standards + Claim → Disclosure Type → Scope + Expiry + Message → Review) mirroring CombinedResponseModal's `directoryPublishMode` primitives (DecisionCard grid sans Decline, ExpiryPicker, scope pickers, StepDots); shared bits (DecisionCard / ExpiryPicker / StepDots) come from ModalShared, and `isoFromPicker` + an SdaLine + the 3-card type set are inlined locally (no cross-modal import). Submit fires `{ rfpId, claimId, message, disclosureType, scope, daExpiryIso }`. **(Part C — handler + cascade.)** `handleCreateSolicitation` mints the DA atomically (grantor = solicitor, grantee = RFP owner, subject = Claim, `_solicitationId`/`_rfpId` stamps) and pushes it into `v22Provisionals` alongside the solicitation; the modal mount threads per-Claim `referencedAssetsByClaim`/`parseResultsByClaim`/`poesByClaim`. A shared `revokeSolicitationDa(daId, reason)` helper cascade-revokes the linked DA on solicitation reject (`handleRejectSolicitation`) and on RFP close (`handleCloseRfp` now rejects every open solicitation + revokes each DA, no double-notify). **Seed caveat:** every Claim is already public in the seed, so the cluster appear/disappear effect isn't observable for seeded Claims — the verified effects are the atomic DA mint (live: "Full Disclosure with GovCo" in Alice's EMI Shield panel), the 4-step modal, and the cascade (a revoked solicitation DA is absent from the owner's view). Owner-side UI (RFP panel rows, Claim-panel actions, warm-path Request EA, notification routing, Reject cascade UI) lands in Phase 18.3.1. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.3.
- **§8.12 — Phase 18.2.3 (Part A) — Post-publish orchestration.** On publish submit, `handlePublishToDirectorySubmit` now stamps `v22RecentlyPublishedClaimId` + fires `routeToPublishedClaimRef.current(claim.id)`. New `routeToPublishedClaim` callback (mirror of `routeToRfp`) closes the parent selection, opens the Directory, sets the selected Directory Claim (auto-opens its Detail Panel), and runs a cancellable 240-frame rAF retry loop calling DirectoryLayer's new imperative `selectClaim(claim, { zoom: 0.6 })`. `selectClaim` resolves the dot via `layoutRef.allDots.filter(kind !== 'rfp')` + a new `focusClaimInternal` helper (pin + pan/zoom with panel-offset). The matching synthetic Claim card is stamped `_isNew` (NEW badge, renders at card-LOD); a clear-on-deselect effect drops the flag when the selected Directory Claim is no longer the published one. 60% landing keeps cluster context (the dot reads as a dot at that LOD). Mirrors the RFP arc's 17.5.1 → 17.5.2 split. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.3.
- **§8.12 — Phase 18.2.3 (Part B) — Already-published gate.** `v22DataWithReveal` stamps `_publishedToDirectory: true` on owned Claim nodes whose Claim already has an active Radiant-Network-grantee DA (same predicate shape as §18.2.1.1). The V22ClaimPanel footer "Publish to Directory" button and the V22ActionBar CLAIM-branch button both read the flag and render disabled with an explanatory tooltip ("…Revoke the existing Disclosure Agreement in the Agreements section to republish."). `ActionButton` gained `disabled` support (muted, `cursor: not-allowed`, inert onClick) so the action-bar parity works. The flag clears reactively on revoke (`v22View` recomputes from `v22Provisionals`); a defensive no-op gate in the `publishToDirectory` dispatch case guards forced/stale clicks.
- **§3 / parse modal — Phase 18.2.3 (Part C) — Parse Asset rename + iframe fallback.** The Parse modal title + entry-point labels rename "Parse Evidence" → "Parse Asset" (the internal dispatch action name `parseEvidence` is unchanged). `AssetFileViewer` (in `AssetEvidencePanel.jsx`) now guards the plain-iframe path: when an Asset's `file.localPath` resolves to the SPA `index.html` fallback (a missing physical PDF — detected on `onLoad` by sniffing the loaded document for the app root `#root` / the app title) or the iframe errors, it swaps to a "No preview available." placeholder instead of rendering the whole app inside the frame. Real PDFs render unchanged.
- **§8.12 / §8 — Phase 18.2.2 — Retire `PublishToDirectoryModal`, reuse `CombinedResponseModal`.** The bespoke publish modal shipped in 18.2 is deleted; the Publish-to-Directory flow now runs through `CombinedResponseModal` with a new `directoryPublishMode` prop. The mental model: publishing is "disclose to the Radiant Network as a non-human auto-accepting grantee" — structurally identical to the cold-path Disclosure Response (§7.1) minus the EA half. The prop branches the existing 4-step flow into **3 steps (Type → Scope → Review)**: `totalSteps` short-circuits to `3`; the Step 1 grid drops the Decline card (4 cols → 3); the Step 3 EA-Terms render is gated `&& !isDirectoryPublish`; the Step 4 review render condition moves from `step === 4` to `step === totalSteps` (matching both flows); the review hides the EA-Expires + Acknowledgments rows; the banner, header titles/subtitles, and scope-step descriptions branch to publish-specific copy; and `handleSubmit` fires `onAccept({ type, scope, daTerms: { expires } })` (DA-only, no `eaTerms`). V2App swaps the mount to `CombinedResponseModal` (synthesized `request` with active actor as `ownerParty`, `'Radiant Network'` as `requesterParty`, `proposedEaTerms: null`; same `referencedAssets`/`parseResults`/`poesForClaim` contract as the cold-path mount) and renames the handler's payload field (`payload.expiryIso` → `payload.daTerms?.expires`). Parity-by-construction: improvements to the response modal now reach the publish surface. Post-publish orchestration is renumbered 18.2.1 → 18.2.3 (still deferred). Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.2.
- **§10.4 / §8 — Phase 18.2.1.1 — V2App PoE picker filter hotfix.** Single-symbol fix at two parallel V2App modal-mount sites: both the `CombinedResponseModal` Proof-Only step (`poesForClaim`) and the `AmendDisclosureModal` Proof-Only scope picker (`candidatePoEs`) pre-filtered PoEs with `poe.claimId === <claim> && poe.owner === da.grantor.party`. The `&& poe.owner === da.grantor.party` clause was wrong: a proof-only Claim DA carries `subject.kind === 'claim'` + `scope.poeIds`, and the grantor's right to grant proof-only disclosure derives from their ownership of the **Claim**, not the PoE — so any PoE *visible to the grantor on that Claim* (including third-party-authored PoEs that reached the Claim owner via the original proof-of-evaluation DA from evaluator → claim owner) is a valid scope candidate. The clause excluded every third-party-authored PoE (the common evaluator-to-claim-owner case — e.g. Carol's PoE on Alice's EMI Shield Claim) and only matched self-evaluation PoEs, so the Proof-Only picker showed the empty state. Fixed by dropping the clause at both sites (filter by Claim membership only). This matches the seed precedent (Phase 17.5.0.4 mock-supplier proof-only Directory DAs publish third-party-authored PoEs) and Phase 18.2's correctly-wired `PublishToDirectoryModal` filter — the two sites here were pre-PoE-era code that survived the Phase 13 migration with stale ownership semantics. The new filter is a strict superset of the old, so the self-evaluation case (`owner === grantor`) still works. No view-builder, modal, or data-model changes (the modal picker rows already show `by {owner}` for evaluator attribution). Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.1.1.
- **Phase 18.2.1 — CombinedResponseModal hotfix (Step 1 button gating + Proof-Only copy parity).** Two surgical fixes surfaced during Phase 18.2 QA; both bugs predate 18.2 (the modal is Phase 11/13-era). **Bug 1 (P0 crash):** the cold-path `totalSteps` formula `(isDecline ? 2 : action ? 4 : 1)` collapsed to `1` at Step 1 before a disclosure type was picked, so `step === totalSteps` evaluated true at Step 1 and rendered the final-step (ungated) "Accept" button instead of the disabled "Continue". Clicking it fired `handleSubmit()` with `action === null`, propagating null `type` + `scope` into V2App's `handleV22Accept` and crashing downstream. Fixed by hardcoding the non-decline cold path to `4` (`(isDecline ? 2 : 4)`): StepDots now reads "Step 1 of 4" from open, Continue renders gated by the pre-existing `canAdvanceFromStep1 = action != null`, and Accept only renders at the true final step (`step === 4`). **Bug 2 (copy regression):** the Step 2 Proof-Only branch's surrounding copy still referenced "Evaluation Results" (pre-Phase-13 — the picker itself was migrated to PoEs in #168). Swept the FieldLabel ("Select Proofs of Evaluation to share"), the description, the empty-state copy + styling (now neutral `var(--bg-card)`/`var(--border)`/`var(--text-dim)` matching the Selective empty state byte-for-byte, copy "No Proofs of Evaluation available. Proof-Only Disclosure requires at least one Proof of Evaluation on this Claim."), and the Step 4 review row label ("Proofs of Evaluation shared"). No data-model, handler, or prop changes. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.1.
- **§8.12 / §8 — Phase 18.2 — Publish Claims to the Public Directory (foundation).** Restores the V2.0/V2.1 publish-to-Radiant-Network mechanic lost in the V2.2 Directory rebuild. New two-step `PublishToDirectoryModal` (Form → Review, cloned from `RfpCreationModal` scaffolding): a Full/Selective/Proof-Only disclosure-type picker (Proof-Only disabled with `cursor: not-allowed` + tooltip when the Claim has no PoEs) + a scope picker that branches on the picked type + an `ExpiryPicker` (default "Never expires"). Entry points (owner-only): the V22ClaimPanel footer ("Publish to Directory", icon `⊕`) + the V22ActionBar CLAIM branch (`onV22CardAction('publishToDirectory')`) — per the universal rule that footer actions mirror to the action bar. On Submit, `handlePublishToDirectorySubmit` mints a Disclosure Agreement via `makeDisclosureAgreement` with `grantee = { party: RADIANT_NETWORK_PARTY, dot: RADIANT_NETWORK_DOT }`, `subject: { kind: 'claim', id }`, the picked `type` (NOT `'provisional'`) + discriminated `scope` (Full → `assetIds`; Selective → `assetIds` + composite `fieldIds` `${parseResultId}::${fieldId}`; Proof-Only → `poeIds`), `status: 'active'`, and pushes it into `v22Provisionals.disclosureAgreements`. **No new merge helper, session-state Map, or builder signature change** — `mergeProvisionals` merges the DA by id and `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole`'s existing public-DA detection (`grantee.party === 'Radiant Network'` + `subject.kind === 'claim'` + `type !== 'provisional'`) surfaces the Claim as a dot in the active actor's own cluster on next render. Radiant Network is an auto-accepting grantee (a directory, not an actor) → no provisional/accept handshake, unlike a party-to-party DA. Post-creation orchestration (auto-route to Directory + variant load animation + auto-select + NEW badge) is deferred to Phase 18.2.1. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.
- **Phase 18.1 — Placeholder RELEASE action on Internal Disclosure Agreement rows.** Every Internal DA row (`isInternal && !isRevoked`) in the shared `<AgreementsSection>` now renders a muted, disabled `<ActionLabel label="Release">` in the AMEND slot of `DisclosureAgreementRow` — across Actor / Asset / Claim / Eval Result / Parse Result Detail Panels (single-source change; no prop-chain plumbing). The tooltip noun resolves from the DA's own `subject.kind` via a new module-scoped `DA_SUBJECT_KIND_LABEL` map (`asset → 'Asset'`, `claim → 'Claim'`, `evalResult → 'Evaluation Result'`, `parseResult → 'Parse Result'`, `poe → 'Proof of Evaluation'`, `'artifact'` defensive fallback) — NOT the panel's node type, so it accurately describes what releasing *that* agreement would affect (e.g. an Asset panel's claim-ref internal DA reads "...to this Claim..."). Copy: "Releasing your disclosure agreement to this [noun] will remove it from your network. Coming soon." `showRelease` and `showAmend` are mutually exclusive by construction (`showAmend` requires `!isInternal`; `showRelease` requires `isInternal`), so they share the slot-1 ternary; the REVOKE slot is untouched, and row geometry (two stacked slots, ~32px) is unchanged. Disabled-only surface — no `onClick`, no V2App handler, no `onRelease` callback, no data-model changes; previews the eventual "release this artifact from my network" mechanism (handler + wiring land in a later phase). Footer rolls forward to v0.18.1.
- **Phase 18.0.1 — Hotfix: sticky edge tooltip on Parent → Directory transition (closes #203).** Diagnosed root cause: V2Canvas's `onMouseMove`-bound `handleEdgeHover` had no Directory-open guard — only the existing `transitioningRef.current` check for V2Canvas-internal dive/surface transitions. After Phase 17.4.3's `clearEdgeUiState` synchronously cleared all three edge-tooltip state layers on the Directory-open transition, mousemove events firing during the ~600ms clip-path wipe (and after, while V2Canvas stays mounted underneath) re-raycasted parent-canvas edges, re-fired `onEdgeHover({ edgeId, sdaType, x, y })`, and re-set V2App's `edgeHover` state — re-rendering the `EdgeHoverMenu` in hover mode (portaled to document.body at z-index 6000, above the Directory). Fix: (1) new `directoryOpen` prop on V2Canvas; early-return guards added to `handleEdgeHover` and `handleBackgroundClick` next to the existing `transitioningRef` check (both useCallback dep arrays updated); new `useEffect` clears V2Canvas's internal hover state (`hoveredEdge`/`edgeTooltipPos`/`hoveredEdgeSdaType`) + cancels the pending 150ms `edgeHideTimeout` debounce on the `directoryOpen` false→true flip. (2) V2App passes `directoryOpen={v22DirectoryOpen}` to V2Canvas; defensive `!v22DirectoryOpen` gate on both `EdgeHoverMenu` mount conditionals (hover-mode + pinned-mode); `clearEdgeUiState` extended to also `setEdgeMenuPanning(false)` for completeness — the prompt referenced a `setEdgeMenuProjected` setter, but no such state exists (the pinned menu re-projects `edgeMenu.anchor` in place via a fade effect keyed on `edgeMenu?.edgeId`, with `edgeMenuPanning` as the fade flag, already reset to false when `edgeMenu` goes null), so this is the real-world belt-and-suspenders equivalent. All four+ Directory-open paths (globe toggle, Radiant-Network double-click, RFP-notification routing, role switch, `routeToRfp`, `routeToAsset` exit) already call `clearEdgeUiState`; the new V2Canvas guard makes the system robust even to a missed clear. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.0.1.
- **Phase 18.0 — Demo prep quick wins.** Four S-effort polish items bundled as the first demo-prep pass (Phase 17's RFP creation arc is fully closed; Phase 18 opens as the demo-prep umbrella). (1) Selection ring centering fixed on `AssetNodeDot`'s `isSelected` branch — the amber ring's `top:-6/left:-6` was measured from the inner dot's *padding edge* (inside its 1.5px border), rendering ~1.5px down-and-right of center; switched to `top:50%/left:50%` + `translate(-50%,-50%)`, which is immune to the border offset (verified concentric, 0px offset). (2) `V22AssetPanel` "View File" button unified across owner + non-owner branches — both now render the large full-width button (formerly only on the non-owner branch, labeled "Open Evidence Viewer"); label changed to "View File"; the small expand-icon header affordance is removed from the owner branch (its owner-only file metadata rows are preserved per Phase 11D #135). Deduped via a shared `viewFileButton` const. (3) Amendment factory `makeAmendedClaim` in `v2_2Data.js` stamps `_isNew: true` + a numeric `_createdAt` on new claim-ref DAs when a Claim is amended to add Asset references; `deriveAgreementEdges` propagates both onto the derived edge so V2Canvas's existing edge-draw animation animates the new edges in (closes backlog #35). Because the claim-ref DA is a *permanent* internal DA, the amend handler clears the reveal flags ~1.1s after the draw-in (otherwise the edge keeps rendering with the brighter/thicker "new" styling) — scoped to the just-added DA ids, no cross-impact on the provisional-edge reveal path. (4) Agreements section extended from V22ActorPanel/V22AssetPanel/V22ClaimPanel (Phase 9C) to **V22EvalResultPanel** (filters DAs by `subject.kind === 'evalResult' && subject.id === node.id`) and **V22ParseResultPanel** (filters by `subject.kind === 'parseResult'`, which yields empty in V2.2 since Parse Results flow through their parent Asset's disclosure — the section renders nothing, matching the 9C empty convention of returning `null`). Reuses the Phase 9C `AgreementsSection` + row components, no new primitives; the existing `disclosureAgreementsForNode` prop pipeline (and `{...props}` router spread) carries the filtered list (closes backlog #116). Footer rolls forward to v0.18.0; Phase 17 is officially wrapped and Phase 18 opens as the demo-prep umbrella.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.2.2 — RFP arc post-closure cleanup.** Four items. (1) `handleRequestAgreement` resolves the target RFP against the full created → closed → removed merge chain — mirror of the 17.5.1.3 `handleCreateSolicitation` fix; the bare `buildV22SharedArtifacts()` call was missing the session-state overlays and bailed at `if (!rfp) return` for user-created RFPs (deps array updated `[]` → `[v22CreatedRfps, v22ClosedRfpIds, v22RemovedRfpIds]`). (2) `selectRfp` imperative handle gained an optional `opts.zoom` parameter; the default `Math.max(zoomRef.current, LOD_THRESHOLD)` is preserved when callers pass no opts (`routeToRfp` + internal click paths stay at full-card LOD). Notification routing now passes `{ zoom: 0.6 }` so RFP notification clicks land at 60% (asymmetric on purpose — the post-creation flow's full-card LOD is the user's deliberate-focus intent; notification clicks are reactive and want surrounding context, mirroring 17.5.2.1's `DIRECTORY_EXIT_ZOOM = 0.6`). (3) `handleReopenRfp` now dispatches a `v22-rfp-reopened` notification to every UNIQUE solicitor party — **REVERSES 17.5.1.4's "Reopen is silent (asymmetric)" decision** (see the §8.11 note below). Symmetric with `handleCloseRfp`'s dispatch shape; click routes the same way as `v22-rfp-closed` (added to the notification-routing type union, lands at 60%). New notification render branch with accent-indigo full-saturation badge (vs the closed badge's muted blend) + `RFP REOPENED` label + "{owner} reopened the RFP … You can resume engagement." body. (4) Directory zoom controls (`top: 73, right: 12` in DirectoryLayer's chrome JSX) gained a panel-aware right offset: `right: detailPanelOpen ? PANEL_W + 12 : 12` with a 200ms ease-out transition. V2App computes `!!(v22DirectorySelectedClaim || v22DirectorySelectedRfp)` and passes it as the new `detailPanelOpen` prop. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.2.2.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.2.1 — RFP arc closing polish (regression fix + transition smoothing + visual cleanup).** Six bundled items. (1) Tab-crash regression on the 17.5.1.5 Anchored RFPs row click fixed — root cause was an uncancelled rAF select-retry chain in `routeToRfp`: 17.5.2 raised the chain's cap 60 → 240 frames (~4s @ 60fps), and because the chain was never cancelled when `routeToRfp` was re-invoked, repeated RFP navigation (Anchored-RFPs click → For-Asset round trip → click again, rapid re-clicks) stacked multiple concurrent 4-second chains, each filtering the ~23k-entry `allDots` every frame and firing `setPinned`/pan-zoom on resolution — saturating the main thread and freezing the tab. Fix: the in-flight chain's rAF id is held in `routeRfpSelectRafRef` and cancelled at the start of `routeToRfp` (and zeroed on terminate), so only one chain is ever active; `routeToAsset` cancels it too on the way back to Parent. (2) `routeToAsset` / the Directory close path extended to animate the Directory camera zoom outward (current → `DIRECTORY_EXIT_ZOOM` ≈ 0.6, `Math.min` so it's outward-only) over the 900ms reverse circle wipe, in parallel with the Parent canvas pre-positioning that was already happening. Driven by a `phase === 'out'` effect in DirectoryLayer, so it applies to every close path (globe button, exit chip, empty-space double-click, For-Asset route). Net effect: a smooth "pull-out through the network → arrive at the Asset" transition. (3) `AssetPanelRfpRow` right-region restructured: icons dropped from CLOSE/REOPEN/REMOVE (text-only labels); REOPEN+REMOVE stack vertically when both render (closed RFP), matching the AMEND/REVOKE EA row treatment (flex column, flex-end, gap 4, minWidth 52); REMOVE keeps its `danger` red. (4+5) `ASSET` and `ACTOR` type badges added to their respective Detail Panel headers (passed as `PanelHeader typeLabel`), matching the existing badge convention used by CLAIM/EVAL RESULT/PARSE RESULT/PoE/BADGE TEMPLATE — reversing the 17.3 #202 removal for these two panels. (6) Changelog modal header copy updated: "Prototype Changelog" → "Changelog", "Radiant V2 — PCN Prototyping" → "Radiant v2.2". Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.2.1.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.2 — Post-creation orchestration + bidirectional Parent↔Directory navigation (closes the RFP creation arc).** Three navigation/transition pieces. (1) `handleRfpCreationSubmit` extends post-add to fire `routeToRfp(newRfp.id, { rfp: newRfp })` and stamp `v22RecentlyCreatedRfpId` for the `_isNew` reveal flag. Because `routeToRfp` already zooms to `LOD_THRESHOLD` (full-card LOD) and auto-selects the marker, no extra zoom plumbing is needed; an `opts.rfp` fallback was added since `setV22CreatedRfps` hasn't flushed at submit time (the merged lookup can't yet resolve the new RFP, so the freshly-built object is passed through). The reveal flag is threaded to DirectoryLayer (`recentlyCreatedRfpId` prop) and stamped as `_isNew` on the synthetic RFP card; AssetNode's RFP-category branch renders the NEW badge (mirroring the Parent layer's `_isNew` pattern from Phase 11D). A clear-on-deselect effect drops the flag when `v22DirectorySelectedRfp` is no longer the new RFP. (2) RfpDetailPanel's "For Asset" row (Phase 17.2.1.1) gains a clickable affordance (hover tint + pointer + chevron) routed through the new `routeToAsset` helper — closes the RFP/Claim Detail Panels + the Directory layer (reverse wipe), then selects + pans/zooms to the Asset on the Parent canvas (mirrors `handleViewEa`'s close-then-pan pattern). Inverse of the click-to-open-RFP routing from 17.5.1.5. (3) DirectoryLayer's canvas container gains an `onDoubleClick` handler that fires `onClose` (existing reverse circle wipe) when the dblclick hits empty canvas space — gated on `phase === 'in'` (no-op during transitions), `e.target === renderer.domElement` (HTML overlays — cards, cluster-label pillboxes, chrome — are different elements), and a `raycast` miss (a marker hit belongs to the marker). With this phase, the RFP creation arc (17.5 → 17.5.0.x → 17.5.1 → 17.5.1.x → 17.5.2) is closed. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.2.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.1.5 (Asset Detail Panel "Anchored RFPs" section).** New section in V22AssetPanel listing every RFP whose `assetId` matches the active Asset, sourced from the post-merge `shared.rfps` (created → closed → removed merge chain, so created, closed, and removed RFPs all reflect correctly). Each row exposes click-to-navigate (closes the Asset Detail Panel via `setSel(null)`, opens the Directory layer, pans to the RFP marker, opens its Detail Panel — same routing as the `v22-rfp-solicitation-received` notification click, extracted into a reusable `routeToRfp(rfpId)` helper). Owner-side lifecycle actions render inline on each row via `ActionLabel` (matching the DA/EA revoke-button visual): Close (open RFPs) or Reopen + Remove (closed RFPs; Remove is a `danger` ActionLabel). Non-owner viewers see the row identity (name + status pill + posted date) + click-to-navigate but no action buttons. The Anchored RFPs section is the parent-canvas surface for managing RFPs — they don't otherwise appear on the parent canvas (Directory-layer only). Sorted newest-first by `createdDate`; section always renders (even empty, with a muted "No RFPs anchored to this Asset." line) so the feature is discoverable. The row lifecycle handlers reuse `handleCloseRfp` / `handleReopenRfp` / `handleRemoveRfp` (Phase 17.5.1.4), so Close from a row fires solicitor notifications and Remove silently cascades. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.1.5.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.1.4 (RFP lifecycle expansion — Close notifications + Remove + Detail Panel refresh + placeholder dimming).** Four bundled items. (1) RFP Detail Panel re-derives its rfp prop from the current view's `shared.rfps` on every render — the mount's merge chain now includes `mergeCreatedRfps` (+ `mergeRemovedRfps`) so a user-created RFP is present in the merged set and its Close/Reopen status flips in-place without deselect/reselect (the seeded path already worked; created RFPs were the gap, since the prior chain omitted `mergeCreatedRfps` and fell back to the captured snapshot). (2) RfpCreationModal name + description placeholder color dropped to a clearly-muted blend (`color-mix(in srgb, var(--text-muted) 70%, transparent)`) via a scoped `::placeholder` rule. (3) New `v22-rfp-closed` notification type dispatched to every UNIQUE solicitor party (deduped) when the RFP owner clicks Close; zero solicitors → zero notifications; Reopen is silent (asymmetric). Click routes Directory → closed RFP marker → Detail Panel, mirroring §8.9.6's solicitation-received routing (the routing branch + RFP lookup now run the full created→closed→removed merge chain). Muted-indigo badge. Close logic centralized in `handleCloseRfp` so the panel footer + card action bar fire the same notification side-effect. (4) New `mergeRemovedRfps` export in `v2_2Data.js` filters removed RFPs out of `shared.rfps` AND cascades to drop associated `rfpSolicitations`. `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` signature extended: `(roleId, provisionals, closedRfpIds, createdRfps, removedRfpIds)` — merge chain order is provisionals → created → closed → removed (so a same-session create-close-remove sequence works). V2App's `handleRemoveRfp` is silent (no notification) and clears the Directory selection if the removed RFP was open; Remove is only available on RFPs in `'closed'` state, enforced at both the Detail Panel footer (danger `ActionButton`) and the action bar (`_directoryRemoveCandidate` stamp → trash-icon ActionButton). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.1.4.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.1.3 (Create RFP cleanup III — solicitation regression + RS Detail Modal subheader + closed-RFP visual).** Three 17.5.1.2 QA fixes. (1) **Solicitation submission regression (user-created RFPs).** `handleCreateSolicitation` resolved the target RFP via `mergeClosedRfps(buildV22SharedArtifacts(), v22ClosedRfpIds)` — omitting `mergeCreatedRfps`, so a user-created RFP wasn't in the lookup set and the handler bailed at `if (!rfp) return`, silently no-op'ing Submit. Fix: thread `mergeCreatedRfps(..., v22CreatedRfps)` into the lookup chain (+ deps). The seeded-RFP "Submit doesn't fire" case is the documented Phase 17.2.1.1 §8.9.6.1 Claim-greying predicate working as intended (Claims already on the buyer's network render greyed, are unselectable, and gate Submit) — no code change; the affordance is already communicated via the "ON NETWORK" pill + not-allowed cursor + tooltip. Fix 1.A (inline amber explanation) was skipped: a greyed Claim can't be selected (its onClick is gated to `undefined`), so the "greyed Claim in selection" state the line would explain is unreachable. (2) **RequirementsSetDetailModal subheader.** Branched on publication state: published → globe + "Published by {owner}"; unpublished (RFP-referenced-but-not-on-network RSes reaching the modal via `v22RfpRsLookupPool`) → plain "by {owner}" with no globe. `isPublished` + `owner` are read off the enriched pool entry (Phase 17.5.1.2) with `_publishedBy`/membership fallbacks. Stops the prior "Published by an unknown actor" misframing for private RSes. (3) **Closed-RFP Directory marker.** Converted the closed-RFP marker from a 1px `THREE.LineSegments` (`LineDashedMaterial` — WebGL ignores `linewidth`, so it read as a hairline next to open RFPs' ~2px ring) to a thick dashed ring built from filled dash quads (`buildClosedRfpRingDashes` → `THREE.Mesh` + `MeshBasicMaterial` vertexColors, DoubleSide). Border thickness matches the open ring (`RFP_BORDER_SCREEN_PX / zoom` world units); the dash pattern stays as the lifecycle indicator; stroke color is a 50/50 muted-indigo blend (`--accent-indigo` lerped toward `--text-muted`). The hover/select per-vertex brightening was generalized from the hardcoded 8-vertex assumption to the build's `vertsPerRfp`. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.1.3.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.1.2 (Create RFP cleanup II — cluster integration + RS click + RS row layout).** Three 17.5.1.1 QA fixes. (1) DirectoryLayer (via `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole`) classifies created RFPs into the owner's existing cluster RFP marker pool, not the orphan pool, when the owner has a visible cluster on the viewer's Directory; for RFP owners without a Claim cluster (e.g. buyers like GovCo) an RFP-only cluster is upserted — the primary-party carve-out (`PRIMARY_PARTIES_FOR_ORPHAN_RFP`) is removed. The 17.5.1.1 orphan-spread fallback is preserved for genuine clusterless orphans. Eliminates the duplicate cluster label that appeared on non-creator views (two same-owner orphan markers each rendered their own owner pillbox label). Data probe verified: a created GovCo RFP joins the GovCo cluster (with the seeded Sentinel-4 RFP) and `otherRfps` is empty on Alice/Carol/Dave views; a created MicroCo RFP joins MicroCo's existing claim cluster on Bob's view; exactly one cluster per owner. (2) RS chip click handler in V2App now resolves against `v22RfpRsLookupPool` (published + RFP-referenced RSes) instead of `publishedRequirementSets` alone — own-private RSes referenced by RFPs are now clickable from the RFP Detail Panel. Single source for chip rendering and chip click. (3) RFP Detail Panel RS rows render in a two-line layout: line 1 shows name + canonical GlobeIcon (rendered when the RS is in `publishedRequirementSets`, regardless of owner or viewer) + version pill; line 2 shows the owner party name in muted secondary text. The `v22RfpRsLookupPool` shape gains derived `owner` (resolved via a roleId→party map over `DEMO_REQUIREMENT_SETS`, `_publishedBy`, or the active actor's party) + `isPublished` fields. CombinedRequestModal and SolicitationCreateModal RS sectioning retains its existing one-line shape (Phase 17.5.1.1). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.1.2.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.1.1 (Create RFP cleanup — cross-actor visibility + implicit-publication-via-reference + Your-first RS ordering).** Three 17.5.1 QA fixes. (1) **Cross-actor RFP visibility.** A user-created RFP now renders on every actor's Directory view, not only the creator's. Diagnostic: the data layer + plumbing were already correct (`v22CreatedRfps` flows unconditionally to DirectoryLayer; the memo deps + `mergeCreatedRfps` are right) — the bug was in DirectoryLayer's orphan-RFP *positioning*: every orphan RFP whose owner-cluster isn't on canvas (the GovCo-on-non-Bob case) was placed at one fixed coordinate, so a created GovCo RFP rendered exactly on top of the seeded Sentinel-4 RFP and was invisible. Fix: a per-anchor index offsets each same-owner orphan RFP into a readable column. (2) RfpDetailPanel's RS resolution pool extended to a new `v22RfpRsLookupPool` = all published RSes + all RSes referenced by any RFP (resolved against the network-wide seed pool `DEMO_REQUIREMENT_SETS` + the active actor's own RSes), regardless of publication status. Architectural rule: an RS referenced in an RFP is implicitly published to anyone who can see the RFP — the RFP's Requirements list IS the public interface for those RSes. The SolicitationCreateModal accordion uses the same pool. The published-RS pool used by other surfaces (Library, the RS picker for new RFPs) is unaffected. (3) `v22AvailableRequirementsSetRows` shape gains an `isOwn` flag; both RfpCreationModal and CombinedRequestModal restructured to render Your RSes first (own-and-published rows stay in the Your section with a trailing GlobeIcon + a tooltip "This Requirements Set has been published to the Radiant Network for any party to evaluate against.") and non-own Published RSes second (leading globe + "Published by {ownerParty}"). Empty sections skip their header. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.1.1.
- **§8.11 / §8 — Phase 17.5.1 (Create RFP flow — RfpCreationModal scaffolding).** New `RfpCreationModal.jsx` two-step modal (Form → Review/Confirm) mirrors CombinedRequestModal's StepDots + footer pattern. Entry from the Asset card action bar or Detail Panel footer (Phase 17.5 wiring); the Asset is pre-selected and pinned in the header copy. Step 1: RFP name + description + Requirements Sets multi-select scrollbox (same shape as CombinedRequestModal — Published RSes with globe icon + "Published by" line first, own RSes below with minimal treatment, dedupe by id). Step 2: labeled-list summary + Back/Submit. Submit builds via `makeRfp` (id generated through `makeArtifactId('rfp', ...)` with name slug + timestamp seed), adds to V2App's `v22CreatedRfps` Map<id, rfp> session state. New `mergeCreatedRfps` export in `v2_2Data.js` mirrors `mergeClosedRfps`' pure-function shape. `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` signature extended: `(roleId, provisionals, closedRfpIds, createdRfps)` — merge chain runs created → closed so same-session create+close works. Parent-canvas view-builder unchanged (RFPs are Directory-layer artifacts). The RS-row pre-shaping is extracted to a shared `v22AvailableRequirementsSetRows` useMemo consumed by both CombinedRequestModal and RfpCreationModal. Post-creation orchestration (layer switch + pan/zoom + auto-open Detail Panel + NEW badge) deferred to Phase 17.5.2. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.1.
- **§6.1 / §8 — Phase 17.5.0.4 (Disclosure boundary cleanup — umbrella DA pull-in + catalog Asset backfill + Library polish).** Three bundled fixes. (1) `buildViewForActor()` gains an `isFullyAnchoredDa` predicate gating two parent-canvas pull-in clauses (proof-only Claim DA pull-in + grantor-side `granteeAssetId` Asset pull-in). A DA is "fully anchored" when paired with an EA, OR subject is a PoE / Eval Result, OR it's a Phase 11D.3 proof-only Claim DA with populated `scope.poeIds`. Umbrella DAs (Phase 16.0 / 17.4 catalog visibility) fail all three checks and no longer pull artifacts onto either party's parent canvas. Directory-layer visibility unaffected. Phase 11D.3 carveout preserved as an explicit exception per architectural decision. (2) Seed backfill: 12 Dave catalog Claims + 5 Alice umbrella-seed Claims gain stub Assets and populated `referencedAssetIds`, closing the Phase 16.0 deferral ("future phases can give them real Assets if needed"). The existing `claimRefEdges` loop handles their internal `da-ref-` DAs automatically. (3) LibraryModal Published Requirements empty state gains the canonical wire-globe icon for visual parity with other tabs. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.0.4.
- **§8 — Phase 17.5.0.3 (Universal neutral footer buttons):** Drops the `accent` prop from all 11 remaining `FooterButton` call sites across Actor / Claim / Eval Result / PoE / Badge Template panels (completing the Phase 17.5.0.1 policy across all panels — Create PoE counts as two branches, active + the 17.5.0.2 disabled variant, so 12 `accent` keywords removed). Every footer button now shares the same indigo-bordered icon-with-hover-expand-label treatment. `danger` and `amber` variants preserved everywhere (Cancel Transfer, Cancel Request, Respond to Request) — they carry semantic urgency (destructive / needs-attention) distinct from "primary CTA" highlighting. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.0.3.
- **§8 — Phase 17.5.0.2 (Cross-panel footer icon propagation + visible-but-disabled audit):** Every Detail Panel `FooterButton` call site in `V22NodeDetailPanel.jsx` (Actor / Claim / Eval Result / PoE / Badge Template panels — Parse Result has no footer) migrated to icon-with-hover-expand-label mode. Icons reuse V22ActionBar glyphs everywhere they overlap (Register Asset `＋`, Request Agreement `⤴`, Parse Evidence `⊞`, Create Claim `◇`, Transfer `→`, Cancel `✕`, Amend Claim `✎`, Self-Evaluate / Run Evaluation `◆`, Re-run Evaluation `↻`, Create PoE `◈`, Request EA `▷`, View EA `◉`, Issue Badge = `BadgeShieldIcon`). Three footer-only actions get new picks: Dismiss `⊠`, Respond to Request `↪`, Create new version `＋`. The Eval Result panel's Create PoE button now renders as visible-but-disabled when `node._alreadyWrapped` is true (mirroring Re-Run's existing Phase 13.1 / 13.3 treatment) — the closed-chain state surfaces as an unavailable affordance with an explanatory tooltip rather than disappearing; this also required dropping the `!node._alreadyWrapped` gate on V2App's `onCreatePoE` prop so the handler reaches the panel (the panel owns the enabled/disabled decision; the disabled button carries no onClick, so a wrapped chain still can't be re-finalized). Mutually-exclusive Claim panel state branches stay conditional (state branches, not disabled actions). Scope boundary: `RfpDetailPanel.jsx` (local `ActionButton`) and the DA / EA agreement panels use their own button components rather than `FooterButton`, so per the stop-and-flag rule they are left untouched — their footer-icon migration is deferred to a follow-up. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.0.2.
- **§8 — Phase 17.5.0.1 (Asset footer styling polish):** `FooterButton` neutral default state (icon-mode) gains a 40%-indigo-blended border (matching `AssetNode.jsx`'s `WARM_BORDER` pattern), a `var(--bg-raised)` hover background tint, and a 60%-indigo-blended border on hover. Accent / danger / amber variants and the legacy label-only branch (other panels) are unchanged. Request Agreement in the Asset footer drops its `accent` prop so all six Asset-footer buttons share the uniform neutral treatment. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.0.1.
- **§8 — Phase 17.5 (RFP creation entry-point foundation):** Asset Detail Panel `FooterButton` refactored to an icon-with-hover-expand-label pattern; default state shows the icon only, hover animates the button width (~150ms ease) to reveal the label inline (the explanatory Tooltip from `title` is preserved — label = verb, tooltip = explanation). The `icon` prop is gated: when present, the new collapse/expand button renders (`flex: 0 0 auto`); when absent, the legacy label-only button (`flex: 1`) renders unchanged, preserving every other FooterButton call site (Claim / Eval Result / dismiss / etc.). All five existing Asset-footer actions migrated to icon mode and a new 6th action "Create RFP" added on both the V22ActionBar ASSET branch and the Asset Detail Panel footer (order: Register Asset → Request Agreement → Parse Evidence → Create Claim → Create RFP → Transfer; the Cancel-Transfer pending-state button also gains the `✕` icon). Both entry points dispatch `createRfp` via `onV22CardAction` / `onCreateRfp`; `handleCreateRfp` in V2App is a placeholder logger in 17.5 — the RfpCreationModal lands in 17.5.1. Icon convention follows existing precedent (`⬚` matches `CATEGORY_CONFIG.rfp.icon`, parallel to Create Claim's `◇` for `CATEGORY_CONFIG.claim.icon`). The Asset footer's six buttons sit in a scoped inner flex row (`justify-content: flex-start; flex-wrap: nowrap; overflow: hidden`) so hover expansion doesn't squeeze siblings — `PanelLayout`'s shared footer container is untouched so other panels are unaffected. The pre-existing Phase 10.2 polish flag ("Five-button footer is intentionally crowded for now — auto-collapsing affordance is a future polish phase") is closed by this phase, and the long-standing Transfer-button cut-off is fixed as a side effect. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.
- **§8.2 / §6.4 — Phase 17.4.5 (Internal "ownership" DA leak on Directory Claim panel):** Seed-data fix. When Alice (MicroCo) viewed a ChipCo Claim's Detail Panel on the Directory layer, the Agreements section showed ChipCo's *internal* ownership DA ("Full Disclosure — Internal — Never expires") next to her legitimate umbrella DA. Root cause was NOT the panel render path or the V2App filter (both correct): the seed's `aliceOwnClaims = claims.map(...)` loop iterated the *full* primary-Claim array (which holds Alice's, Dave/ChipCo's, and the Phase 17.4 MicroCo Claims) while hardcoding `owner: alice.party`, minting spurious MicroCo→MicroCo internal ownership DAs over every ChipCo Claim — with ids (`da-own-${c.id}`) that collide with `daveOwnClaims`'. The MicroCo/MicroCo duplicate passes the Directory mount's party-presence filter (`grantee.party === active || grantor.party === active`) because Alice IS both parties of the internal DA, so it surfaced as an "Internal" row. Fix restricts `aliceOwnClaims` to `claims.filter((c) => c.owner === alice.party)`, so each internal ownership DA's party matches the Claim's actual owner. Codifies the principle that **an internal ownership DA (grantor.party === grantee.party) must only exist for, and be visible to, the Claim's owner** — counterparty internals are private (§6.4). Owner-side behavior is unchanged: Dave still sees his ChipCo internal ownership DA on his own Claims (parent canvas + Directory). Data-layer probe confirmed: Alice → ChipCo Flash Memory now resolves to only the `ChipCo→MicroCo` umbrella DA; Bob → only `ChipCo→GovCo`; Dave → still the `ChipCo→ChipCo` internal + umbrellas; Alice's own-claim internal DAs drop from 22 → 8 (her 3 original + 5 MicroCo), zero over non-MicroCo Claims; Dave's 14 ChipCo ownership DAs intact. (Four benign pre-existing `da-ref`/`da-parse` ChipCo-internal duplicate ids remain — both copies carry the correct ChipCo owner and never leak; out of scope.) Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.5.
- **§8.2 — Phase 17.4.4 (Revoke wiring on Directory Claim panel + #203 backlog entry):** Wiring fix, not an architectural change. The Directory mount of V22ClaimPanel (`v22DirectorySelectedClaim`) rendered the umbrella DA row's Revoke button but never passed `onRevokeDa` / `onRevokeEa`, so the click was a no-op. Both props are now wired to the existing `handleOpenRevocationConfirm(agreement, type)` — the same handler the parent-canvas mount uses, which already supports grantee-side revocation (it branches on `agreement.grantor.party === activeRole.party` and the DA-annotation + paired-EA-cascade path in `handleRevokeConfirm` is direction-agnostic). No new handler logic. After confirm, the reactive flow handles cluster reshape automatically: `setV22Provisionals` → `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` filters `!d._revokedMeta`, so the revoked Claim drops from `umbrellaClaims`, the perimeter recomputes, and an umbrella-only Claim (no public DA) disappears from the cluster entirely. Claims that are both umbrella AND publicly disclosed stay visible as public dots (public-takes-precedence per §8.2.2) even after the umbrella DA is revoked. Grantor-side Amend/Revoke and row-click-into-DA-Detail-Panel remain intentionally omitted from the Directory mount (the umbrella viewer is the grantee). Backlog #203 filed for a rare edge-tooltip residual surfaced during QA. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.4.
- **§8.2 — Phase 17.4.3 (Polish wrap fix: Minkowski perimeter + legend z + edge tooltip cleanup):** Two 17.4.2 items still misbehaving + a long-standing ghost-tooltip bug. **Minkowski perimeter.** `umbrellaOutlinePath`'s 3+ branch now calls `collinearStadium(points, DOT_RADIUS + DOT_GRID / 2)` (Minkowski sum with disk — circle around each point + convex hull) unconditionally. The 17.4.2 `isCollinear` gate (perpendicular-distance epsilon `DOT_RADIUS / 2`) was too tight: near-collinear umbrella subsets (e.g. Bob's 7 ChipCo umbrella dots packed by `packClusterDense` into a vertical strip with ≤1 cell of horizontal spread) didn't trip the threshold and fell through to `offsetPolygonOutward` on a very thin hull, still reading as a line at typical zoom. The capsule treatment is consistent for collinear, near-collinear, and genuinely-2D subsets alike (rounded corners, never degenerates). `isCollinear` + `offsetPolygonOutward` are retained in the codebase but no longer called (marked `void` per the file's existing convention). **Legend z.** `DirectoryLegendBar` `zIndex: 50 → 400` — above the active cluster label (`Z_ACTIVE_CLUSTER_LABEL = 300`, which was covering it) and below zoom controls (1700) / card overlay (1500/1600) / tooltips (9999). **Edge tooltip cleanup.** Two state layers drive parent-canvas edge tooltips: V2Canvas's internal hover indicator (cleared via the imperative `clearHoverState`) and V2App's `edgeHover` (hover tooltip) + `edgeMenu` (the pinned "SELECT EDGE TO VIEW" box). The V2App states were never cleared on layer/role change, so a pinned edge menu persisted over the Directory and reappeared on role switch-back (the menu render gates on `resolveAgreementsForEdge` resolving in the active role's edges — un-rendered but not un-set). New `clearEdgeUiState()` helper (clearHoverState + setEdgeHover(null) + setEdgeMenu(null)) is called from all three Directory-open paths (chrome/globe toggle, node-double-click, RFP-solicitation notification) + the role-switch handler (`handleSwitchRole`). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.3.
- **§8.2 — Phase 17.4.2 (Polish wrap fix: collinear perimeter + legend positioning + distance-based active-cluster buffer):** Three 17.4.1 items that visual QA showed didn't deliver. **Collinear perimeter.** The 17.4.1 inflation halving (`DOT_GRID / 2`) was correct, but when the umbrella subset's relocated cells land in a straight grid row, `convexHull` returns a degenerate near-zero-area polygon and `offsetPolygonOutward(lineSegment, …)` produces a thin rectangle that reads as a line. New `isCollinear(poly, eps)` test (every point within `eps = DOT_RADIUS / 2` perpendicular distance of the line through poly[0] and its farthest point) gates a new `collinearStadium(points, r)` fallback — circle around each point + convex hull (Minkowski sum with disk), generalizing the 2-dot stadium to N collinear points. The genuinely-2D path keeps the existing `offsetPolygonOutward(hull, DOT_GRID / 2)` output unchanged, so non-degenerate perimeters are not reshaped. **Legend positioning.** `DirectoryLegendBar` `bottom: 12 → 40` to clear the ~32px global app footer bar that was covering it (z-index 50 + tooltips were already correct). **Distance-based active-cluster buffer.** The 17.4.1 area-based buffer (`4 × DOT_GRID²` added to the active cluster's target area) only widened the Voronoi cell radius by ~`sqrt(4 / π) ≈ 1.13` DOT_GRID and Lloyd's centroidal relaxation redistributes area non-isotropically, so it never produced a guaranteed visible gap. Removed and replaced by a post-Lloyd's seed correction: each non-active seed is pushed radially outward from the active seed until the active + neighbour cluster edges (radius ≈ `sqrt(targetArea / π)`) are separated by at least `8 × DOT_GRID`; the final Voronoi is then recomputed with the adjusted seeds. Seeds clamp to the usable canvas (`[DOMAIN_INSET_LEFT, CANVAS_WIDTH − DOMAIN_INSET_RIGHT] × [DOMAIN_INSET_TOP, CANVAS_HEIGHT − DOMAIN_INSET_BOTTOM]`); a seed clamped short of its target separation logs a console warning and accepts the partial buffer (better than spilling off-canvas). `packClusterDense`'s existing shrink-collapse fallback handles any cell that ends up too thin after the seed shift. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.2.
- **§8.2 — Phase 17.4.1 (Polish wrap: perimeter shape fix + Directory legend + active-cluster buffer + active label z):** Four QA polish items on the umbrella DA arc. **Perimeter inflation halved** — `umbrellaOutlinePath`'s three branches (1-dot bounding circle, 2-dot stadium, 3+ convex-hull outward offset) switch from a full `DOT_GRID` offset to `DOT_GRID / 2`. Because the dot matrix spaces dots one `DOT_GRID` apart, a half-cell offset lands the perimeter corners at the midpoint between an umbrella dot and an adjacent non-umbrella dot (the prior full-cell offset over-extended past adjacent dots, cutting through dot positions). The geometry function is otherwise unchanged — only the inflation amount. **DirectoryLegendBar** — new bottom-left legend on the Directory layer, paralleling V2Canvas's parent-canvas LegendBar. Four rows: Full / Selective / Proof-Only disclosure (indigo / amber / green filled dots) + Request for Proposals (indigo hollow square, matching the RFP marker). Inline hover tooltips (no react-dom portal — the legend sits in a corner where an absolutely-positioned tooltip clears the surrounding UI). **Active-cluster buffer** — the active actor's own cluster (clusterSpecs[0], isOwnCluster) gains a 4-dot breathing-room buffer (`4 × DOT_GRID²`) in the Lloyd's `target_area_i` computation so neighbouring mock clusters stop crowding it; the freed area redistributes among non-active clusters via the centroidal relaxation. **Active label z-order** — the active actor's label pillbox renders at a dedicated `Z_ACTIVE_CLUSTER_LABEL = 300`, above the other cluster labels' 100–~200 dot-count-inverse ranks (so a neighbouring label can never cover it) and below the RFP labels (1100) / zoom controls (1700) / card overlay / tooltips. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.1.
- **§8.2 + §8.10 — Phase 17.4 (Umbrella DA arc: perimeter fix + seed expansion + Directory panel/card parity + perimeter hover):** Ratifies Andrew's Option #3 — umbrella DAs are visualised as a per-cluster grouping perimeter (convex hull of the umbrella-subset dots, offset outward 1 cell, amber stroke + 8% amber fill), NOT as edges. An umbrella DA is a regular DA with `grantee = non-active-party` + `subject.kind = 'claim'` (no new artifact type). **Perimeter rendering fix.** Root cause: `packClusterDense` placed the umbrella subset on the innermost grid cells (sorted by distance from cluster center), which ring the central label hole — the convex hull then enclosed the hole + actor pillbox. Fix: the packer relocates the umbrella subset into a coherent edge-blob (anchored at the inner cell furthest from center, grown by nearest-neighbor) so the hull is a tight off-center polygon wrapping just the umbrella dots. RFP cells stay outermost. `umbrellaOutlinePath` itself is unchanged. **Seed expansion.** 14 new umbrella DAs across actor pairings (was only Dave→Bob): Alice→Bob (5, full/selective/proofonly variety), Alice→Carol (3, all full — auditor access), Dave→Alice (3, 2 selective + 1 proofonly — sub-supplier sharing), Dave→Carol (3, all full — auditing). Five new non-public MicroCo Claims added because MicroCo's three existing Claims are all publicly disclosed and public takes precedence over umbrella per §8.2.2 (an umbrella DA on a public Claim wouldn't render an amber subset). Dave's disclosures reuse his seven existing non-public Claims (a Claim can carry umbrella DAs to multiple grantees). Per-role result: Bob → ChipCo (7) + MicroCo (5); Alice → ChipCo (3); Carol → MicroCo (3) + ChipCo (3); Dave → none (suppliers don't receive umbrella disclosures by design). **Directory ⇄ parent Claim Detail Panel parity.** The Directory mount of V22ClaimPanel now threads the DA/EA Agreements section lists (from merged shared artifacts, filtered to this Claim + agreements the active actor is a party to) + name resolvers, plus the warm-path Request EA handler — so clicking an umbrella Claim shows the same DA listing + EA-required callout + warm-path footer button (opens EARequestModal) the parent canvas shows. Cold-path (no DA → AssetPickerModal → CombinedRequestModal) + View-EA (EA exists) states from 17.3 are preserved. DA/EA mutation handlers (Amend/Revoke) are intentionally omitted from the Directory mount — those are grantor-side actions and the umbrella viewer is the grantee. **Card action bar warm-path parity.** New `_directoryWarmPathRequestCandidate` synthetic-node marker (active DA exists, no EA), mutually exclusive with `_directoryRequestEaCandidate` (no DA, no EA) and `_directoryExistingEa` (EA exists). AssetNode's Claim card renders the warm-path Request EA button; click dispatches a distinct action V2App routes to the EARequestModal. DirectoryLayer now receives `disclosureAgreements` to compute the marker. **Perimeter hover + tooltip.** Point-in-polygon hit-test in the Directory mouse-move handler (projects each cluster's `umbrellaPathWorld` to screen, tests the cursor) runs independently of the dot raycast so it never blocks Claim clicks. Hover bumps border opacity 0.55 → 1.0 + stroke 1.5 → 2.5px + fill tint 8% → 16%, and renders an HTML tooltip near the cursor ("You have Disclosure Agreements with the Claims inside this perimeter." + "From {grantor party}" sub-line, viewport-edge-clamped). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4. (Parent-layer EA-required-message bug remains deferred — symptom not recalled.)
- **§8.8.5 — Phase 17.3.2 (Fan-out improvements + leak investigation + RFP owner card action bar):** Five items before Phase 17.4 opens. **Fan-out tuning.** `DEFAULT_JITTER_MS` 250 → 600 (per-instance randomness now ~50% of base radial duration — clearly jagged wavefront); `DEFAULT_WAVE_SPEED` 7000 → 9500 wu/sec (faster radial propagation). Combined, total fan-out duration stays in the 1.2–1.5 s band; the visual reads more organic. **CSS wipe duration** bumped 550 → 900 ms (`clip-path` transition in DirectoryLayer's scene container) so the wipe and the fan-out finish closer in time and read as one continuous transition. **Cinematic zoom-out.** New animation-effect block starts the camera at `INITIAL_ZOOM × 1.3` (30% tighter) and eases back to `INITIAL_ZOOM` over 1500 ms via the same `easeOut(t) = 1 - (1-t)^3` curve the dots use.
