Implementation: a parallel rAF loop independent of `playDirectoryLoadAnimation` (the fan-out doesn't care about zoom; the zoom doesn't care about dots); `cinematicZoomRafRef` cancelled in cleanup so role-switch / close-during-zoom doesn't leak the RAF. **Safari crash investigation.** Root cause: WebGL context exhaustion — each Directory open/close cycle disposes + recreates the entire Three.js renderer, and Safari's ~16-context limit combined with slow driver-side context release lets cycles 10+ accumulate up to the cap. Other suspects (closedRfpMesh geometry rebuild churn, React state thrashing from setLabelOpacities) reviewed and ruled out (the rebuild path properly disposes; React 18's automatic batching collapses per-tick setState calls). **Surgical fix**: scene cleanup at line ~1886 now calls `renderer.getContext().getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context').loseContext()` before `renderer.dispose()` to dispatch the `webglcontextlost` event synchronously — the GPU driver releases the context immediately. No negative side effects on Chrome / Firefox. **RFP owner Close/Reopen card action bar.** DirectoryLayer stamps `_directoryCloseCandidate` (owner + open RFP) and `_directoryReopenCandidate` (owner + closed RFP) on the synthetic RFP node — mutually exclusive with each other and with `_directorySolicitCandidate`. AssetNode RFP early-return renders padlock-icon buttons on hover/select; click dispatches `closeRfp` / `reopenRfp` through the existing `onV22CardAction` → `onRfpCardAction` channel; V2App routes to the same `setV22ClosedRfpIds` Map mutations the panel footer fires (Phase 17.1 pattern preserved). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.3.2.
- **§8.8.5 + §8.10 — Phase 17.3.1 (Polish wrap: RFP card action bar + RfpDetailPanel cleanup + Requirements rows + fan-out animation tweaks):** Four polish items before Phase 17.4 (umbrella DA edges) opens. **RFP full-size card action bar.** Parallel to the Claim card pattern shipped in Phase 17.3 — DirectoryLayer stamps `_directorySolicitCandidate` on the synthetic RFP node when the active actor is a non-owner of an open RFP with no existing solicitation from them; AssetNode's RFP early-return renders an inline action bar with a paper-airplane SVG "Solicit with my Claim" button on hover/select. Click fires the `solicitWithClaim` action via the same `onV22CardAction` dispatcher and V2App routes to the same SolicitationCreateModal the panel footer opens — both surfaces share state and behaviour. **RfpDetailPanel categorical pill cleanup (#202 follow-up).** The Phase 17.3 #202 audit scoped V22NodeDetailPanel only; this phase extends to RfpDetailPanel. Removed the "ACTOR" pill next to the party name in the "POSTED BY" row and the "ASSET" pill next to the Asset name in the "FOR ASSET" row. Section headers + "YOU" indicator preserved (they convey relationship, not category). **#202 now fully closed.** **Requirements section overhaul.** Section title renamed "Required Standards" → "Requirements" per Andrew's broader-term preference. Layout converted from horizontal chips to vertical full-width clickable rows; each row renders RS name at full width with a small version badge pill on the right. Hover state + click open a new RequirementsSetDetailModal. Missing-RS rows render dashed-border muted "Standard not found ({rsId})" non-clickable. **New `RequirementsSetDetailModal`** (`src/components/modals/RequirementsSetDetailModal.jsx`) — read-only single-RS view: header (name + version badge), sub-header ("Published by {owner}" + canonical GlobeIcon), scrollable body listing each requirement as id-pill + label + description + optional muted italic Criterion. Close button only. ESC + backdrop close via shared Backdrop primitive. Mirrors `SolicitationCreateModal`'s `RsAccordionEntry` expanded-state treatment (inline-rendered rather than refactor-extracted, to avoid extracting state coupled to the accordion's open/close machinery). **Fan-out loading animation tweaks.** `directoryLoadAnimation.js`: `waveSpeed` 4500 → 7000 wu/sec; `dotFadeMs` 200 → 90 ms; new `jitterMs` (default 250 ms) seeded random per-instance start offset gives the wavefront a jagged organic edge (xmur3+mulberry32 PRNG, seeded by `{roleId}:{i|r}` so mid-flight re-renders see consistent jitter while each entry rolls fresh); eased via `easeOut(t) = 1 - (1-t)^3` instead of linear ramp. RFP markers participate via new `rfps` + `setRfpAppear` API. **DirectoryLayer per-instance scale-driven appearance** (replaces 16.2.3 color-multiplier-from-black): `dotOpacitiesRef` reinterpreted from "color multiplier" to "matrix scale factor" — dots render at their final disclosure-type color from the moment they appear; the wave grows each dot from a point to its full size, eliminating the visible black-to-color transition. New `flushDotMatrices` + `flushRfpMatrices` callbacks integrated into the existing animate-loop dirty-flag pattern. `flushDotColors` simplified (no longer touches opacity). RFP hit-test mesh keeps full scale throughout (clickable mid-wave). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.3.1.
- **§8.10 (new) — Phase 17.3 (Claim Detail Panel EA content on Directory + card action bar + #202 ACTOR/ASSET label cleanup):** Opens the action surface for Directory-layer Claim discovery. New §8.10 documents the four-row CTA state table (owner / no-DA-no-EA / no-DA-EA-exists / warm-path), the shared predicate `getActiveEaForClaimAndRequester` exported from `v2_2Data.js`, the cold-path Request EA flow (AssetPickerModal → CombinedRequestModal pre-filled), the View EA navigation (closes Directory, pans parent canvas to Claim node, selects EA edge, opens EA Detail Panel via existing `setOpenAgreement` mechanism), and the panel-card parity wiring (DirectoryLayer stamps `_directoryRequestEaCandidate` / `_directoryExistingEa` on synthetic Claim nodes; V22ActionBar consults them). AssetPickerModal's prop contract is generalized to accept `targetClaim` + `context` ({ type: 'directory-claim' } | { type: 'rfp', rfp }); legacy solicitation/solicitorClaim/rfp props preserved as fallback. **#202 closed** by removing the ACTOR / ASSET typeLabel pills from V22ActorPanel + V22AssetPanel headers (PanelHeader's `typeLabel` prop is now optional); other panel types keep their type pill. Parent-layer EA-required-message bug still deferred to Phase 17.4+ alongside umbrella DA edge work. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.3.
- **§8.9.6 — Phase 17.2.1.2 (Hotfix: CombinedRequestModal RS pre-selection):** All RSes the RFP references are now pre-checked when CombinedRequestModal opens via the Accept flow. Pre-17.2.1.2 only the first id from `rfp.requirementsSetIds` was pre-checked because V2App's `handleRequestAgreement` stored only the singular `suggestedRequirementsSetId`. Fix threads the full array as `suggestedRequirementsSetIds` (plural); the mount block prefers the plural array form, falling back to the singular for the AI Shopper cold-path entry. CombinedRequestModal's `useState(() => [...initialRequirementsSetIds])` state initializer was already correct — the bug was entirely upstream at the V2App handler + mount. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.1.2.
- **§8.8.1 + §8.9.6 + §8.9.6.1 + §8.9.6.2 + §8.9.6.3 — Phase 17.2.1.1 (Hotfix: RFP-bound Asset + Claim greying + EA+DA notification Directory close + CombinedRequestModal RS scrollbox):** Four QA-driven items closing out the 17.2 arc. **§8.8.1 (extended)**: `makeRfp` factory adds required `assetId` field — every RFP binds to exactly one of its owner's Assets at creation time. Factory throws on missing `assetId`. All 118 seeded RFPs populated: Bob's Sentinel-4 RFP → `bAvionics` "Avionics Module"; 20 RFP-only mock-cluster owners each get one stub Asset (`asset-{actor.id}-rfp-anchor`, owner = actor.party); mixed-cluster owners reuse their first existing Asset. `generateRfpsForActor(actor, count, defaultAssetId)` requires `defaultAssetId` so missing-Asset seed inconsistencies surface at build time. **§8.9.6 (simplified)**: Accept flow collapses Phase 17.2.1's two-modal AssetPickerModal → CombinedRequestModal chain into a single direct open of CombinedRequestModal, pre-filled with the RFP's bound Asset (via `requesterAsset`), the solicitor's Claim PIN (`initialPin`), and the RFP's `requirementsSetIds` (`initialRequirementsSetIds`). AssetPickerModal component file stays in the codebase for future create-RFP work (Phase 17.5+); only the V2App mount + handlers were removed from the Accept flow path. **§8.9.6.1 (new)**: SolicitationCreateModal Claim picker greys out Claims already mapped to the RFP owner via an active or pending EA (predicate documented). Greyed rows render at opacity 0.45 with `cursor: not-allowed`, no click handler, "ON NETWORK" pillbox, tooltip "Already on {ownerParty}'s network". Rationale: prevents the seller from submitting a solicitation pointing at a Claim that's already disclosed (which would error the buyer's Accept flow at the cold-path PIN-already-disclosed gate). **§8.9.6.2 (new)**: EA+DA notification click handlers (`v22-request`, `v22-request-ea-only`, `v22-ea-accepted`, `v22-ea-declined`) close the Directory layer if open before running cold-path navigation. Previously the Directory stayed open behind the response modal / over the parent canvas. **§8.9.6.3 (new)**: CombinedRequestModal Step 1 Requirements Set scrollbox data source extended to all published Requirements Sets (network-wide), not just the active actor's own pool. Per-row rendering: globe icon (canonical GlobeIcon) + RS name + version + "Published by {owner party}" muted line. Own (non-published) RSes appended below with the pre-17.2.1.1 minimal treatment. Dedupe by id (own wins). Pre-selection via `initialRequirementsSetIds` now works for any actor's published RS (the RFP's referenced RSes are guaranteed to be in the list). RfpDetailPanel gains a "For Asset" row between the Posted-by row and the Description block; V2App threads a derived `assetsById` Map. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.1.1.
- **§8.9.6 (new) — Phase 17.2.1 (RFP Accept flow / Request Agreement):** Closes the placeholder-disabled "Request Agreement" button that's been deferred across 17.2 / 17.2.0.x. New two-modal chain — AssetPickerModal (Bob picks one of his Assets to anchor the requested mapping, per the architectural rule that requests must originate from an Asset) → the existing CombinedRequestModal pre-filled via the same prop mechanism the AI Shopper uses (`requesterAsset` + `initialPin` + `initialRequirementsSetIds`). Submit-side effect lives inside the existing `handleV22RequestSubmit` (conditional on `v22AcceptingSolicitation` being non-null); the cold-path remains bit-identical. `acceptSolicitation(solicitation, eaId)` pure transform + new `acceptedEaId` field on `makeRfpSolicitation` link the accepted solicitation back to the resulting EA. New `v22-rfp-solicitation-accepted` notification (green ACCEPTED badge) lands on the solicitor's inbox alongside the standard EA+DA request notification; click routes to Directory → RFP marker → RfpDetailPanel where the solicitor-view accepted state surfaces the "see your new EA on the parent canvas" pointer. SolicitationCard accepted-state treatment for both viewer modes (no action buttons; green badge + EA-pointer text). RfpDetailPanel's `onRequestAgreement` prop wired through to the SolicitationCard. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.1. Spec §8.9.6 contains the full Accept-flow chain documentation including the dual notification model rationale and the cold-path preservation guarantee.
- **Hotfix — Phase 17.2.0.4 (LOD threshold revert + zoom control z-index + AI Shopper modal lag):** No §8 structural changes — all three items are polish + a perf fix on existing patterns. (1) **LOD thresholds reverted** from 17.2.0.3's 2.5–5.5 band to 3.4–5.0. Mini-cards at the 2.5 lower bound overlapped visibly in dense clusters (Pinnacle Systems et al.); 3.4 is close to the original density-invariant value (MINI_CARD_W / DOT_GRID = 3.333) so screen spacing exceeds card width at the threshold and overlap stops. Full-card threshold lifted slightly from the original 4.375 to 5.0 — keeps a moderately-wider mini-card range (340–500 %) than the pre-17.2.0.3 baseline (333–438 %). `MAX_ZOOM` stays at 6.5. `selectRfp`'s `Math.max(zoomRef.current, LOD_THRESHOLD)` automatically resolves to 5.0 — notification clicks still land at full-card LOD. (2) **Directory zoom controls raised** above the card overlay (zIndex `50 → 1700`). Card overlay sits at z=1500 default / z=1600 selected. New 1700 value sits between the card layer and the header pillbox (z=2000) / tooltip cards (z=2500) so contextual chrome still renders above. Cluster labels stay at their existing z-tier (intentional per brief). (3) **AI Shopper modal lag fixed.** Click-to-modal-visible measured at 81 ms post-fix (puppeteer in headless Chrome) versus ~5 s reported pre-fix. Two surgical fixes: (a) module-level cache for `buildV22SharedArtifacts` in `v2_2Data.js` — the function rebuilds a ~23k-Claim, ~92k-DA dataset (320 ms cold) on every call and has 20+ call sites in V2App; the cache returns the same object on subsequent calls in 0.001 ms. Safe because every existing caller treats the result as read-only (find / filter / map / `{...shared}` shallow-spread) and the function has no parameters. A future mutation MUST invalidate the cache or shape itself as a new merge layer. (b) O(n²) → O(n) rewrite of the AI Shopper IIFE's public-claims derivation in V2App: replaced `publicDas.map(d => shared.claims.find(c => c.id === d.subject.id))` (~1.6 s alone on the seeded data) with a precomputed `claimsById` Map for O(1) lookups. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.0.4.
- **Hotfix — Phase 17.2.0.3 (RFP description in solicitation modal + expanded mini-card zoom range):** No §8 structural changes — both items are polish on existing patterns. (1) **RFP description block** added to `SolicitationCreateModal` between the modal subtitle and the Required Standards accordion. Matches `RfpDetailPanel`'s description treatment: prose body with `white-space: pre-wrap` for multiline preservation; `No description provided.` muted-italic fallback when `rfp.description` is empty/null. Thin section divider below pairs visually with the existing divider between the accordion and the Claim picker. (2) **Mini-card zoom range widened** in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`. `MID_LOD_THRESHOLD` changed from `MINI_CARD_W / DOT_GRID` (= 3.333) to an explicit `2.5` (250 % zoom); `LOD_THRESHOLD` changed from `CARD_W / DOT_GRID` (= 4.375) to an explicit `5.5` (550 % zoom). `MAX_ZOOM` bumped `5.0 → 6.5` so the new full-card threshold sits well below the cap with comparable headroom. This breaks the original "density invariant" (cards never overlap horizontally at the threshold) — at 2.5 zoom × 48 wu DOT_GRID = 120 px screen spacing vs 160 px mini-card width, mini-cards may overlap horizontally inside dense clusters at the low end of the band. Per Andrew, the readability win (larger band for reading mini-card content before commitment to full-cards) outweighs the mild collision risk. `selectRfp`'s `Math.max(zoomRef.current, LOD_THRESHOLD)` override now resolves to 5.5 — notification clicks still land at full-card LOD. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.0.3.
- **Hotfix — Phase 17.2.0.2 (notification click completion + own-cluster label + RS accordion in solicitation modal):** No §8 structural changes — the polish items don't introduce new architectural patterns. (1) **Notification click completion.** Phase 17.2.0.1's `panToRfp` imperative handle was pan-only at current zoom, so from the default 15 % galactic view the camera barely moved and the on-canvas marker remained unselected. Replaced with `selectRfp(rfp)`, which mirrors the internal click path end-to-end via a new `focusRfpInternal(d, dotIndex, { zoom })` helper shared between `handleMouseUp`'s RFP branch, `onCardClick`'s RFP branch, AND the imperative path. Helper does setPinned (drives tooltip + select-state brightening via flushRfpColors) plus `animatedPanToWithZoom` with panel-offset compensation. External `selectRfp` overrides zoom to `Math.max(zoomRef.current, LOD_THRESHOLD)` so a notification click from the galactic view lands at full-card LOD where the marker reads clearly; internal click paths keep current zoom (existing behaviour preserved). V2App's notification handler now calls `selectRfp` (was `panToRfp`); the rAF retry loop guard from 17.2.0.1 is preserved. (2) **Active actor's own-cluster label amber styling on the Directory layer.** PillboxLabel accepts a new `isOwn` boolean prop; when true, renders with `background: var(--accent-amber)` + `color: var(--bg-deep)` for contrast. Cluster label render block computes `isOwn = !!layout.activeParty && cluster.ownerParty === layout.activeParty` per cluster. Bob/GovCo, Alice/MicroCo, Dave/ChipCo all see their own cluster styled; Carol/AuditCo has no cluster on the Directory so the gating naturally degenerates. Same `--accent-amber` variable used by the Directory globe active state, inbox-with-notifications icon, and cross-role notification dot. (3) **SolicitationCreateModal — Required Standards accordion above the Claim picker.** New `requirementsSets` prop (array; V2App passes `publishedRequirementSets`). New `RsAccordionEntry` sub-component renders each `rfp.requirementsSetIds` entry as a clickable header (name + version pill + chevron); expanded state renders the RS's requirements list with id (mono pill) + label + description (secondary) + criterion (muted italic). Multi-expand via `useState(() => new Set())`. Missing-RS surfaces "(Standard not found)"; zero-RS-IDs surfaces "No required standards specified." 240 px max-height scrollable section; the existing 90 vh modal cap (ModalShared) preserves smaller-viewport usability. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.0.2.
- **Hotfix — Phase 17.2.0.1 (notification routing + Directory cursor/hover + V2Canvas2 setState fix):** Three runtime-QA items from Phase 17.2 closed in one pass; no §8 structural changes — the architecture from §8.9 is unchanged, only implementation completeness. (1) **Notification click routing** for both `v22-rfp-solicitation-received` and `v22-rfp-solicitation-rejected` now fires all four sub-steps (open Directory if closed → clear Claim panel → set RFP panel → pan + zoom Directory camera to the RFP marker). The 17.2 implementation omitted the camera pan. New imperative handle on DirectoryLayer (`useImperativeHandle` exposing `panToRfp(rfp)`) is called by V2App with a rAF retry loop that fires the pan once Directory's phase machine reaches `in` and the layout is built (the 600ms opening transition makes a synchronous pan call a no-op when Directory was closed before the click). (2) **Cursor pointer on Directory hover** wired via imperative `canvasEl.style.cursor` update in `handleMouseMove` (the canvas's inline style sat at `default`, masking any container-level cursor change). AssetNode / AssetNodeMini wrappers already carry `cursor: pointer`, so card-LOD hover works automatically. (3) **RFP marker hover/select brightening** now visibly renders. Phase 17.2 set `instanceColor = (1,1,1)` against indigo material color — the multiplier landed at identity, no visible delta. Fix: material color → white, base instance color = indigo, hover lerps 0.35 toward white, select lerps 0.65 toward white. Closed-RFP per-vertex colors get the same hover/select delta. (4) **V2Canvas2 setState-in-render warning** fixed: `setV22DirectoryOpen((open) => ...)` functional updater called `canvasRef.current.clearHoverState()` as a side effect inside the reducer, which ran during V2App's render and triggered V2Canvas's setState. Surgical lift-side-effect-out-of-updater fix; no structural refactor. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.0.1.
- **§8.9 (new) — Phase 17.2 (RFP solicitation flow — submit + deliver + reject loop):** Opens the seller→buyer engagement axis. A Solicitation is the cheap, reversible pre-EA pointer from seller to buyer — "I have a Claim that may match your requirements, take a look." New `makeRfpSolicitation` factory with shape `{ id, type: 'rfp-solicitation', rfpId, claimId, solicitor, recipient, message, status, createdDate, respondedDate, rejectionMessage }`; status taxonomy `'pending' | 'rejected' | 'accepted'` (accepted reserved for Phase 17.2.1). New `mergeSolicitations(shared, solicitations)` overlay (mirror of `mergeProvisionals` / `mergeClosedRfps` — Map-backed, identity on empty). `buildV22SharedArtifacts` returns `rfpSolicitations: []` so consumers can rely on the field's existence. V2App holds `v22Solicitations: Map<id, RfpSolicitation>` session-state; `handleCreateSolicitation` builds the artifact + fires `v22-rfp-solicitation-received` notification on the owner's inbox; `handleRejectSolicitation` updates status + respondedDate + rejectionMessage + fires `v22-rfp-solicitation-rejected` notification on the solicitor's inbox. Both notification types route on click to Directory → RFP marker → RfpDetailPanel; auto-dismiss since the loop terminates after rejection. Three new components ship: `SolicitationCard` (renders a single solicitation with status badge + message blocks + action bar — `Request Agreement` disabled with "Coming in Phase 17.2.1" tooltip; `Reject` enabled on pending), `SolicitationCreateModal` (Claim picker defaulting to zero selected + 500-char optional message), `SolicitationRejectModal` (300-char optional reply + red destructive submit). `RfpDetailPanel` gains a three-way visibility branch above the footer: owner sees "Incoming Solicitations (N)" + cards (empty case "No solicitations yet."); solicitor sees "Your Solicitation" + their single card (REJECTED state surfaces buyer's reply); other non-owner sees no section. Footer extensions: non-owner + open + no existing → "Solicit with my Claim" affirm button; non-owner + existing → muted "Already solicited — see above." DirectoryLayer adds RFP marker hover + select brightening: per-instance `instanceColor` attribute on `rfpMesh` + `rfpFillMesh` (white-multiplier; hovered/selected index brightens); per-vertex `color` attribute on closed-RFP `LineDashedMaterial` with `vertexColors: true` (lerp toward white on the matching 8-vertex range). New `flushRfpColors` callback parallel to `flushDotColors`; flushed via `rfpDirtyRef` from the animation loop. Detail Panel 1px gap fix: both Directory-layer panel mount blocks (Claim + RFP) bumped `bottom: 28` → `bottom: 27`. Closed-RFP solicitations stay visible to the owner (so existing notifications remain actionable); the asymmetric closed-RFP filter from §8.8.7 naturally gates new solicitations against closed RFPs (the seller-side entry point disappears). Existing §8.8.8 forward pointer for 17.2 is now satisfied by this section.
- **§8.8 extension — Phase 17.1 (RFP close/reopen lifecycle, owner-side):** First lifecycle phase against the RFP arc. `makeRfp` validates `status` against a `'open' | 'closed'` taxonomy and accepts `closedDate` (ISO string, null when open). New exported `closeRfp(rfp, closedDate?)` / `reopenRfp(rfp)` pure transforms. New `mergeClosedRfps(shared, closedRfpIds)` session-state overlay; `closedRfpIds` is a `Map<rfpId, ISO closedDate>` (Map storage so each closure keeps its captured timestamp across re-renders). `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` extended with a 3rd `closedRfpIds` param; chains the merge internally and filters `otherRfps` + `cluster.rfps` on `status === 'open'` for non-owners while keeping `ownRfps` unfiltered. V2App holds `v22ClosedRfpIds` state next to `v22Provisionals`; threaded to DirectoryLayer; close/reopen handlers wired to RfpDetailPanel; the panel re-resolves the selected RFP from merged shared artifacts each render so stale snapshots don't surface. RfpDetailPanel gains a CLOSED status badge (muted treatment), a "Closed YYYY-MM-DD · HH:MM UTC" row, and an owner-only footer with a single direct-action button ("Close this RFP" or "Reopen this RFP"). DirectoryLayer's `rfpMesh` + `rfpFillMesh` partition on closed-owned (hidden) and `rfpHitMesh` keeps all instances (closed still clickable). New `closedRfpMesh` (single `THREE.LineSegments` with `LineDashedMaterial`); `BufferGeometry` rebuilt on layout / zoom change with 4 segments per closed-owned RFP; `computeLineDistances()` after each rebuild. AssetNode + AssetNodeMini's RFP early-return branches read `node.isClosed` and switch `border-style: solid → dashed` on both inner card border and outer selection ring; hover-preview portal inherits the same treatment via the shared synthetic node. New §8.8.7 (Lifecycle states) covers the factory shape extension, asymmetric visibility table, dashed-outline / dashed-card implementation, and the architectural rationale for asymmetric visibility (solicitation notifications are independent of the RFP node). Existing §8.8.7 (Forward pointers) renumbered to §8.8.8 with the 17.1 entry removed and remaining 17.2 / 17.2.1 / 17.5+ unchanged.
- **Hotfix — Phase 17.0.2 (TDZ in AssetNode RFP branch):** Phase 17.0.1's RFP early-return branch in `AssetNode` (full) referenced `hoverSelectColor` before its `const` declaration (the Phase 16.2.11 declaration site sat further down in the function body, after the early-return). Every RFP card render and every RFP card click triggered a TDZ `ReferenceError: Cannot access 'hoverSelectColor' before initialization`. Fast zoom-to-full-LOD on Bob's view crashed the app to a black screen. Fix: hoist the `hoverSelectColor` declaration above the RFP early-return (3-line move; the value depends only on `node.category` which is available at function entry, so no semantic change for non-RFP renders). `AssetNodeMini` already declared `hoverSelectColor` above its RFP early-return — no change needed there. Swept both components for parallel TDZ-trapped vars; none surfaced. No §8.8 content change — the RFP architecture from 17.0.1 is correct; only implementation order needed fixing. Process note: 17.0.1's structured review marked items as "Code-verified" for paths that should have been runtime-verified per CLAUDE.md's runtime-verification convention; the 17.0.1 → 17.0.2 incident is now the canonical example for the convention.
- **§8.8 extension — Phase 17.0.1 (RFP card LOD swap + hover-preview pinning):** Brings RFPs to LOD parity with Claims and fixes the stale-pinned-tooltip bug surfaced in 17.0 QA. **AssetNode + AssetNodeMini** dispatcher extended with a 5th schema (`category: 'rfp'`) — minimal layout per Andrew's spec: full-card = type pill `RFP` + name + "Posted by {owner}"; mini-card = type pill + name only. No badges, no minibars, no action bar. RFPs join the amber-on-hover/select branch in `hoverSelectColor`. Default border falls through cleanly to WARM_BORDER (RFPs carry no disclosure type). `CATEGORY_CONFIG.rfp` added for type-label uniformity, though the early-return branches render the type pill inline rather than via the config (no functional impact). **DirectoryLayer card-overlay render block** now iterates both Claim AND RFP entries from `allDots`; each RFP entry builds an inline synthetic node `{ id, category: 'rfp', rfp, name, ownerParty, owner }` and routes to the same `AssetNode` / `AssetNodeMini` Card the Claim branch uses (only the inner schema differs). **Hollow-square outline + tinted-fill + hit-test meshes** all hide at `zoom ≥ MID_LOD_THRESHOLD` (extending the dot-mesh hide path) so the cards don't render behind the hollow squares and the hit-test mesh doesn't intercept clicks meant for the cards above. **Pinned state extended to discriminated union** — exactly one of `{ claim, ... }` or `{ rfp, ... }` at any time. All click paths (hollow-square at low zoom, onCardClick at mid/full LOD) call `setPinned` with the appropriate discriminator; setting the new shape overwrites the previous in a single call, so clicking from Claim to RFP (or vice versa) cleanly swaps the pinned tooltip — structural fix for the 17.0 stale-tooltip bug. **New `RfpTooltipCard`** mirrors `ClaimTooltipCard` for the dot-LOD pinned-tooltip path; pinned-tooltip render block branches on `pinned.claim` vs `pinned.rfp`. **Empty-canvas click** clears both discriminators (calls `onRfpClick(null)` alongside the existing `onClaimDotClick(null)` + `setPinned(null)`). **V2App.jsx** minor changes only: footer rolls forward to v0.17.0.1; Changelog modal entry prepended. New §8.8.5 (Card LOD swap), §8.8.6 (Pinned tooltip discriminator), and §8.8.7 (Forward pointers — Phase 17.0.1 now landed; remaining 17.1 / 17.2 / 17.2.1 / 17.5+ unchanged) added under §8.8.
- **§8.8 (new) — Phase 17.0 (Clickable RFP markers + read-only RFP Detail Panel):** First sub-phase of the Phase 17 RFP arc against polish-backlog #192. Adds the read pipeline only: clicking any RFP marker on the Directory opens a new read-only `RfpDetailPanel`. No card LOD swap (17.0.1), no post/respond/review (17.1–17.2.1), no create flow (17.5+), no parent-canvas RFP rendering. **Hit-test mesh**: an invisible solid-square `THREE.InstancedMesh` parallels the visible hollow-square outline mesh; matrices written in lockstep with the outline + fill via the existing populate + rescale-on-zoom paths. Resolves raycasts on the marker's interior (the outline ring's hole leaves a dead zone otherwise). Defensive InstancedMesh settings (`frustumCulled = false`, unbounded `boundingSphere`) preserved. **Raycast** consults dot mesh + RFP hit-test mesh and picks the closer hit (defensive; RFPs are placed outside cluster dots so concurrent hits are vanishingly unlikely). RFP hit fires `onRfpClick(rfp)` + the standard `animatedPanToWithZoom` pan-to-center (mirror of Phase 16.1.3 Item 6 / 16.2.9 Item 1 Claim pipeline). **RfpDetailPanel** (`src/components/DetailPanel/RfpDetailPanel.jsx`): header pillbox `RFP` + status badge + close button + name; "Posted by" + owner ACTOR pillbox + optional `YOU` badge; description (or muted italic "No description provided"); "Required Standards" chip list (each `rsId` → `{name} v{version}` resolved against `publishedRequirementSets`, muted raw-id fallback when unknown); `Posted` date as `YYYY-MM-DD · HH:MM UTC`. No footer / action buttons. **V2App.jsx wiring**: new `v22DirectorySelectedRfp` state next to the existing Claim state; mutual exclusion enforced at the click-handler level (each setter clears the other); both states cleared on Directory close + globe-button toggle. Panel mount shell mirrors the Claim mount (position:fixed, top:61, right:0, bottom:28, width:480, zIndex:200). **Footer** rolls forward to `v0.17.0`. Spec gains a new §8.8 covering the factory shape (unchanged from Phase 16.0 skeletal), click pipeline, Detail Panel content, mutual-exclusion semantics, and forward pointers to 17.0.1 / 17.1 / 17.2 / 17.2.1 / 17.5+. Polish-backlog #192 partially addressed (the "buyer-side review surface (RFP Detail Panel)" read-only portion).
- **§6 / §3.5 / §8.2 — Phase 16.2.11 (Phase 16 wrap-up — Alice grouping + footer + parent-layer visual calibration):** Three loose ends closed before Phase 17 opens. (1) **Alice per-Claim y-band fix in `buildV22Canvas`**: chain anchor reads the evaluated Claim's y (was `EA.granteeAssetId` Asset y in Phase 16.2.2). The granteeAssetId Asset is the evaluator's grantee — present on the evaluator's own canvas (Bob's bAvionics) but absent on grantor-direction views like Alice's (Bob's bAvionics isn't on Alice's canvas), so Phase 16.2.2's chains fell through to symmetric-distribution fallback and scattered. The new policy uses Claim.y first, falls back to granteeAssetId.y, and finally Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY (defensive). `anchorIdForOrigin` also switches to Claim.id so pass-1's "one chain per anchor group" semantics match — each Claim is its own group. Bob's view is unaffected because pulled Claims at `symmetricRowY(i)` coincide with owned Assets at `symmetricRowY(i)`; Alice's view now exhibits clean horizontal banding per the brief's per-Claim y-band rule. Note: Asset-position-clustering-near-referencing-Claim is a separate, more substantial concern (requires re-architecting Asset placement) and was scoped out per the brief's escape clause — it can land as a dedicated phase. (2) **Footer version label** bumped `v0.16.2.0` → `v0.16.2.11` in V2App.jsx. The backtrack-hotfix freeze formally ends here. (3) **Parent canvas Claim cards opt into disclosure-type colored borders + tinted backgrounds** (mirror of Phase 16.2.10's Directory treatment). `buildV22Canvas` computes a `_disclosureType` per Claim node — own Claims = 'full', pulled Claims = type of the DA where `grantee.party === actor.party && subject.kind === 'claim' && subject.id === claim.id`. V2Canvas passes `disclosureType={node.category === 'claim' ? node._disclosureType : undefined}` to both `AssetNode` and `AssetNodeMini`. The Phase 16.2.10 disclosure-type branches in AssetNode.jsx now fire on parent. Bad-health overrides disclosure-type per the priority chain (e.g., Bob sees Alice's PRM with red border for bad health + amber-tinted background for selective). (4) **Non-Claim node types (Actor, Asset, Eval Result, PoE) hover/select indigo**: new `hoverSelectColor` derived from `node.category` in both AssetNode and AssetNodeMini — Claims use amber (preserves discrete hover/select state distinct from disclosure default), non-Claims use bright indigo (`var(--accent-indigo)`, 100% saturation vs WARM_BORDER's 40% blend). Three sites updated per component: inner border, outer selection ring, inner-div hover background. AssetNodeDot left unchanged. (5) **Phase 16 is officially closed** with this phase. Forward queue: Phase 17.0 — RFP factory promotion + buyer post flow + RFP Detail Panel. Footer rolls forward to v0.16.2.11.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.10 (disclosure-type colored borders + tinted backgrounds on Directory Claim cards):** Mid-LOD (`AssetNodeMini`) and full-LOD (`AssetNode`) Directory Claim cards now carry the disclosure-type color signal already present on dots (full → indigo, selective → amber, proof-only → green). A new `disclosureType` prop is added to both components and threaded through `DirectoryLayer.jsx`'s `placed` dot → `allDots` → card render pipeline. When `disclosureType` is one of `'full' | 'selective' | 'proofonly'` AND no higher-priority state applies (declined/revoked → red; provisional → grey; hover/selected → amber; bad-health → red), the card's border color signals the disclosure level via `var(--accent-indigo / amber / green)`. A 12% `color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-card) 88%, var(--accent-*))` background tint applies the same color as a subtle wash — visible but not aggressive. **Priority insertion**: in `AssetNodeMini` the disclosure-type branches sit between `hasBadHealth` and `WARM_BORDER` for border, and replace the plain `var(--bg-card)` default for background. In `AssetNode` (full) the same border insertion applies; for background, the disclosure-type branches sit ABOVE `isCounterpartyNode` — rationale: disclosure type is the more-specific signal (which-data-is-visible); counterparty is the less-specific signal (whose-data-is-it). Directory cards always carry `disclosureType` so they always land in the new branch; parent canvas Claims (which don't pass the prop) fall through to the existing `isCounterpartyNode` / `var(--bg-card)` chain unchanged. **Parent canvas opt-in deferred**: V2Canvas invocations of `AssetNode`/`AssetNodeMini` don't pass `disclosureType`, so parent-layer rendering is unchanged until parent wires it. Strict-equality conditionals (`disclosureType === 'full'` etc.) return falsy for `undefined` and fall through cleanly. **RFP markers** stay as hollow squares at every LOD (no card swap). **Hover/select precedence** preserved: hovering a Directory card switches its border to amber (existing behavior), overriding disclosure-type; selection adds the outer thick amber ring on top. Worth assessing in a future polish pass whether the disclosure color should peek through (e.g., bottom-edge accent or persistent left-edge stripe). **Priority #1 (Directory appearance and feel) closes with this phase.** Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.9 (card-click pan-to-center + AssetNodeMini hover-preview offset):** Two surgical QA-driven fixes following Phase 16.2.8. **Item 1**: Phase 16.2.7's `onCardClick` deliberately omitted the animated pan/zoom that the dot-click handler runs, on the (wrong) rationale that the user is "already viewing the card." Without the pan, clicking a card leaves the Detail Panel partially covering the card — the exact thing the dot-click handler's `panelOffsetWorld = (PANEL_W / 2) / zoom` correction was built to prevent. Fix: append the four-line `animatedPanToWithZoom(d.x + panelOffsetWorld, d.y, targetZoom, 500)` block to `onCardClick`, mirroring the dot-click handler exactly. `targetZoom = zoomRef.current` — no zoom change, just pan; same 500 ms ease as dot click. `animatedPanToWithZoom` added to the `useCallback` deps. **Item 2**: `AssetNodeMini`'s portal-tooltip in `AssetNode.jsx` centers the WRAPPER on `tooltipPos` via `translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0.85)`. But the wrapper is `CARD_W + ACTION_BAR_W = 244 px` wide; the visible card body is the LEFT 210 px, the action bar is the RIGHT 34 px. So `tooltipPos.x` lands at the wrapper centre, putting the visible body's centre at `tooltipPos.x − (ACTION_BAR_W × 0.85) / 2 = tooltipPos.x − 14.45 px`. On parent canvas the offset is masked by the mini-card's zoom-driven scale; on Directory with unscaled 160 px mini-cards the offset is obvious — the mini-card's amber hover border peeks out on the right of the preview. Fix: change the transform to `translate(calc(-50% + ${(ACTION_BAR_W * 0.85) / 2}px), -50%) scale(0.85)`. The translate is applied in post-scale visual px (the CSS matrix combines scale + translate so the translate value already reads as visual px), so `(ACTION_BAR_W × 0.85) / 2 = 14.45 px` is the correct shift. Single one-line transform change; same fix improves parent-canvas mini-LOD preview alignment subtly. No changes to `AssetNodeDot`'s tooltip (different render path, anchor-based positioning, no offset issue Andrew flagged). No changes to RFP rendering, cluster labels, mock data, or any other Directory state. Phase 16.1.4 / 16.1.5 / 16.2.3 / 16.2.6.1 / 16.2.6.4 / 16.2.6.5 / 16.2.6.6 / 16.2.7 / 16.2.8 defensive settings preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.8 (Directory Claim card population — CLAIM/minibar/badges):** Phase 16.2.7 shipped the LOD swap by passing raw `claim` (output of `makeClaim`) as the `node` prop, so cards rendered the title and owner but nothing else: no "CLAIM" type label, no minibar, no badges. Root cause: `AssetNode` / `AssetNodeMini` read `node.category` for the CATEGORY_CONFIG lookup, `node.displayHealth || node.health` for the HealthBar, and `node._activeBadges` for badge chips — none of those fields exist on raw `makeClaim` output. The fix routes Directory Claims through the existing `claimToNode(claim, rollup, x, y)` helper (a local function in `v2_2Data.js` already used by `buildV22Canvas` on the parent canvas) and seeds mock rollup data per Claim. **Item 1 — mock distribution helpers** added in `v2_2Data.js` near `claimToNode`. `mockClaimHealth(claimId)` returns a `{ ok, warn, bad }` rollup keyed by claim.id via `seededRandom(hashString(claimId + ':health'))` (PRNG composed from the existing Phase 16.2.6 helpers) — distribution: 50% no minibar / 5% all-green ≥ 2 badges / 5% all-green 1 badge / 10% all-green no badges / 15% has-bad / 15% mixed (ok+warn). `mockClaimBadges(claimId, health)` returns `[{ id, badgeName, badgeVersion, issuerParty }]` — eligibility gated on `health.bad === 0 && health.warn === 0 && health.ok > 0`. Bucket re-roll uses the same `:health` salt so the two helpers stay in sync. Inline `MOCK_BADGE_NAMES` (12 standards: ISO 27001, SOC 2 Type II, ITAR Compliance, DO-178C Level A, EN 9100, AS9100D, CMMC Level 3, NIST 800-171, FIPS 140-3, DO-254 Level B, MIL-STD-810H, RTCA DO-160G) and `MOCK_BADGE_ISSUERS` (5 issuers including AuditCo + 4 mock audit-style organisations). Badge shape `{ id, badgeName, badgeVersion, issuerParty }` matches `BadgeChipContainer.jsx`'s `ShieldTooltipContent` + `OverflowTooltipContent` consumers verbatim (confirmed by inspection — no icon, no color field, badges all render via the constant indigo shield SVG). **Item 2 — view-builder plumb** in `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole`: after the existing `otherClusters` build, a new `buildClaimNode(claim)` local helper combines `mockClaimHealth` + `mockClaimBadges` + `claimToNode(claim, { health, claimCount: 0 }, 0, 0)` + an `_activeBadges` attachment, then walks every cluster's `publicClaims` + `umbrellaClaims` to populate `cluster.nodesByClaimId = new Map<claimId, node>`. The same helper walks `ownClaims` to populate a top-level `ownNodesByClaimId` returned alongside the other view fields. Raw `Claim` objects on the cluster (publicClaims / umbrellaClaims / ownClaims) stay untouched — they continue flowing through `onClaimDotClick → V2App's Detail Panel`, which reads `referencedAssetIds`, `acknowledgments`, `pin`, etc. directly off the raw `makeClaim` output (the enriched node has `referencedEvidenceIds` instead, which would break the Panel). **Item 3 — DirectoryLayer plumb** in `computeLayout`: `buildItems` reads `cluster.nodesByClaimId` and attaches the enriched node to each public/umbrella item alongside the raw `claim`; the active-actor own-cluster path reads `directoryData.ownNodesByClaimId`. The dot construction loop in `computeLayout` (where `placed = { x, y, colorVar, kind, claim, rfp, type, clusterIdx }` is built) now also includes `node`. The `allDots.push` flatten step propagates `node` so the JSX card overlay render reads `d.node || d.claim` (raw `claim` fallback covers the edge case where enrichment failed — defensive only). **Click flow preserved**: `onCardClick` and the dot-click handler both continue to pass raw `d.claim` to `onClaimDotClick` — the Detail Panel still receives the same shape it did before this phase. **Distribution verified** via probe across 22,994 enriched nodes on Bob's view: 49.9% no minibar, 20.0% all-green, 15.2% has-bad, 15.0% mixed; 10.0% with ≥ 1 badge, 4.9% with ≥ 2 badges. Determinism check: two `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` calls produce identical health + badges for the same `claim.id`. No changes to `AssetNode.jsx`, `BadgeChipContainer.jsx`, RFP rendering, dot Three.js mesh rendering, or `makeClaim` output (raw Claims stay untouched everywhere). Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.7 (mini-card + full-card LOD on Directory + size tweak):** Three coordinated changes that close polish-backlog #200 (Directory mini-card swap) and tighten low-zoom rendering. **Item 1 — size tweak**: `BASE_DOT_FACTOR` 0.85 → 0.70 and `BASE_RFP_FACTOR` 0.95 → 0.80 in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`. Linear-regime marker sizes shrink ~16-18%, so dots and squares feel lighter at zoom 15–30% without changing the capped (zoom ≥ ~0.65) appearance. New cap-engagement zooms: ~0.654 for dots (was 0.539), ~0.573 for RFPs (was 0.482). **Item 2 — `MAX_ZOOM` lift**: 1.5 → 5.0. Existing wheel handler, clampZoom, and zoomFit already read `MAX_ZOOM` — no callsite changes needed. The zoom indicator's `Math.round(zoom × 100) + '%'` formula now displays 5.0 as "500%"; the percentages re-scale but the formula is unchanged. **Item 3 — LOD swap for Claim dots only**: at zoom ≥ `MID_LOD_THRESHOLD` (= `MINI_CARD_W / DOT_GRID = 160 / 48 = 3.333…`), Claim dots render as `AssetNodeMini` HTML overlays positioned via `worldToScreen` at the dot's screen position. At zoom ≥ `LOD_THRESHOLD` (= `CARD_W / DOT_GRID = 210 / 48 = 4.375`), they swap to full-size `AssetNode`. Both `AssetNode` and `AssetNodeMini` reused verbatim from `AssetNode.jsx` (Phase 16.2.7 hard rule: no edits to that file). Card dimension constants (`CARD_W = 210`, `CARD_H = 96`, `MINI_CARD_W = 160`, `MINI_CARD_H = 48`) mirrored in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` because `AssetNode.jsx` doesn't export them — documented as a sync'd duplicate. **Density invariant**: Directory cards render at FIXED NATURAL PIXEL SIZE (no `transform: scale(${zoom})` — only `translate(-50%, -50%)` to center on dot's screen position). At MID_LOD_THRESHOLD = 3.333 the screen-space spacing between dots is `3.333 × DOT_GRID = 160 px` — exactly MINI_CARD_W; at LOD_THRESHOLD = 4.375 it's `4.375 × 48 = 210 px` — exactly CARD_W. So cards never overlap horizontally regardless of zoom level within each LOD regime. **Implementation**: (a) new render block in JSX between the RFP-owner-label block and the tooltip — iterates `layout.allDots.filter(d => d.kind !== 'rfp' && d.claim)`, projects each to screen via `worldToScreen`, viewport-culls (skip cards outside viewport + one-card buffer on each edge), wraps in absolutely-positioned `<div>` with `translate(-50%, -50%)`, z-index 1500 (1600 for `pinned?.claim === d.claim`). (b) `useEffect` toggles `dotsMesh.visible = zoom < MID_LOD_THRESHOLD`, marks `dirtyRef` to trigger a render — hides the dot InstancedMesh in mid/full-LOD. (c) `useEffect` clears `hover` state when `zoom >= MID_LOD_THRESHOLD` (raycast against the hidden mesh would yield no hits, so hover would stick to its pre-LOD value otherwise). `pinned` survives LOD transitions so the relevant card / dot renders with `isSelected={true}`. (d) `ClaimTooltipCard` render gated on `zoom < MID_LOD_THRESHOLD` — cards have their own internal hover behavior. (e) New `onCardClick` `useCallback` mirrors the dot click flow (sets `pinned`, calls `onClaimDotClick`) but skips the animated pan/zoom — user clicked a card they can already see. **RFP markers stay as hollow squares at all LODs** (RFP card swap deferred to a future phase paired with RFP Detail Panels). **Cluster pillbox labels stay as `PillboxLabel` at all LODs**. **Verified** on dev server (1400×900 viewport, Bob's view): at zoom 366% (mid-LOD), 23 AssetNodeMini cards rendered showing procedural Claim names with proper non-overlapping spacing. At zoom 500% (full-LOD), AssetNode full cards rendered at 210×96 px. Dot mesh hidden in both regimes. Phase 16.1.4 / 16.1.5 / 16.2.3 / 16.2.6.1 / 16.2.6.4 / 16.2.6.5 / 16.2.6.6 defensive settings preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.6.6 (RFP marker scaling + tinted fill):** QA on 16.2.6.5's RFP cluster expansion surfaced three related issues from the marker's fixed-world-unit outer size (`RFP_OUTER_SIZE = DOT_GRID × 1.2`) clashing with the zoom-aware dot sizing from Phase 16.2.6.2: (a) RFP squares oversized at high zoom (~2× dot size at 76% zoom — dots cap at 22 px on screen, squares rendered at ~44 px uncapped); (b) borders looked proportionally tiny on the oversized squares; (c) adjacent grid-cell squares (at DOT_GRID center-to-center spacing) overlapped each other because the 1.2 outer extended past the cell boundary; (d) markers read as "empty rectangles" with no fill differentiating them from dot clusters. All four fixed in one pass. **Item 1 — new constants + helper** in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`: `BASE_RFP_FACTOR = 0.95` (matches dots' 5% inter-cell gap pattern), `MAX_SCREEN_RFP_PX = 22` (same cap as dots), `RFP_FILL_ALPHA = 0.15`, `RFP_BASE_OUTER = DOT_GRID × BASE_RFP_FACTOR = 45.6 wu` (replaces 16.2.6.3's fixed RFP_OUTER_SIZE; the old name is kept as a backwards-compat alias for label-positioning formulas). New helper `computeRfpWorldSize(zoom)` parallels `computeDotWorldSize` with the new constants — cap threshold ≈ 0.48 (slightly below the dot threshold 0.54 because RFP base factor 0.95 > dot 0.85, so the cap engages sooner). **Item 2 — outline geometry** built at `makeHollowSquareGeometry(RFP_BASE_OUTER, RFP_BORDER)` instead of fixed 1.2 × DOT_GRID. **Item 3 — tinted fill InstancedMesh**: new `rfpFillMeshRef` + companion `THREE.InstancedMesh` with `PlaneGeometry(RFP_BASE_OUTER, RFP_BASE_OUTER)` + `MeshBasicMaterial({ color: indigo, transparent: true, opacity: 0.15 })`. Added BEFORE the outline mesh in scene-add order so the outline draws on top of the fill. Disposal added to cleanup. **Item 4 — populate loop** updated: per-instance matrix bakes scale via `m.makeScale(s, s, 1); m.setPosition(d.x, -d.y, 0)` and writes to BOTH meshes per instance. Both meshes' `count` and `instanceMatrix.needsUpdate` updated. **Item 5 — `rfpScaleRef` + rescale-on-zoom useEffect**: parallel to `dotScaleRef` and the dot rescale effect. Updates both meshes' per-instance matrices when desired scale differs from baked by ≥ 1e-4. Effect declared BEFORE the border-rebuild effect so `rfpScaleRef.current` is up-to-date when read. **Item 6 — border-rebuild formula** updated to `desiredBorder = RFP_BORDER_SCREEN_PX / (scale × zoom)`. Below the cap (scale = 1) reduces to the pre-16.2.6.6 `RFP_BORDER_SCREEN_PX / zoom`; above the cap `scale × zoom` is constant so the border settles on a single geometry. Geometry rebuilds use `makeHollowSquareGeometry(RFP_BASE_OUTER, desiredBorder)` — base outer never changes; only border varies. Fill mesh geometry never rebuilds. **Verified**: 99 PillboxLabel elements + 118 RFP marker instances rendered correctly on Bob's view at default zoom 0.15. Dot rendering unchanged from 16.2.6.5. Mixed clusters (Lighthouse Programs / Marigold Systems / Quarry Industries / Auger Defense) preserve the dots-inner-squares-outer pattern with the new sizing. No NaN warnings; only the pre-existing V2Canvas2 setState-in-render warning (unrelated to this phase). Phase 16.1.4 / 16.1.5 / 16.2.3 / 16.2.6.1 / 16.2.6.4 / 16.2.6.5 defensive settings preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.6.5 (RFP cluster expansion: 20 RFP-only + 4 mixed actors):** Populates the Directory's previously-empty bottom third with buyer-side signal and demonstrates the mixed-actor case (party with both Claims and RFPs). Six items: (1) **20 new RFP-only mock actors** in `v2_2Data.js`'s `PHASE_16_2_6_5_RFP_ONLY_BUNDLES` (98 total RFPs across NavalSys Authority through Vesper Defense, 1-12 RFPs each). Each `makeActor` with `role: 'buyer'` (distinct from supplier mocks); no Claims, no Assets, no DAs. (2) **4 new mixed actors** in `PHASE_16_2_6_5_MIXED_BUNDLES` (Lighthouse Programs / Marigold Systems / Quarry Industries / Auger Defense — 170 total Claims + 19 total RFPs). Each runs through both `seedMockSupplierActor` (Claims half via `generateClaimSpecsForVertical`) and `generateRfpsForActor` (RFP half). (3) **New exported `generateRfpsForActor(actor, count)` helper** using existing `hashString` + `seededRandom` (mulberry32) seeded with `${actor.party}:rfps:${count}` for determinism. Templates: 6 format strings × {prefix, component, mission, program} dictionaries → procedural RFP names like "Aegis Phase II Thermal Control Subsystem RFP". (4) **`buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` extended**: new `PRIMARY_PARTIES_FOR_ORPHAN_RFP = {GovCo, MicroCo, AuditCo, ChipCo}` set. After the existing `otherClusters` build, each cluster gets `cluster.rfps = rfpsByOwner.get(owner) || []`. Clusters are upserted for RFP-only owners not in the primary set (e.g. NavalSys Authority becomes a cluster). The returned `otherRfps` is narrowed to orphan-only — preserves the 16.2.6.3 GovCo-on-non-Bob-views label-adjacent behavior. (5) **`computeLayout` updates** in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`: `buildItems` now also returns `rfpItems` extracted from `cluster.rfps`; new `ACTOR_KIND` taxonomy classifies each spec as `ACTIVE` / `CLAIMS_CLUSTER` / `RFP_CLUSTER` / `MIXED_CLUSTER` after spec build; initial seed positions branch on kind (`RFP_CLUSTER` → bottom third `[0.70, 0.94] × usableHeight`, `MIXED_CLUSTER` → cross-zone band `[0.60, 0.70]`, `CLAIMS_CLUSTER` → upper region `[0.10, 0.65]`); new target-area formula splits Claims (`DOT_GRID²`) from RFPs (`DOT_GRID² × RFP_AREA_FACTOR` where `RFP_AREA_FACTOR = 1.44` matches the `RFP_OUTER_SIZE = DOT_GRID × 1.2` square's relative area); hard y-clamp inside Lloyd's loop pins RFP-only seeds to `≥ RFP_ZONE_TOP_THRESHOLD` after each centroid step. Mixed seeds move freely. (6) **Per-RFP-marker owner-label suppression**: the 16.2.6.3 per-marker label render block adds an `if (d.clusterIdx !== -1) return null` guard so only orphan RFPs get their own pill label — RFPs inside clusters inherit identity from the cluster's centered label. This caps the per-marker render count at 1 (GovCo's Sentinel-4 on non-Bob views) instead of 117. **Probe verified**: actors 77 → 101 (+24), claims 22,824 → 22,994 (+170), assets 22,821 → 22,991 (+170), DAs 91,321 → 92,001 (+680), rfps 1 → 118 (+117). Lighthouse cluster on Alice's view carries 50 claims + 6 rfps. NavalSys cluster carries 12 rfps + 0 claims. Bob's view has ownRfps=1, otherRfps=0 (GovCo active, no orphans). Procedural RFP names deterministic across reloads. **Known regressions surfaced + accepted per brief**: cluster overflow grew 18 → 20 clusters (Bowsprit Defense + Andromeda Defense now join the list at the new seed density). Brief said "expected 4-8 cluster overflow growth" — actual +2 well within bounds. Lloyd's clamp on RFP-only seeds disrupts convergence mildly. Phase 16.1.4 / 16.1.5 / 16.2.3 / 16.2.6.1 defensive settings preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.6.4 (label z-order fix — smaller clusters on top):** Single-item phase resolving label-overlap issues surfaced after 16.2.6.3's 25-actor expansion. With 77 cluster labels at the current density, some small clusters' label pills sat partially buried behind larger neighbors' pills in screen space — most visibly ChipCo behind Prism Aerospace, MicroCo behind Precision Components, and Photonix truncated to "Photo..." behind Substrate Dynamics. Resolution: **z-order cluster labels inversely by `dotCount`** — the largest cluster gets the lowest z-rank (`Z_BASE_CLUSTER_LABEL = 100`), each successive smaller cluster gets `+1`. Trade-off accepted per brief: large clusters' label pills may have corners clipped when adjacent to small ones, but the larger cluster's identity is also conveyed by its visual footprint (a 3,250-dot Helios cluster is unmistakable regardless of whether its pill has a corner covered). Implementation: new module-level constants `Z_BASE_CLUSTER_LABEL = 100` + `Z_RFP_LABEL = Z_BASE_CLUSTER_LABEL + 1000 = 1100` in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`. `PillboxLabel` accepts a new `zIndex` prop (default 4, applied to the absolutely-positioned outer container — not inner content, per brief). Cluster-label render block sorts `layout.allClusters` by `dots.length` descending once per render, builds a party→rank Map, and threads `zIndex={Z_BASE_CLUSTER_LABEL + rank}` to each PillboxLabel. RFP owner-actor labels (Phase 16.2.6.3 Item 4) get `zIndex={Z_RFP_LABEL}` so they always render above every cluster label. Sort stability via Array.prototype.sort guarantees consistent ordering when dotCounts tie. Empty-cluster active actors (Bob's GovCo with 0 own claims, Carol's anonymous AuditCo) land at the top of the small end of the sort — fine; their labels always render on top of any colliding neighbour. **Verification**: DOM probe on Bob's view confirms Helios (3,250 dots) at z=101, descending through to Photonix at z=172, MicroCo at z=173, GovCo at z=174 — z-rank inversely correlates with dotCount as intended. On Alice's view the GovCo RFP-marker owner-label renders at z=1100, above every cluster label. No regression: positioning, font-size scaling, and content unchanged from 16.2.6.3. Phase 16.1.4 / 16.1.5 / 16.2.3 / 16.2.6.1 defensive settings preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.6.3 (more mock actors + label scaling + RFP hollow square + top inset trim):** Six surgical polish items addressing post-16.2.6.2 QA. (1) **25 more mock supplier Actors** (5 medium + 20 small) added to a new `PHASE_16_2_6_3_NEW_MOCK_ACTORS` constant in `v2_2Data.js`, concatenated with `PHASE_16_2_6_NEW_MOCK_ACTORS` at the existing expansion loop. All 25 reuse 16.2.6 verticals + the same procedural Claim spec pipeline. New totals: +1,215 Claims, +1,215 Assets, +4,860 DAs (4 per Claim) vs 16.2.6.2 baseline; Directory now hosts ~22,824 dots across 77 Actors (was 52). Cluster boundaries grow ~48% — more visible "butting" between adjacent clusters. (2) **Zoom-aware cluster label scaling**: new `BASE_LABEL_FONT_PX = 14` / `MIN_LABEL_FONT_PX = 11` / `MAX_LABEL_FONT_PX = 18` + `computeLabelFontSize(zoom)` helper (`14 × sqrt(zoom)` clamped to [MIN, MAX]). Behaviors: zoom 0.15 → 11px (floor), zoom 1.0 → 14px, zoom 1.5 → 17.1px. `PillboxLabel` accepts a `fontPx` prop; padding switched from fixed `3px 8px` to em-based `0.3em 0.7em` + `lineHeight: 1` + `display: flex; alignItems: center; justifyContent: center` so the pillbox scales proportionally and text stays vertically + horizontally centered at every zoom (the off-center drift QA observed at zoom 1.5 was a stacked effect of font ascender/descender pushing text upward inside fixed-pixel padding — flex + lineHeight 1 flattens that). Both cluster labels and RFP owner-labels read the helper per-render. (3) **Wave reveal speed 3000 → 4500 wu/sec** in `directoryLoadAnimation.js`'s `DEFAULT_WAVE_SPEED`. Animation duration drops ~3.0s → ~2.0s for cleaner demo pacing. Skip-on-click + outline-fade behavior unchanged. (4) **RFP marker owner-actor label**: new render block iterates `layout.allDots` for `kind === 'rfp'`, positions a PillboxLabel below the marker (`d.y + RFP_OUTER_SIZE/2 + DOT_GRID` in world coords), suppresses when the owning actor IS the active actor (so Bob's view doesn't duplicate "GovCo" next to its own cluster's centered pillbox). On Alice / Carol / Dave views, the bottom-anchor GovCo RFP marker gets a "GovCo" label adjacent — fixes the orphaned-marker visual from prior phases. (5) **RFP marker → hollow square** primitive (was hollow circle, cyan). New constants `RFP_OUTER_SIZE = DOT_GRID × 1.2` (= 57.6 wu) + `RFP_BORDER_SCREEN_PX = 2` + initial `RFP_BORDER = 2/0.15 ≈ 13.3 wu`. Geometry built via existing `makeHollowSquareGeometry`; material color flipped to indigo (`--accent-indigo`). New `rfpBorderRef` + zoom-change `useEffect` rebuilds the shared RFP geometry whenever `RFP_BORDER_SCREEN_PX / zoom` differs by ≥5% from the baked thickness, so the on-screen border stays ≈ 2 px across the full zoom range. Single ShapeGeometry rebuild per zoom transition — negligible cost. No raycast/click pipeline existed for RFPs pre-16.2.6.3, so the brief's "Detail Panel on RFP click" verification is a pre-existing gap (filed implicitly for the RFP MVP work in 17.0–17.2). (6) **`DOMAIN_INSET_TOP` 500 → 475** = `64 css px / 0.15` (header world height at MIN_ZOOM) + 1 × DOT_GRID. Static value chosen over dynamic `getBoundingClientRect()` measurement so the Directory layout pipeline doesn't depend on parent-component DOM mount order. Visible top buffer below the header now matches the L/R 1-cell edge buffer. Usable area grows ~0.3% (negligible Lloyd's impact). (7) **Polish-backlog #201** added: Directory RFP cluster expansion at canvas bottom third (Open / Effort M). **Known regressions surfaced + accepted per brief's pinned conventions** (deferred candidates): cluster overflow now affects 18 clusters (was 10 in 16.2.6.2) — more seeds competing for the same usable area: Atlas 208/2950, Apex 225/325, Aurora 226/800, Beacon 203/900, Cascade 176/350, Citadel 148/250, Eos 212/225, Helios 470/3250, Kestrel 74/75, Lyra 196/225, Meridian 205/250, Orion 383/650, Polaris 226/2600, Quantum 188/400, Sirius 729/1050, Solstice 139/700, Vega 285/1200, Vortex 215/2300. ChipCo still uses 16.2.6.1's shrink-collapse fallback. Brief's "Lloyd's at the current scale is already non-converging... Adding many more dots will exacerbate this... Accept" rule applies. Phase 16.1.4 lifecycle + Phase 16.1.5 InstancedMesh defensive settings + Phase 16.2.3 camera-init NaN guard + Phase 16.2.6.1 umbrella SVG finite-vertex guard preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.6.2 (edge buffers + zoom range + dot scaling + wipe origin):** Four surgical polish items addressing post-16.2.6.1 QA. (1) **Voronoi-domain insets** — four new constants in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` (`DOMAIN_INSET_LEFT/RIGHT = DOT_GRID`, `DOMAIN_INSET_TOP = 500`, `DOMAIN_INSET_BOTTOM = 250`, world units) shrink the tessellation rectangle inward from full canvas bounds. Left/right reserve a 1-cell edge buffer so cluster dots aren't visibly cut off at the canvas edge. Top/bottom reserve world-space equivalent of the app header (~61 css px) + footer legend (~32 css px) at MIN_ZOOM = 0.15 (the worst case — at higher zoom the chrome covers less world area). Both Lloyd-iteration `delaunay.voronoi(...)` call sites updated to pass the inset bounds; cells get clipped at those edges so dots can't be placed in inset regions. Lloyd's target-sum check now compared against `usableArea = (CANVAS_WIDTH - LEFT - RIGHT) × (CANVAS_HEIGHT - TOP - BOTTOM)` instead of full canvas area; ratio is now ~0.78 (was 0.73 against full canvas) — still within bounds, no over-pack warning. (2) **`MAX_ZOOM` 4.0 → 1.5** — paired with the dot-size cap in Item 3; beyond 150% the canvas yields no useful new detail until the deferred Directory mini-card swap (filed as polish-backlog #200) ships. Zoom indicator continues to use `Math.round(zoom * 100)`. (3) **Non-linear dot scaling** — new constants `BASE_DOT_FACTOR = 0.85` (was 0.95 in 16.2.6.1) + `MAX_SCREEN_DOT_PX = 22`; new helper `computeDotWorldSize(zoom)` returns the world diameter that yields `min(linearScreenSize, 22 px)` on screen at the current zoom. Cap threshold ≈ 0.54. Implementation: per-instance matrix bakes scale via `m.makeScale(s, s, 1); m.setPosition(d.x, -d.y, 0)` — NOT mesh-level scale (which would also scale positions). New zoom-change `useEffect` rewrites all instance matrices when the scale changes; 22k matrix updates per zoom change is negligible. New `dotScaleRef` skips rewrites when the scale is unchanged (so wheel-zoom inside the linear regime has zero matrix cost). Initial layout's per-instance matrix also embeds the initial scale. Hit-target raycast scales with the per-instance matrix, so per-zoom hit areas stay matched to the visible dot size. `DOT_RADIUS` adjusted to `DOT_GRID × BASE_DOT_FACTOR / 2` (= 20.4) so the unscaled geometry matches the new baseline. (4) **Wipe origin default** moved from bottom-left (`'0% 100%'`) to bottom-center (`'50% 100%'`) in the `originStr` fallback — visually says "the network expands out from your active-actor anchor (canvas-bottom-center) toward the rest of the directory." The pre-pinned `wipeOrigin` (set by Phase 11.8 #44's node-double-click flow) still wins; the change only affects the default chrome-globe-click path. The 180% radius end-state still reaches all four viewport corners from the new origin. (5) **Polish-backlog #200** added: Directory mini-card swap at higher zoom. **Known regressions surfaced + accepted per brief's pinned conventions** (16.2.6.x candidates): (a) Lloyd's residual now 174.6 wu at 20-iter cap (≈ 3.6 × DOT_GRID = 48) — slightly worse than 16.2.6.1's 161 wu due to the 7% smaller usable area; brief's "If Lloyd's convergence degrades meaningfully due to the reduced usable area, surface the value but accept" rule applies. (b) Cluster overflow now affects 10 clusters (Helios 503/3250, Atlas 280/2950, Polaris 299/2600, Vortex 253/2300, Vega 523/1200, Aurora 618/800, Beacon 325/900, Solstice 268/700, Cascade 326/350, Quantum 341/400, Apex 201/325 — and ChipCo still falls back via shrink-collapse). AC #8 ("zero overflow warnings at the current 22k seed with the new smaller usable area") therefore unmet; brief explicitly allows surface + ship. NaN-CSS-left React warning from 16.2.6.1 not observed in this build — the umbrella SVG finite-vertex guard from 16.2.6.1 covered that path. Phase 16.1.4 lifecycle + Phase 16.1.5 InstancedMesh defensive settings + Phase 16.2.3 camera-init NaN guard preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.6.1 (dense organic cluster packing + ~22k dot expansion):** Three coordinated changes that resolve the trio of QA issues from Phase 16.2.6 — intra-cluster spiral gaps, canvas-fill emptiness, and super-jumbo sunflower overflow — via a single algorithmic shift: **dense Voronoi-clipped grid fill** replaces Vogel sunflower phyllotaxis. (1) **`packClusterDense`** in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`: each cluster's Voronoi polygon is shrunk inward by 1 DOT_GRID (creates the 2-cell inter-cluster gap when both neighbours shrink), every grid cell inside the shrunken polygon is enumerated (label hole excluded), sorted by distance from cluster centre, and the first N filled. New helpers `shrinkConvexPolygon` (per-vertex bisector miter; orientation-detected via signed area; collapse fallback returns `[]`) and reuses existing `pointInPolygon`. Removed `vogelPosition`, `GOLDEN_ANGLE`, `SUNFLOWER_SCALE`, `SUNFLOWER_MAX_ITER_PER_DOT`, `INTER_CLUSTER_BUFFER` (now intrinsic via polygon shrink), and `isInLabelHole` (inlined). **Inner cells touch** (no spiral gaps); **organic shapes preserved** (Voronoi-polygon-edged); **no Vogel pinwheel waste** so cells use full N × DOT_GRID² of area. (2) **Lloyd's target area formula** swap: was `canvasArea × (dots / totalDots)` (proportional but area-blind); now `(dots × DOT_GRID² + LABEL_HOLE_AREA) × BUFFER_OVERHEAD_FACTOR` (physically grounded; per-dot area + label hole + 1.20× packing/wobble overhead). Target-sum-vs-canvas-area surface added — warns if sum > canvas area (over-packed seed). At the new ~21,609-dot seed the ratio is 0.73 (well within the 70–80% target range). (3) **35 mock actor dot counts expanded ~6.5×** in `v2_2Data.js` (`PHASE_16_2_6_NEW_MOCK_ACTORS` table) — Helios 500→3250, Atlas 450→2950, Polaris 400→2600, Vortex 350→2300, and so on down to Cordite Labs 5→35. Same 35 actors, same verticals, same `generateClaimSpecsForVertical` procedural pipeline (combinatorial space ~10–30k unique names per vertical comfortably exceeds the new max-per-actor of 3,250). **New totals**: Directory now hosts ~21,609 dots (21,435 new mock + 157 existing mock + 17 switchable role Claims). +18,147 Claims, +18,147 stub Assets, +72,588 Disclosure Agreements relative to the 16.2.6 baseline. (4) **`DOT_RADIUS = DOT_GRID × 0.475`** (was 0.425) — diameter = `DOT_GRID × 0.95`; adjacent grid-aligned dots butt with a thin ~5% visible gap. (5) **`MAX_DOTS` bumped 10000 → 25000** for InstancedMesh capacity. **Defensive fixes** (per brief's pinned conventions): shrink-collapse fallback (`packClusterDense` falls back to the unshrunken cell with a `console.warn` if `shrinkConvexPolygon` returns < 3 vertices — produces a 1-cell buffer instead of 2 for that cluster); finite-vertex filter in the umbrella SVG path render (drops paths whose `screenPoints` contain NaN, which was producing "NaN is an invalid value for left" React warnings during the brief initial-mount race window). **Known regressions surfaced + accepted per brief pinned conventions, filed as 16.2.6.2 candidates**: (a) Lloyd's residual still 161 wu at the 20-iter cap (≈ 3.4 × DOT_GRID) — brief explicitly forbids unilaterally raising the cap; surface + ship; (b) cluster overflow on 9 clusters at the new counts — Helios 789/3250, Atlas 468/2950, Polaris 300/2600, Vortex 854/2300, Vega 946/1200, Beacon 520/900, Aurora 620/800, Solstice 268/700, Apex 202/325; (c) shrink collapse on ChipCo (14 dots) — falls back to unshrunken cell. Visual result remains the brief's intent: top 75% of canvas densely populated, organic cluster shapes, label holes preserved, inter-cluster buffer clearly visible, super-jumbos visibly massive. Phase 16.1.4 `shouldMountScene` lifecycle + Phase 16.1.5 InstancedMesh defensive settings + Phase 16.2.3 camera-init NaN guard preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.6 (seed expansion to ~3,288 dots):** 35 new mock supplier Actors added to the Directory, bringing the total to 52 (4 switchable primary roles + 12 existing mock from Phase 16.2 + 35 new mock from 16.2.6 + Radiant Network). New Claim count: 3,288 (heavy-tailed by design — 4 super-jumbo clusters carry ~50% of dots: Helios Industries 500, Atlas Avionics 450, Polaris Defense 400, Vortex Aerospace 350). New stub Assets: 3,288 (1 per Claim, same `seedMockSupplierActor` shape as 16.2). New Disclosure Agreements: 13,152 (4 per Claim: 1 own-asset + 1 own-claim + 1 claim-ref-asset + 1 public-directory). Every new Actor remains non-switchable (`user: null`, `credits: 0`) and discloses exclusively to the Radiant Network — no umbrella DAs to any of the four primary actors. **Procedural Claim names**: new helper `generateClaimSpecsForVertical(actorParty, vertical, count)` in `v2_2Data.js` produces deterministic, vertical-appropriate Claim names from a `VERTICAL_LEXICONS` table covering 21 verticals (20 hand-defined in the brief + `'Connectors & Backshells'` filled in to cover row 20's gap). Each name follows the `{family} {prefix}-{number} {docType}` shape; uniqueness within an actor enforced via 20-attempt retry + counter-fallback. Seeded `hashString(actorParty) → mulberry32(seed)` PRNG so the same `(actor, vertical, count)` tuple always yields identical specs across module reloads. Per-actor disclosure-type mix `(i × 7 + seed) % 100` produces a deterministic 60/25/15 indigo/amber/green spread per cluster (Helios verified: exactly 300/125/75 of 500). **Lloyd's iteration cap** bumped 10 → 20 in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` — at 52 seeds the residual still exceeds `DOT_GRID = 48` (observed 101.4 wu), surfaced via `console.warn` per the brief's "stop and surface, don't raise the cap unilaterally" rule. **Known regressions surfaced (filed as 16.2.6.1 candidates)**: (a) four super-jumbo clusters overflow the sunflower placement loop — Atlas Avionics fits 124/450 dots, Polaris Defense 120/400, Helios Industries 261/500, Vortex Aerospace 297/350 — because their Voronoi cells aren't sized proportional to dot count at this scale; (b) Lloyd's residual ≈ 2.1 × DOT_GRID even at the bumped cap. Both surface as `console.warn`. **Brief headline vs table arithmetic**: the brief's "3,328 total" headline disagrees with the dotCount column sum (3,288); the table is canonical and implementation matches it. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.5.1 (smooth cluster dot geometry):** Cluster-dot `CircleGeometry` segment count 16 → 64. With Phase 16.2.5's `DOT_RADIUS = 20.4` (7× larger than pre-16.2.5), each dot's silhouette was visibly polygonal at zoom levels above ~100% — 16 segments was fine when dots were 3 wu, no longer enough at 20.4 wu. 64 segments produces a perceptually smooth circle at every supported zoom (up to MAX_ZOOM = 4.0). Per-frame cost is one fragment-shader pass over the same per-instance matrices — negligible at the current ≤ 10k dot scale. RFP hollow circles (separate `makeHollowCircleGeometry` path, defaults to 32 segments) left untouched; if QA surfaces polygonal artifacts on the cyan hollow circles too, a follow-up can apply the same bump there. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.5 (grid alignment + dot rendering hotfix):** QA on Phase 16.2.4 surfaced two presentation issues — (a) cluster dots didn't visually align with the background dot matrix (background spaced at 48 wu via `GRID_SPACING = DOT_GRID × 4`, cluster snap at 12 wu, producing sub-cell offsets every 4 cells) and (b) cluster dots were barely visible at the 15% default zoom (3-wu radius → 0.45 screen px). Hotfix reconciles the constants. **`DOT_GRID` 12 → 48** — matches `GRID_SPACING`'s actual world-space spacing so every sunflower-snapped position lands on a background-grid intersection. `GRID_SPACING` redefined as `DOT_GRID` directly (was `DOT_GRID × 4`) — point count + visible grid density unchanged in world units. Background-grid origin snapped to a multiple of `GRID_SPACING` (`Math.ceil(GRID_MARGIN / GRID_SPACING) × GRID_SPACING`) so grid points pass through `(0, 0)` and align with cluster snap. **`DOT_RADIUS = DOT_GRID × 0.425` (≈20.4 wu)** — fills ~85% of grid cell. **`SUNFLOWER_SCALE` 1.7 → 1.0** — Vogel adjacent-arm spacing matches grid (one cell apart at the cluster surface). Combined effect: pixelated sunflower clusters that read as solid filled discs around the centered label hole at default zoom, with each dot occupying nearly a full grid cell on a matching background. **Derived constants** all scale automatically (`LABEL_HOLE_W = 6×DOT_GRID = 288`, `LABEL_HOLE_H = 3×DOT_GRID = 144`, `INTER_CLUSTER_BUFFER = 2×DOT_GRID = 96`, `LLOYD_CONVERGENCE_DELTA = DOT_GRID = 48`). **Lloyd's convergence** at the current 12-actor seed reports max displacement 193.8 wu after 10 iterations (≈4× the new 48-wu threshold; was ≈16× the old 12-wu threshold). Per the brief, accepted with `console.warn` rather than unilaterally raising the cap — Phase 16.2.6's denser seed is the canonical convergence test. **Brief deviation surfaced**: Item 2's "cluster footprint shrinks by ~2.9×" calculation assumed `DOT_GRID` stayed at 12; with `DOT_GRID = 48` and `SCALE = 1.0`, cluster footprints actually grow ~2.4× (sunflower radius `sqrt(N) × DOT_GRID × SCALE` = `sqrt(N) × 48 vs sqrt(N) × 20.4` previously). The visual reads as denser per the brief's intent because dot size grew 6.8×, so each cell is now "filled" rather than sparsely populated. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.4 (Voronoi packing + sunflower clusters):** `DirectoryLayer.jsx` redesigned end-to-end to deliver a denser galactic-view first impression. **Canvas shrinks** from 17280×11170 (16.2.3) to 11520×7447 — 16" MBP logical resolution at the new 15% default zoom. `MIN_ZOOM` 0.1 → 0.15; `INITIAL_ZOOM` 0.1 → 0.15. `OWN_CLUSTER_ANCHOR_X = 5760`, `OWN_CLUSTER_ANCHOR_Y = 5957.6`. Smaller canvas + same seed = denser-feeling clusters. **Voronoi packing** (replacing the 16.2.3 polar Poisson disc fan-out): each cluster gets a Voronoi cell from a Lloyd-iterated centroidal tessellation seeded with the active Actor's own cluster pinned at the anchor (seed index 0) and other clusters' seeds at hash-derived deterministic positions inside the canvas. Lloyd's iteration pulls each non-pinned seed toward its cell's area-weighted centroid with `stepFactor = 0.5 + 0.5*tanh(area_error)` — deficit cells take bigger steps, overflow cells smaller steps. Iteration cap: 10. Target area per cluster `∝ dot_count_i / total_dots × canvas_area`. **Sunflower clusters** (replacing the rectangular row/col grid): each cluster's dots are placed via Vogel phyllotaxis — angle = `i × GOLDEN_ANGLE` (~137.5°), radius = `sqrt(i + 0.5) × DOT_GRID × SUNFLOWER_SCALE` (SCALE = 1.7) — around the Voronoi cell centroid. A reserved 6×3 cell `LABEL_HOLE` at the cluster center holds the Actor label. Exclusion checks during sunflower placement: (a) inside label hole → skip; (b) outside Voronoi cell → skip; (c) within `INTER_CLUSTER_BUFFER = 2 × DOT_GRID` of a dot from a different cluster → skip; (d) within DOT_GRID × 0.9 of an already-placed dot in this cluster → skip. Umbrella items go first (inner spiral arcs) so the convex-hull outline reads as a tight connected subset. **Centered Actor label**: PillboxLabel HTML overlay moved from `top = y - ACTOR_SQUARE/2 - ACTOR_LABEL_OFFSET` to `top: y; transform: translate(-50%, -50%)`. The Three.js Actor-squares InstancedMesh is kept (scene-init / dispose symmetry) but rendered at count=0 — the centered HTML pill replaces it visually. `cluster.squareWorld` now coincides with `cluster.center` so existing PillboxLabel + hover-fade lookups continue to work. **Umbrella outline path** (replacing the L-shape rectangular border): convex hull of umbrella dots via Andrew's monotone-chain algorithm, offset outward by 1 DOT_GRID for visual breathing room. Fallbacks: bounding circle (1 umbrella dot), stadium (2 dots). Stroked in `var(--accent-amber)` 1.5px + 8% amber fill — reverts the Phase 16.1.3 grey treatment per the brief. The old `CLUSTER_PAD`-based L-shape construction is removed. **Loading animation v2** (`src/v2/directoryLoadAnimation.js` extended): new `umbrellaOutlines` parameter accepts `[{ party, distFromAnchor }]`; the wave fades each outline 0 → 1 in sync with its cluster label (start time = `distFromAnchor / waveSpeed + labelDelayMs/1000`). `skip()` snaps dot + label + outline opacities to 1. **Camera-init NaN guard** + Phase 16.1.4 `shouldMountScene` + Phase 16.1.5 `frustumCulled = false` / unbounded `boundingSphere` all preserved. **d3-delaunay added as a dependency** (`^6.0.4`). **Lloyd's convergence caveat**: at the current 12-actor seed, max displacement after 10 iterations is ~194 wu (above the 12-wu convergence threshold); we accept those positions and surface a `console.warn` per the brief's "stop and surface, don't raise the cap unilaterally" rule. The visual is still acceptable — Phase 16.2.5 (seed expansion to ~3k dots) will exercise convergence on a denser data set. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§6 / §3.5 — Phase 16.2.2 (parent canvas node spreading):** `buildV22Canvas` (the layout function that assigns `x, y` to every parent-canvas node before V2Canvas consumes them) gained chain-row coalescing + an enlarged grantee-direction column gap. **Y-anchor**: each evaluation chain (one row per `chainOrigin`) is now y-anchored to its evaluator's `EA.granteeAssetId` Asset on canvas via a two-pass allocator. Pass 1 — the first chain at each anchor Asset claims that anchor's `y` exactly, so the visual association between chain row and grantee Asset is exact. Pass 2 — subsequent chains pick the nearest non-colliding `y` to their anchor via symmetric outward search (`±ROW_STEP`, `±2·ROW_STEP`, …). Chain successors (re-runs) share the y of their chain origin so a chain reads as a single horizontal row, not a column of staggered rows. Fallback to the legacy `symmetricRowY(i) + COL_Y_OFFSET` pattern when the anchor Asset isn't on canvas (proof-only-pulled paths where the grantee Asset isn't visible to the active actor). **X-gap**: `COL_PULLED_CLAIM` bumped 2100 → 2400 (+ `COL_PULLED_ASSET` 2500 → 2800, `COL_PUBLIC` 2900 → 3200, `COL_OWN_POE` 2100 → 2400). On Bob/Carol/Dave's grantee-direction views this gives a 400-unit gap from the rightmost chain node (owned PoE at `COL_OWN_EVAL_eff + chainLength·ER_COL_SPACING` = 2000 for non-supersession chains) to the pulled Claim at 2400 — comfortably above the brief's ≥240 minimum. **PoE column order on grantor-direction views**: `proofOfEvalPulledPoEs` placement moved from `COL_OWN_EVAL_eff - ER_COL_SPACING` (between Claim and ER — 100-unit gap to Claim, tight visual overlap) to `COL_OWN_EVAL_eff + ER_COL_SPACING` (right of ER, matching the brief's `Claim → ER → PoE → Asset` order). **Proof-only-pulled PoE y** now inherits from the wrapped ER's `y` when on canvas so PoE + ER share a chain row on Dave-style views; falls back to the legacy `sourceClaim.y + 200` pattern when the wrapped ER isn't visible. **Architectural deviation surfaced**: the seed has both Bob's evaluations (PRM + VReg) carrying `granteeAssetId: bAvionics` — so the brief's "bAvionics for PRM eval, bThermal for VReg eval" example doesn't hold under the actual seed. Strictly anchoring each chain to its respective grantee Asset is impossible when two distinct chains share an anchor; stacking is the deterministic fallback. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.1 (hotfix):** `aVregTestReport` ownership DA restored. Phase 15.4 promoted the Asset to an unattached "floating" entry that Alice attaches to her VReg Claim during the Re-Run demo prereq, but omitted the Actor→Asset ownership DA. Without it the Asset rendered floating on Alice's parent canvas with no Full Disclosure edge to the MicroCo Actor card. One-line fix: add `aVregTestReport` to the `aliceOwnAssets` array in `buildV22SharedArtifacts`.
