New internal DA `da-own-asset-vreg-test-report` (grantor + grantee both MicroCo, subject {kind:'asset', id: aVregTestReport.id}, type: 'full'). Disclosure-agreements grows by exactly 1. Re-Run demo prereq flow (Alice amends VReg Claim to add `aVregTestReport` later) unchanged — ownership and Claim-reference are orthogonal. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2.3 (galactic view + loading animation):** `DirectoryLayer.jsx` rewritten to use a bounded design surface + radial fan-out. **Bounded canvas**: 17280×11170 world units, matching 16" MacBook Pro logical resolution at 10% zoom (`INITIAL_ZOOM = 0.1`). At default zoom the whole canvas fits exactly in an MBP viewport so the user sees the full Directory on first load — "galactic view first impression". `MIN_ZOOM` lowered 0.5 → 0.1; `MAX_ZOOM` unchanged at 4.0. Zoom-percentage display now maps directly: `Math.round(zoom * 100)` ("10%", "100%", "400%"). **Anchor**: active Actor's own cluster anchors at `(8640, 8936)` — canvas-bottom-center, 20% up from the bottom edge. Carol (anonymous, no own cluster) still uses the same anchor as the polar origin for non-own placement. **Radial polar fan-out (replaces the deterministic-bbox-overlap loop)**: each non-active cluster picks `(θ, r)` via Poisson disc sampling — θ ∈ [-75°, +75°] from straight up (upper 150° arc), r ∈ [2000, 8377.5] (~75% of canvas height). Cartesian projection: `cx = anchor.x + r·sin(θ); cy = anchor.y - r·cos(θ)`. Deterministic seed via `hashString(ownerParty) + attempt`. Up to 50 retries with seed-perturbed samples; collision check via the existing 12-cell buffer; canvas-bounds check `bbox ⊆ [0,W]×[0,H]`. Sort input by descending Claim count so jumbo clusters get first pick. Fallback after 50 retries logs a `console.warn` and keeps the last candidate. **Pan-bounds recompute per zoom**: `clampPan` now derives `[minX,maxX] × [minY,maxY]` from `(CANVAS_WIDTH/HEIGHT, viewport, zoom)` on every call. At zoom=0.1 the bounds collapse to a single point (full canvas already fits); at higher zoom the bounds open up to allow canvas traversal without revealing void beyond the canvas. **FIT** recalculates zoom from `min(viewport.w / CANVAS_WIDTH, viewport.h / CANVAS_HEIGHT)` and centers camera on `(canvas-center-x, canvas-center-y)`. **Loading animation** (new helper `src/v2/directoryLoadAnimation.js`): on every Directory entry (initial mount + role switch), cluster dots fade in via a radial wave emanating from the active Actor anchor. Per-dot `t_start = d / waveSpeed` (waveSpeed = 3000 world units / sec); ramp opacity 0 → 1 over `dotFadeMs = 200ms`. Per-cluster labels fade in `labelDelayMs = 100ms` after the first dot in the cluster appears. Total duration ~3.2s for the 17280×11170 canvas. Dot opacity is realised via per-instance color multiplication (since `MeshBasicMaterial` doesn't support per-instance alpha cleanly, and the Directory background is opaque dark `--bg-deep`, scaling color → 0 makes the dot disappear into the background). Click on empty canvas during animation calls `skip()` on the returned handle and snaps everything to opacity 1.0; dot clicks open the Detail Panel normally and DO NOT skip; pan / zoom run independently of the wave. Phase 16.1.5's `frustumCulled = false` + unbounded `boundingSphere` settings on every `InstancedMesh` left intact; Phase 16.1.4's `shouldMountScene` lifecycle pattern left intact. **#196 (force-directed Directory layout) closed** — superseded by the polar Poisson disc fan-out. **Camera-init guard** added: `updateCamera` bails when `container.clientWidth ≤ 0 || container.clientHeight ≤ 0` (briefly true during initial mount), preventing a zero-width frustum from producing NaN projection-matrix entries that propagate through `worldToScreen` and break HTML overlay positioning. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.2 (seed expansion):** Round 17 opens. The Directory now hosts 12 new mock supplier Actors representing the defense-electronics supply chain under Bob's Sentinel-4 satellite program: NovaFab (25 Claims), ElectroGrid (24), Precision Components (18), AvionicSys (17), Substrate Dynamics (16), Helix RF (11), Optech Sensors (10), SolarVantage (10), ThermaCore (9), CompoStruct (9), Photonix (4), Cryotek (4). Total: 157 new Claims, 157 stub Assets (no `localPath` — Detail Panel "Document preview not available" fallback covers them), and 628 internal + public Disclosure Agreements (1 own-asset + 1 own-claim + 1 claim-ref-asset + 1 public per Claim). Every new Actor is non-switchable (`user: null`, `credits: 0`) and discloses exclusively to the Radiant Network — no umbrella DAs to any of the four primary actors. The four existing actor seeds (Bob/GovCo, Alice/MicroCo, Carol/AuditCo, Dave/ChipCo) are frozen: none of their Claims, Assets, DAs, EAs, RFPs, PoEs, Eval Results, or Parse Results were modified. New exported helper `seedMockSupplierActor({ id, party, vertical, claimSpecs, baseDate })` in `v2_2Data.js` produces an Actor + Claims + 1 stub Asset per Claim + ownership DAs + public DAs in one call; a companion `pickDirectoryType(i, total)` interleaver assigns disclosure types per Claim to target ~60% full / 25% selective / 15% proofonly so every cluster paints a visible indigo + amber + green mix (with a forced fixed pattern for n ≤ 4 to keep Photonix and Cryotek non-monotone). Placement-scaling patch in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`: the initial cluster x-spread band scales with cluster count `N` (`xSpread = Math.max(800, 600 × (N − 1))`) instead of the previous hardcoded 800-px band. With 14 non-own clusters on Bob's view the initial band is ~7800 px — comfortably enough that the 30-attempt up-zigzag retry no longer needs to compensate. Force-directed layout (#196) remains deferred. Phase 16.1.5's defensive `frustumCulled = false` + unbounded `boundingSphere` settings on every `InstancedMesh` left intact; Phase 16.1.4's `shouldMountScene` lifecycle pattern left intact. Footer rolls forward to v0.16.2.0.
- **§8.5 — Phase 16.1.5 (hotfix):** Two QA issues surfaced in 16.1.4, both downstream of the lifecycle change: (1) dots disappeared at zoom > ~60%; (2) dot clicks no longer opened the Detail Panel. Shared root cause — `THREE.InstancedMesh.boundingSphere` is auto-computed from underlying geometry vertices, which sit at world origin because instance positions live in per-instance matrices, not the geometry. The first computed sphere ended up tiny (radius = `DOT_RADIUS = 3`, centred at origin) and was cached on the mesh. The rendering frustum check (`Frustum.intersectsObject`) and the raycast pre-filter (`InstancedMesh.raycast` → `raycaster.ray.intersectsSphere(_sphere)`) both consult this cached sphere — the tiny origin-sphere fell outside the camera frustum at high zoom and outside click rays at any zoom, so the entire mesh was rejected before per-instance work could run. Phase 16.1.4 stabilized the scene lifecycle but didn't touch the bounding-sphere caching, so both symptoms surfaced once the lifecycle stopped masking them. Fix: on each InstancedMesh (dots + Actor squares + RFP), set `frustumCulled = false` and override `boundingSphere = new THREE.Sphere(origin, Infinity)`. The former opts out of the renderer frustum check entirely; the latter forces the raycast pre-filter to always pass so per-instance raycast runs correctly. Note: the brief's alternate Item 2 diagnosis ("closure staleness in event listeners attached in the scene-init effect") did not match the actual code — mouse handlers in DirectoryLayer.jsx are React JSX event props bound via `useCallback` with full deps, not `addEventListener` calls inside a long-lived effect. No handler refactor was required; the bounding-sphere fix addressed the click symptom via the shared raycast pre-filter. Footer rolls forward to v0.16.1.5.
- **§8.5 — Phase 16.1.4 (hotfix):** Single-issue hotfix for the regression Phase 16.1.3 introduced — dots no longer appeared on the Directory layer because the scene-init `useEffect` declared `phase` in its dependency array. Each internal phase transition (`opening → in`, `in → out`) fired the effect's cleanup, which disposed the populated `InstancedMesh` + renderer. The body re-ran and created a fresh empty mesh (`count = 0`). The downstream `useLayoutEffect` that repopulates dots had deps `[threeReady, layout]` — neither registered a change because React batched the `setThreeReady(false)` (in cleanup) with the `setThreeReady(true)` (in the new body) into a single update whose net value was unchanged. Result: empty mesh + no repopulation = blank canvas. Fix: derive a stable `shouldMountScene = phase !== 'closed'` boolean and use it as the dep instead of `phase`. The scene-init effect now runs setup only on the `closed → non-closed` boundary and cleanup only on `non-closed → closed`. Internal phase transitions no longer tear down and rebuild the scene. Verified across hard-reload + first open, 10 open/close cycles, and 5 rapid-succession toggles in 3 seconds. Footer rolls forward to v0.16.1.4.
- **§8.5 — Phase 16.1.3 (parent-parity fixes + color scheme):** Nine items. (1) **Item 1 — first-transition dot lifecycle**: InstancedMesh population moved from `useEffect` to `useLayoutEffect` so the mesh is populated before browser paint. Transition-end listener on the wipe container forces an explicit render after the `clip-path` animation completes — guarantees dots are painted whether the animate-loop tick happens before or after the wipe. InstancedMesh pre-allocated with generous capacity (MAX_DOTS = 10000, MAX_SQUARES = 64, MAX_RFPS = 256); `mesh.count` updated per layout without recreating the mesh. (2) **Item 2 — Actor square as hollow ShapeGeometry**: replaced `THREE.LineSegments` with `THREE.Mesh` using a `THREE.Shape` (outer 6×6 square + inner 4×4 hole). Renders as a single `THREE.InstancedMesh` so all Actor squares share one draw call. Border thickness scales naturally with camera zoom because the geometry IS the border. (3) **Item 3 — squareCell reservation**: cluster layout computes `squareCell = { row, col }` BEFORE placing umbrella/public/RFP cells; placement scans skip the squareCell. Eliminates dot-on-square overlap visible in earlier QA screenshots. (4) **Item 4 — zoom controls position**: bumped to `top: 73` (was 12) to clear the 61px chrome bar; matches parent layer's apparent zoom-controls position. (5) **Item 5 — Detail Panel height**: `bottom: 28` (was 0) so the panel ends above the v0.16.x app footer. (6) **Item 6 — click pans to center**: `handleMouseUp` calls `animatedPanToWithZoom(targetX + panelOffsetWorld, targetY, currentZoom, 500)` where `panelOffsetWorld = (PANEL_W/2) / zoom`. Mirrors V2Canvas's pattern with the same panel-aware offset and the same cubic-ease curve (`t<0.5 ? 4*t³ : 1 - pow(-2*t+2, 3)/2`). (7) **Item 7 — Request EA modal close**: removed `setV22DirectorySelectedClaim(null)` + `setV22DirectoryOpen(false)` from the `onRequestEvaluationAgreement` callback in V2App.jsx. The modal opens on top of Directory + Detail Panel; closing/cancelling the modal leaves Directory + Detail Panel intact. (8) **Item 8 — disclosure-type-based dot colors**: replaces the visibility-scope-based color scheme. Full → indigo, selective → amber, proof-only → green. The DirectoryLayer resolves each Claim's effective disclosure type via inline DA walking (umbrella DA takes precedence over public DA when both exist). Seed types diversified across MicroCo public DAs + ChipCo public DAs + ChipCo→Bob umbrella DAs to produce visible color variety per cluster. L-shape boundary around the umbrella subset changed from amber to neutral grey (`color-mix(in srgb, var(--text-secondary) 60%, transparent)` stroke + 6% fill). (9) **Item 9 — RFP hollow circle**: replaces filled green dot with `THREE.Mesh` using `ShapeGeometry` (outer circle + inner circular hole). Color: `var(--accent-cyan)` (#22d3ee in dark theme; reads cleanly against the dark canvas). Border thickness scales with camera zoom. Bob's seeded RFP green dot becomes this new cyan hollow circle. Footer rolls forward to v0.16.1.3.
- **§8.5 — Phase 16.1.2 (spatial model rewrite + bug fixes):** Eight items. (1) Corner card removed from Directory entirely; the user's own representation becomes a regular cluster. (2) User's own cluster (Actor square + own Claims + own RFPs) anchored at canvas-horizontal-center + bottom-third vertically on initial load. Anonymous actors (Carol/AuditCo, no own Claims OR RFPs) render no own cluster — bottom-third is just a virtual layout anchor for fan-out. New `isUserVisible` flag on `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` return shape. (3) Actor squares migrated from HTML overlays to Three.js `LineSegments` (4 line segments per square, indigo color, 6×6 world units). Squares now scale with camera zoom identically to dots. Pillbox labels remain HTML overlays projected via `worldToScreen` (text stays readable at all zooms). (4) All edges dropped from Directory. The umbrella DA edge SVG path removed; `umbrellaEdges` field on the view-builder output is now an empty array (retained for backwards compatibility). Amber L-shape boundary around umbrella subset remains as a cluster-internal SVG decoration. (5) 12-cell minimum buffer between cluster bounding boxes (user's own + each other's). Naive deterministic placement: own cluster at (0, +bottom-third world units); others fanned above with hashed-party-name jitter; bbox overlap check + nudge-upward-and-sideways retry up to 30 attempts. Force-directed layout deferred (backlog #196). (6) **First-transition dot lifecycle stabilized**. The Phase 16.1.0 InstancedMesh was destroyed and recreated on every layout change, leaving a window where the animate loop ticked with no mesh attached. Phase 16.1.2 keeps the mesh alive across data changes — created once at desiredCount + 64 capacity, subsequent updates via `setMatrixAt` + `setColorAt` + `mesh.count = desiredCount`. Unused instances collapse to `makeScale(0,0,0)`. Mesh grows (recreates) only when desiredCount exceeds capacity. Stable across 5+ open/close cycles. (7) Parent-layer node + edge hover + selected state cleared on Directory transition (verified comprehensive after Phase 16.1.1 + this phase's audit). (8) Zoom controls migrated to match parent-layer pattern: top-right vertical stack of `+/-/FIT` buttons + percentage display. `FIT` computes the bounding box across all visible clusters and adjusts camera position + zoom to fit. Old bottom-right `zoom 1.50x` indicator removed.
- **§8.5 — Phase 16.1.1 (hotfix):** Eleven QA fixes on top of Phase 16.1.0. (1) **Item 1** — wheel listener `useEffect` dep array gains `phase` so it re-binds when the layer mounts; previously `containerRef.current` was null at first-effect-run and the listener was never attached. (2) **Item 2** — zoom indicator `bottom: 16 → 56` clears the app footer. (3) **Item 3** — first-transition dot rendering race fixed via a synchronous `renderer.render(scene, camera)` call inside the dots `useEffect` after the InstancedMesh attaches; dots appear on the very first frame instead of waiting for the next animate tick. (4) **Item 4** — removed the `+DOT_GRID/2` offset from dot world-coord computation; cluster dot centers now sit ON background `THREE.Points` grid intersections, not between them. (5) **Item 5** — new `clearHoverState` imperative method on V2Canvas via `useImperativeHandle` clears `hoveredEdge`, `edgeTooltipPos`, `hoveredEdgeSdaType`; V2App invokes it from both Directory entry points so parent-layer edge tooltips don't persist behind the wipe. (6) **Item 6** — amber L-shape boundary restored as an SVG `<path>` overlay projected from world coords on every camera change. Boundary computed from umbrella cell positions with `CLUSTER_PAD` (5 world units) breathing room; rendered with stroke `var(--accent-amber)` 1.5px + fill `color-mix(var(--accent-amber) 8%, transparent)`. (7) **Item 7** — `ClaimTooltipCard` 4th row (`UMBRELLA · date` / `PUBLIC · date`) removed; tooltip is now badge + name + owner only. (8) **Item 8** — 1-cell buffer in all four directions around the umbrella subset. New `layoutClusterCells` helper packs umbrella starting at row 1 (top buffer), computes the buffer set as orthogonal-adjacent cells, then fills public via row-major scan skipping both umbrella and buffer cells. ChipCo's Bob view: row 0 = top buffer (empty), row 1 = 6 amber, row 2 = 1 amber + buffer at col 1 + 4 indigo at cols 2-5, row 3 = 3 indigo at cols 1-3. (10) **Item 10** — `CORNER_CARD_BOTTOM` 32 → 60 (= left margin 32 + footer height ~28); card-to-footer spacing now matches card-to-viewport-left. (11) **Item 11** — own RFP green dot box-shadow halo removed; the dot was already 6×6 but the 5px glow visually inflated it. (12) **Item 12** — cluster vertically centered on Actor square. `anchorY = snapGrid(center.y - ((maxRow + minRow) / 2) * ROW_GAP)`. With ChipCo's 4-row cluster (row 0 top buffer + rows 1-3 dots), the Actor square sits cleanly in the inter-row gap between rows 1 and 2. (Item 0 / footer convention) Footer rolls forward to v0.16.1.1 per the convention codified in 16.1.0.
- **§8.5 — Phase 16.1.0 (Three.js migration):** Directory Layer's rendering pipeline migrated from HTML/CSS dots + CSS-tiled background to Three.js scene + `InstancedMesh` dots + `THREE.Points` grid + scroll-zoom + drag-pan. Mirrors V2Canvas's hybrid architecture: Three.js for scene/grid/dots/camera; HTML for tooltip card, label pillboxes, Actor squares, umbrella SVG edges (all positioned via `worldToScreen` projection). Hover/click on dots routes through `THREE.Raycaster` against the dot `InstancedMesh`; per-instance hover effects via `setColorAt` (whiten the hovered dot, lerp cluster siblings 15% toward white via a precomputed `clusterByDotIndex` lookup). World coords are integer multiples of `DOT_GRID = 12` so dots and the background grid `Points` always coincide (rolls the strict-alignment work from 16.0.3 into the world-coord computation rather than CSS positioning). Three.js scope kept narrow — only dots and the grid; edges (curved Bezier `<path>`), labels (pillbox HTML), tooltips (HTML card with viewport-edge flip), and Actor squares (HTML) all stay HTML/SVG to preserve the Phase 16.0.3 visual treatment without re-implementing in Three.js. Pan/zoom mirror V2Canvas's mouse handlers — drag (no modifier) translates the camera; wheel zooms around cursor. `MIN_ZOOM` = 0.5, `MAX_ZOOM` = 4.0, `INITIAL_ZOOM` = 1.5 (narrower than parent layer's 0.20–1.50 range since clusters are smaller than parent-layer node graphs). Bob's own RFP green dot remains an HTML overlay anchored to the corner card (matches the "active-actor-cluster-around-corner-card" pattern); other-actor RFPs ride along in the InstancedMesh as green dots. Drag-vs-click distinction via a 4-pixel movement threshold (mirrors V2Canvas's `wasDragRef` pattern). Bug fix: one-grid-cell phantom buffer between amber (umbrella) and indigo (public) dots within a cluster — preserved from 16.0.3 but now enforced in world-coord computation rather than CSS phantom-slot positioning. **Footer version convention** also corrected this phase: previous 16.0.x backtrack-hotfix cycle held the footer at v0.15.9. Going forward, forward-progress phases roll the footer; backtrack-hotfix versions (inserted between existing entries) leave the footer unchanged. Footer reads v0.16.1.0.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.0.3:** Directory Layer polish + 16.0.1 leftover fix. Six items, all in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` + a footer z-index bump in `V2App.jsx`. (1) **Item 0**: 16.0.1's "RADIANT NETWORK" header pillbox `top` value bumped from 24 to 80 so it clears the top chrome bar (chrome z-300, layer z-150 — pillbox at top:24 rendered behind chrome). (2) **Item 1**: umbrella DA edge from Bob's corner card to ChipCo's Actor square now renders as an SVG `<path>` cubic Bezier curve (`M start C cp1 cp2 end`) with control points at horizontal midpoints of the start/end Y values, producing a smooth horizontal-exit / horizontal-entry S-curve. Replaces the prior straight `<line>` element. Visual character mirrors parent-layer full-disclosure edges. (3) **Item 2**: one-cell phantom-slot gap inserted between umbrella (amber) and public (indigo) dots within a cluster. New `placeClusterRowMajor` logic places umbrella in pure row-major order, then evaluates whether the public subset can flow on the same row at `lastUmbrella.col + 2` (with a gap at col+1) or must drop to the next row (where the row break itself separates subsets, no extra gap needed). For ChipCo's 7-amber + 7-public Bob view: row 0 = 6 amber, row 1 = 1 amber + gap + 4 indigo, row 2 = 3 indigo (was 6+5+3 with no gap before; now 6+(1+gap+4)+3 = same total). The L-shape amber border auto-wraps the same cells (umbrella positions unchanged). (4) **Item 3**: ClaimTooltipCard rewritten to mirror parent-layer `AssetNodeDot` tooltip pattern — anchored at dot's right-center via `transform: translateY(-50%)`, with viewport-edge flip via `wouldClipRight` check (mirrors AssetNode.jsx:1144 logic) that swaps anchor to dot's left-center via `translate(-100%, -50%)`. CLAIM badge sits tight to the top of the card (padding-top reduced from 12 to 10). (5) **Item 4**: app footer (`Connected to AWS S3 · v0.15.9 · Changelog`) raised to `position: relative; zIndex: 300` so it stays visible when the Directory layer (z-150) is open. (6) **Item 5**: explicit `DOT_GRID = 12` constant + `snapGrid(v) = Math.round(v / DOT_GRID) * DOT_GRID` helper. All cluster centers, cluster grid anchors, own-cluster anchor, own RFP positions, and free-standing-RFP slots snap to the dot grid. Cluster `placeClusterRowMajor` derives every dot position from the snapped anchor + integer column/row offsets, so dots inherit alignment automatically. The previous `MATRIX_GRID = 16` constant retained for the background dot pattern only — Phase 16.1 may unify these.
- **§8.2 — Phase 16.0.1:** Directory Layer layout polish. Eight changes on top of 16.0's visual model: (1) header banner replaced with a single "Radiant Network" pillbox at top-center; (2) "N clusters · M Claims" subtitle removed; (3) in-canvas "Launch AI Shopper" button removed (chrome-bar entry remains); (4) Claim + RFP dot size 8×8 → 6×6; (5) cluster layout switched from freeform polar fill to row-major grid (max 6 dots per row, 12px cell stride, one-dot-width gaps) — umbrella claims fill first so the amber subset occupies the leading cells in row-major order; ChipCo's 7-amber + 7-public Bob view yields 6+6+2 rows with an L-shaped amber border traced via SVG `<path>` (handles N-row L-shapes, not just two); (6) Actor squares reduced to 6×6 hollow indigo borders (dot-sized) with party labels lifted to pillbox-styled (`var(--bg-card) 92%, var(--text-dim)`) labels above each square; pillboxes fade to `opacity: 0.25` when an underlying Claim dot is hovered (intersection check against pillbox bbox), with the dot picking up a `0 0 8px rgba(255,255,255,0.6)` halo so it reads on top; (7) MicroCo + ChipCo cluster centers re-anchored to vertical center of the canvas, distributed horizontally (x≈35% / 65% for 2 clusters, even split for 3+); (8) Bob's RFP green dot anchored directly above his corner card (`cardCenterX, cardTopY - 24`), horizontally centered on the card. Own-cluster placement (Alice/Dave) keeps adjacent-to-corner-card anchoring but applies the same row-major grid + 6-wide max.
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.6 (#172 closing):** Re-Run auto-fill from new Asset evidence. Anchor entries gain optional `discoveredValue: string`; the V22RunEvaluationModal carry-forward path (`buildRowsForRs` → row map step) wraps each prior row in an `autoFillRow` closure that auto-populates MISSING rows whose anchors point to newly-in-scope Assets — status flips to `satisfactory`, value flips to the matching anchor's `discoveredValue`. Newly-in-scope = an Asset present in current `evidenceAssets` but NOT in prior `evidenceUsed`. Scope is intentionally narrow (only MISSING rows, only on newly-in-scope Assets, only when `discoveredValue` is non-empty) so existing SAT/UNSAT/N/A rows are never silently overwritten. Fresh evaluations (no `priorActiveResult`) bypass the transformation entirely. The prior Eval Result artifact is NOT mutated — auto-fill operates on a derived row array used by the modal's working state. Demo arc: prior eval shows gaps → Alice amends with new evidence → Bob's re-run auto-populates the missing rows → save → 7/7 SAT → create PoE. **#172 closed.**
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.5:** VReg Re-Run demo simplified into a coherent narrative arc — prior eval shows 5 SAT + 2 MISSING criteria → Alice amends with Test Report → Bob's re-run "discovers" values for the missing rows in the new evidence → save → 7/7 SAT → create PoE. The Phase 13.2 supersession chain on VReg (V0/V1/V_main) collapsed to a single standalone Eval Result; chain Eval Results + their proof/own-eval auto-disclosure DAs removed. Two new requirements added to MIL-PRF-55681 v1: req-006 (Single Event Latch-up immunity) + req-007 (Burn-in qualification). req-004 value updated from "TID > 100 krad(Si)" to "TID ~ 80 krad(Si)" matching the Datasheet PDF for visual coherence (the prototype RS doesn't encode threshold logic so the SAT label is independent of the value content). Test Report PDF rewritten as a focused 1-page supplementary document containing only req-006 + req-007 sections (was a redundant 2-page mirror of the Datasheet's req-001-005). Demo trick (anchors reference an Asset outside `evidenceUsed`) narrowed to req-006/007 only — the 5 SAT rows reference Datasheet exclusively. Carol's EMI Eval Result is now the only standalone unwrapped Eval Result remaining; the chain-DA pattern from Phase 13.2 is no longer exercised by the current seed, though the rendering code path stays available for future scenarios.
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.4:** Re-Run demo seed corrected. Phase 15.3 over-eagerly attached the Test Report Asset to `cVreg.referencedAssetIds` and extended `daAliceToBobVreg.scope` at initial seed time — that left VReg's Expand modal showing `Asset 1 of 2` from first boot, duplicating PRM's role as the canonical multi-Asset Expand demo. Phase 15.4 reverts both to single-Asset initial seed (Datasheet only). The Test Report Asset stays defined and stays in `assets`, but Alice-owned-and-unattached — surfacing in her amend Asset picker as the named candidate for the Re-Run prereq. Compliance Notes Asset (Phase 15.3 addition) retired entirely; PDF deleted; generator spec entry removed (the `paragraphs[]` page-spec affordance preserved on the generator). **Demo trick (prototype convention):** `erBobVreg.evidenceUsed` reverts to `[aVregDatasheet.id]` (what was actually evaluated at chain-head time) but its `results[].evidenceAnchors[]` arrays deliberately retain references to both Datasheet AND Test Report. When Alice attaches the Test Report during the amend prereq, Bob's subsequent Re-Run accordion renders annotations against those pre-stamped anchors. In production, anchors shouldn't reference Assets outside `evidenceUsed` — that's a data-integrity invariant. For prototype demo purposes the inconsistency is accepted because it enables the amend-then-rerun-with-annotations narrative without contriving a prior eval that already used the Test Report. Inline comment block in `v2_2Data.js` flags the trick.
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.3:** Re-Run demo seed enhancement. VReg Claim now seeded with two pre-existing Assets — `aVregDatasheet` + new `aVregTestReport` (id `asset-vreg-test-report`, generated PDF `microco-vreg-test-report.pdf`, anchored on all 5 MIL-PRF-55681 v1 requirements). Chain-head `erBobVreg` Eval Result extended to multi-Asset anchors: every `results[]` row carries `evidenceAnchors: [{ sourceAssetId: aVregDatasheet.id, ... }, { sourceAssetId: aVregTestReport.id, ... }]` — Asset switcher in the Expand modal flips between annotated PDFs, and the Re-Run flow's accordion inherits multi-Asset annotations via `priorActiveResult.results`. Test report PDF values authored at parity with seed (`5.0V ±0.4% under load`, `< 1.5W at rated current`, etc. — identical strings) so PDF text and seed values stay in sync. Predecessor `erBobVregV0` / `erBobVregV1` retain single-Asset anchors (they predate the test report; preserves chain causality). New `aVregComplianceNotes` Asset (id `asset-vreg-compliance-notes`, generated PDF `microco-vreg-compliance-notes.pdf` — paragraph-only, no anchors) pre-seeded as Alice's owned-but-unattached Asset for the deterministic Re-Run amend prerequisite. PDF generator script extended with optional `paragraphs[]` page-spec field for documentation Assets (no row-based anchor capture). EVIDENCE → ASSETS rename in `V22RunEvaluationModal` Δ delta info box (the missed surface from the 15.1.2 sweep). VReg is now the canonical multi-Asset Re-Run demo path; PRM remains the canonical multi-Asset Expand modal demo path. Walkthrough doc updated — Section 2 rewritten as multi-Asset, Section 5a names the Compliance Notes Asset with rationale, Section 5b describes the three-Asset accordion.
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.2 (#172 part 3 close):** Download icon button onClick + Tooltip wired. The Phase 15.1.2 button shipped disabled because the spec premise (a pre-existing Phase 13.4 Download handler ready to bind) was wrong — the original `<DownloadButton>` placeholder has always been disabled with `title="Export coming soon."`. Phase 15.2 introduces the first working Download in the eval-output / poe surfaces: `<DownloadIconButton>` accepts an `onClick` prop and renders via the shared `<Tooltip>` primitive (was native `title=""`); `EvalResultOutputBody` wires `handleDownloadJson` (Blob-from-`getEvalResultJsonRecord` → transient `<a download="eval-result-<id>.json">` click → revokeObjectURL on next tick). PoE schema inherits since it renders `<EvalResultOutputBody>`. Demo walkthrough guide expanded at `docs/PHASE-15-DEMO-SCENARIOS.md` covering all four scenarios with the Re-Run prerequisite (Alice amends VReg Claim before Bob's re-run, gated by `hasNewAssetsForRerun` from Phase 13.3) documented explicitly. Five unreferenced legacy `/public/*.pdf` files removed (connectorassembly, pcbsubstrate, powerregulationmodule, thermalinterfacepad, voltageregulator); four still-referenced legacy PDFs (emishielding, prm-3a-ic-datasheet, prm-3a-ic-qualification-report, voltage-reference-ic-datasheet) retained — surfaced as a Phase 15.3 candidate to migrate to MicroCo seed-pdfs/ or trim from seed.
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.1.2:** Eval Result + PoE Expand modal Output tabs consolidated. Timestamp + party metadata moved to the canonical modal header (above the tab bar) — Eval Result gains "Evaluated: ... · Evaluator: ..." inline; PoE gains "Created: ... · Owner: ...". Phase 15.1.1's thin header band inside the Output body removed entirely. Download button promoted into the right-panel "EVALUATION RESULTS" title bar as a 24×24 icon button at the right edge (download glyph + tooltip "Download Evaluation Results JSON"); the healthbar block now hosts aggregate + minibar only. File info repositioned: left panel scroll content reads `[ASSET title bar]` → `[file viewer]` → `[combined 6-row metadata block: Filename, Size, MIME, Hash, Owner, Registered]`; Owner + Registered moved up from a tertiary footer into the same metadata card. EVIDENCE badge renamed to ASSET in the eval-output / poe Output tab left-panel title bars (canonical noun across the prototype data model; EVIDENCE was a Phase 12.4 holdover). The Δ EVIDENCE diff label in V22RunEvaluationModal Re-Run review left as-is (different context). PoE Output tab cleanup: full-width PROOF OF EVALUATION info box removed (PoE name + Created + Owner already render in canonical modal header); "Final Evaluation" SectionHeading dropped (the wrapped Eval Result's own EVALUATION RESULTS title bar makes the context unambiguous); body becomes structurally identical to Eval Result Output tab body. AssetEvidencePanel refactored: new `AssetFileViewer` + `AssetFileMetadata` (with `variant: 'file-info' | 'combined' | 'owner-row'`) sub-component exports let consumers rearrange viewer + metadata. `AssetEvidenceViewer` default export composes them in legacy order so every existing call site is unaffected.
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.1.1:** Annotation labels simplified to `{rowOrdinal}` (per-RS, 1-indexed; was `{assetOrdinal}.{rowOrdinal}` in 15.1) — the row's number is stable across Asset switches, RS color coding distinguishes RS membership. Annotation shape: rounded rectangle 32×22 with `borderRadius: 6` (was 26px circle). Right-panel row indicators + Run Evaluation review-row indicators picked up the rounded-rectangle treatment at 28×20 to match the shape language at slightly tighter scale. Eval Result Expand modal Output tab layout reorganized: healthbar (SAT/UNSAT/MISSING minibar + aggregate count) and Download button moved out of the full-width header into the right panel's top section; the thin header band above the side-by-side row keeps only the Eval Result name + Evaluated date + Evaluator. Right panel gains a new "EVALUATION RESULTS" title bar mirroring the left panel's "EVIDENCE" strip (amber accent, same height + padding). Same layout reorganization applies to PoE Expand modal Section 1 automatically (it calls `<EvalResultOutputBody>`). Global `<TabHeaderActions>` Download button suppressed on the Output tab for `eval-output` and `poe` schemas — the right-panel Download is the canonical export trigger for those schemas; JSON tab keeps the global Download for all schemas. The `assetOrdinal` prop on `AnnotatedPdfViewer` preserved in the API but no longer renders in the label.
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.1 (#172 part 2):** Annotation visual redesigned — each anchor now renders a translucent highlight rectangle (RS color, 15% opacity) over the cited text + a numbered indicator (26px circle, 12px mono bold label) immediately to the LEFT of the rect (clamped to `left: 4` if naturally negative). Highlighted state: rect bumps to 30% opacity, indicator gains `0 0 0 3px <RS color>` outside its 2px white border, results-table row tints at 8% RS color. Side-by-side layout for Expand modal Output tab (eval-output + poe schemas): 60/40 grid (PDF-favored), sticky left, vertical-stack fallback below 900px viewport. Per-RS results tables gain a 38px indicator column on the left of each row; rows with empty `evidenceAnchors[]` get an empty slot. Bidirectional row↔dot interaction with auto-flip Asset switcher on cross-Asset clicks: row click → setHighlightedAnchorId + setCurrentAssetIndex (if cross-Asset) → AnnotatedPdfViewer's `key={displayAsset.id}` forces a fresh PDF mount → next-frame `scrollIntoView` lands the user at the highlighted dot. Dot click is the inverse direction. Same interaction wired to V22RunEvaluationModal Step 2/3 review surface (accordion's expanded Asset is the auto-flip target). New `src/v2/data/anchorIds.js` utility synthesizes stable IDs `{sourceAssetId}|{requirementsSetId}|{requirementId}|{page}|{x}|{y}` shared between dot + row consumers. Implementation note: row anchors must be enriched with `(requirementsSetId, requirementId, label, value)` from the row at synthesis time — the seed anchor only carries `(sourceAssetId, page, x, y, w, h)`, so without enrichment the synthesized ID would have empty `||` fields and not match the PDF-side ID.
- **§17.5 — Phase 15.0.1 (annotation rendering hotfix):** `makeEvaluationResult` factory in `src/v2/v2_2Data.js` silently dropped `evidenceAnchors` from each result row's `results.map((r) => ({ … }))` projection. The Phase 15.0 seed authoring populated `evidenceAnchors: [...]` correctly on every row, but the factory's explicit field-preserve list excluded it; result rows reaching the consumer showed zero anchors and the overlay layer rendered nothing. Factory now preserves `evidenceAnchors`, cloned per-anchor for safety. AnnotatedPdfViewer also gains fit-to-width — render scale derived from the host container's `clientWidth` at load time (capped 1.6× base scale) so PDFs no longer force horizontal scroll inside narrow modal columns. EvalResultOutputBody gains a minimal multi-Asset switcher (Previous/Next arrows + per-Asset render via `key={displayAsset.id}`) — Phase 15.1 will add auto-flip on dot click. New `docs/PHASE-15-DEMO-SCENARIOS.md` captures the four QA scenarios.
- **§Workflow lessons — Phase 15.0.1:** Runtime probes verify components, not integration. Phase 15.0 shipped with `<AnnotatedPdfViewer>` standalone-tested via dynamic import + mocked anchors — both PDFs rendered, both dots positioned correctly. But the actual user path (open Detail Panel → click Expand → Output tab) was never exercised end-to-end with seed data. The factory bug surfaced on the very first such walkthrough during 15.0.1 diagnosis. Going forward, any phase that touches a shared seed factory should add an explicit user-path walkthrough verification step before declaring complete: drive the actual UI flow that consumes the factory output, screenshot the expected outcome, confirm visually.
- **§17.X — Phase 15.0 (#172 part 1 of 3):** PDF.js integration. Three branded demo PDFs generated by `scripts/generate-seed-pdfs.mjs` (a pdf-lib-based deterministic script committed alongside the artifacts) land at `public/seed-pdfs/`. Eval Result `results[]` rows gain an `evidenceAnchors[]` array — `[{ sourceAssetId, page, x, y, w, h }]` per anchor, coordinates in PDF point space (origin bottom-left). New `<AnnotatedPdfViewer>` component (in `src/components/`) renders PDFs via pdfjs-dist and overlays numbered dots labelled `{assetOrdinal}.{rowOrdinal}` with per-Requirements-Set color coding (`getRsColor` helper in `src/v2/data/rsColors.js`). Opt-in via the new `usePdfJs` prop on AssetEvidencePanel — applies to the Run Evaluation modal Step 1 evidence panel, Eval Result expand modal Output tab, and PoE expand modal Output tab; iframe rendering preserved everywhere else. PDF.js worker bundled via Vite's `?url` import (`src/v2/components/pdfJsWorker.js`). pdfjs-dist v5 implementation note: render must use the new `canvas` parameter (passing the legacy `canvasContext` parameter on v5 paints the canvas but the returned promise never resolves — the render task hangs indefinitely). New "Evidence Annotation" architecture subsection covers the coordinate system, dot scheme, and a forward-looking note on non-PDF evidence: real-world Assets may be CSVs, TXTs, structured data, or images, and a future-general locator schema (e.g. `{ type: 'pdf-rect' | 'csv-cell' | 'txt-line-range', ... }`) will generalise from the Phase 15 PDF-only shape. Static rendering only in 15.0 — Phase 15.1 wires bidirectional row-click ↔ dot-click interaction; Phase 15.2 ships the walkthrough markdown + final polish.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.6.2 (Library + action-bar polish, backtrack from Phase 15.6):** Six tightly-scoped follow-ups on the Phase 14.6 / 14.6.1 badge work. (1) **Item 1** — `LibraryModal` tab strip badge count now mirrors `BadgesPanel`'s 14.6.1 own-only filter: `activeParty ? badgeTemplates.filter((t) => t.ownerParty === activeParty).length : badgeTemplates.length`. Bob/Alice/Carol/Dave see 2/1/1/0. Other tabs (Parsing Templates, Requirement Sets, Published Requirements) keep their existing logic — the badge-private rule applies to badge templates only. (2) **Item 2** — `BadgesPanel` toolbar div gains `minHeight: 50` so the row no longer collapses when the "+ Create New Badge" button hides during create / new-version mode (button has 6px+12px padding + 11px font; the bare count line is shorter). (3) **Item 3** — Toolbar button label "+ Create new badge" → "+ Create New Badge" (title-case "New" + "Badge"). (4) **Item 4** — Create-mode form `headerText` "Create Badge Template" → "Create New Badge"; new-version mode header (`New Version: ${sourceName}`) unchanged. (5) **Item 5** — Right-panel ViewDetails Referenced Requirements Sets list now displays the RS owner attribution per row in place of the technical RS id on line 2. Resolution: Published Standards carry `_publishedBy` (e.g. 'GovCo') and override; own RSes from `requirementSets` (no explicit owner field — they live per role) fall back to the threaded `activeParty`. (6) **Item 6** — `AssetNode` action bar Issue Badge entry-point icon swapped from the star glyph (`★`) to the canonical `<BadgeShieldIcon size={13} color="currentColor" />` so the entry point matches the Phase 14.4+ chip stack rendering. `BadgeShieldIcon` already accepts `color` as a CSS-color string and feeds `currentColor` through to the silhouette stroke, so passing `currentColor` makes the icon inherit from the action button's text color exactly the way Unicode glyphs do. Tooltip + onClick + gate logic unchanged. **Footer remains v0.15.9** (NOT rolled back); v0.14.6.2 inserted into Changelog modal in chronological order between v0.15.0 and v0.14.6.1.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.6.1 (hotfix sub-phase, backtrack from Phase 15.6):** Two bug fixes + two backlog rollups. (1) **Bug A** — Badge Library narrowed from network-wide visibility to own-only. `BadgesPanel` pre-filters `badgeTemplates` to `ownTemplates` at the parent level (memoized over `activeParty`); `TemplateList` consumes the pre-filtered list and drops the cross-actor sectioning. Toolbar count + empty-state message both reference the filtered count. The full `badgeTemplates` list stays the source for `selectedTemplate` resolution + `handleNewVersion`'s lineage walk so id-based lookups still work. (2) **Bug B** — `STEP_FAN` constant in `BadgeChipContainer.jsx` tuned 22 → 16 to compensate for the halo + SVG stroke geometry from Phase 14.5; the visible shield footprint exceeds `SHIELD_SIZE` so the prior `SHIELD_SIZE + 4` math overshot, producing a ~10px on-screen gap during fan-out. Empirically tuned to land the on-screen gap at the 4px target. `STEP_IDLE`, `SHIELD_SIZE`, `HEIGHT` unchanged. (3) **#184** — V2.1 holdover `evidence` subtype config entry purged from `CATEGORY_CONFIG` in `AssetNode.jsx`. The string never rendered (no current seed Asset uses subtype `evidence`); the `CATEGORY_CONFIG[node.category] || CATEGORY_CONFIG.product` lookup pattern provides graceful fallback for any straggler V2.1 data. Broader `SUBTYPE_CFG` audit deferred to the future #50 V2.1 cleanup sweep. The `V2Canvas` filter (`c.category === 'evidence'`) was retained — it returns empty arrays in V2.2 and is also future-#50 cleanup material. (4) **#183** — Investigation-only this phase. All four legacy-PDF Assets (`aEmiDatasheet`, `dPrmIcDatasheet`, `dPrmIcTestReport`, `dVrefDatasheet`) have heavy downstream chains (Claims, Eval Results, DAs, Parse Results); per the brief's "STOP and surface" rule for non-orphan Assets, the trim was deferred without code changes. Per-Asset decision (keep + accept unbranded PDF / cascade-trim / substitute branded PDF) deferred to Andrew. **Footer remains v0.15.9** (NOT rolled back); v0.14.6.1 inserted into Changelog modal in chronological order between v0.15.0 and v0.14.6.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.6 (badge polish trio, backtrack from Phase 15.6):** Three fixes to the Phase 14 Badge work that got deprioritized when Phase 15 took over. (1) **#187** — `BadgesPanel` ViewDetails + `V22BadgeTemplatePanel` Active Issuances rows complete the Phase 14.2 `targetPoeId` → `targetClaimId` migration. Rows now read `b.targetClaimId` and pull Claim label + ownerParty from a new `claimNameLookup` (was `poeNameLookup`). `BadgesPanel` + `LibraryModal` prop signatures swapped `proofsOfEvaluation` for `allClaims`; the V2App pass-through dropped the now-unused `proofsOfEvaluation` prop. (2) **#188** — Badge Template create + new-version forms render the globe icon on YOUR REQUIREMENTS SETS rows whose RS is also in the Published Standards pool. Built via a `publishedRsIdSet` membership Set in `EditorForm`; `renderRsRow` consults it for own-section rows in addition to the existing `fromSection === 'published'` path. (3) **#189** — RS-coverage gate at IssueBadgeModal's template picker. Each row computes `disabledReason` from `template.referencedRequirementsSetIds` minus `coveredRsIds` (Set unioned from active PoEs' wrapped Eval Result `requirementsSets[].id`); failing rows render greyed-out (opacity 0.45, not-allowed cursor, no hover state) with a Tooltip listing missing RS names. SUGGESTED label suppressed on greyed rows. Two failure shapes: "no PoE at all" → generic "no Proof of Evaluation" tooltip; partial coverage → list missing RS names. `handleV22IssueBadge` re-checks the gate at the data layer and silently rejects with a console warning when bypassed; `mergeProvisionals(seed, v22Provisionals)` honored so PoEs created during the session count toward coverage. **Footer remains v0.15.9** (NOT rolled back); v0.14.6 inserted into Changelog modal in chronological order.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.5 (#176c):** Chip container visual tuning. Each shield carries a 2px halo stroked in the rectangle's exact background color (`color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent-indigo) 18%, var(--bg-card))`), producing the negative-space cut between adjacent overlapping shields that reads as the classic overlapping-tokens look. Shield size 20px → 18px (silhouette stays roughly the same since the halo reads as background). STEP_IDLE 15 → 12 for more pronounced overlap. STEP_FAN 24 → 22 (= 18 + 4px gap). Container height 26 → 24. Shields vertically centered (`top: (HEIGHT - SHIELD_SIZE) / 2`). Single-shield case (`total === 1`, no overflow) renders the rectangle as a 24×24 square with the lone shield horizontally + vertically centered via `inset: 0` flex; multi-shield right-anchored layout unchanged. `BadgeShieldIcon` gained an optional `strokeColor` prop that renders a wider halo path beneath the silhouette path; `overflow: visible` on the SVG so the halo doesn't clip at the viewBox edge.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.4 (#176b):** Badge chip container visual polish — circular wrappers around each shield and the pill wrapper around "+N" both removed; shields scaled from 16px to 20px to fill the freed space; "+N" renders as plain indigo monospaced text. Container styling (rounded rectangle background + border + shadow), fan-out animation timing, and tooltip behavior unchanged. STEP_IDLE rescaled 12 → 15 (75% of 20px) and STEP_FAN 20 → 24 (20px + 4px gap) to preserve the 25% idle overlap and 4px hovered gap relationships.
- **§17.X / §10 — Phase 14.3 (bug fix):** Issue Badge picker scope. Badge Templates are issuable only by their owner — the picker now filters strictly to the current actor's own templates. Other actors' templates remain visible in the Library tab but are not cross-actor issuable. The Phase 14.1 sectioned picker (My Badges + per-Actor) was a leak; templates owned by another actor should never have surfaced as issuance candidates. The picker collapses to a single un-sectioned scroll list of own templates with the existing latest-version SUGGESTED auto-promotion preserved.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.3 (#176a):** Badge chip container refactored from independent absolute-positioned chips + a separate "+N" pill into a single rounded-rectangle container (matches the NEW pill's visual treatment — 4px border-radius, indigo-tinted background, subtle shadow). Hover fan-out: when a card has 2+ badges, hovering the rectangle expands it leftward; the rightmost shield + "+N" stay anchored, previously-overlapped shields slide left to un-overlap with 4px spacing. Per-shield tooltip (badge name + version on line 1, issuer party on line 2) reveals on shield hover; "+N" tooltip reveals a list of buried badges (`Badge Name · Issuer Party` rows). Both tooltips auto-flip below their anchor when insufficient vertical space above. Animation: 180ms ease-out on container width + per-shield position. Click guard: chip rectangle clicks no longer trigger card-level interactions. Implementation lives in `src/v2/BadgeChipContainer.jsx` (extracted from `AssetNode.jsx`); per-node `_activeBadges` decoration in `V2App` enriched with `{ id, badgeName, badgeVersion, issuerParty }` shape so tooltips render without a second prop chain.
- **§17.X / §10 — Phase 14.2 (#169a):** Badge Issuance target shifts from PoE to Claim. Badges live on Claims; PoE-side display derives via aggregation (PoE → wrapped Eval Result → Claim → badges). Issuance gate becomes `issuerParty !== claim.ownerParty` — any user with disclosure access to a Claim or to a PoE wrapping an Eval Result of that Claim can issue a badge against the Claim, except the Claim owner. Enables third-party-issued badges on self-evaluations: an external reviewer (e.g. OSHA) can endorse Alice's Claim regardless of who created the proving PoE. Notification recipient routing now resolves via Claim owner (was PoE owner — same in most cases; OSHA scenario newly correct). JSON record carries `targetClaimId` (was `targetPoeId`); `getJsonRecordFor` dispatcher context shape is `{ claims, badgeTemplates }` (was `{ poes, badgeTemplates }`).
- **§17.X — Phase 14.2 (regression fix):** PoE creation now fires `v22-poe-created` notification on the Claim owner's inbox. Phase 13.1 wired the visibility (proof-of-eval DA grantee=Claim owner makes Bob's PoE appear on Alice's canvas — confirmed working via empirical probe in 14.2) but the inbox notification was missing. PoE visibility itself was never broken; only the inbox cue was.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.2 (#169b):** Standalone Badge Issuance Detail Panel removed. Badge Issuance row clicks open the expand modal directly (modal-over-Detail-Panel is the correct overlay pattern for non-canvas artifacts; Detail Panel-over-Detail Panel violated the prototype's UX conventions). Badge data lives in Claim + PoE Detail Panel sections + Actor "Badges Received" section.
- **§17.X / §10 — Phase 14.1 (#169 part 2 of 2):** Badge Issuance artifact introduced. References a specific Badge Template version (frozen, not lineage) + a target Proof of Evaluation. Recipient is NOT stored — derived at render time from `targetPoe.ownerParty`. Self-issuance is blocked: an Actor cannot issue a Badge against their own PoE (gated at action bar + Detail Panel footer + modal final guard). Two-step issuance modal (sectioned template picker + description), single-step revocation modal (required reason). Three notification types: `v22-badge-issued` + `v22-badge-revoked` (single-grantee, click pans to target PoE) + `v22-badge-template-new-version` (multi-grantee fan-out — when a template owner publishes a new version, every recipient with an active issuance against any prior version of the same lineage receives an informational notification). Card chip rendering on PoE + Claim cards (max 3 visible at 25% overlap + N overflow indicator; chip stack positions LEFT of NEW label when both present). Three Detail Panel sections: PoE Badges populated (was placeholder), Claim Badges NEW (aggregated walk: claim → eval results → PoEs → badges), Actor "Badges Received" NEW (filtered to received-only — issued-by-this-actor badges deliberately excluded). New `V22BadgeIssuancePanel` standalone Detail Panel + `'BADGE ISSUANCE'` router entry. Active Issuances section in Badge Template Detail Panel populated (per-version list + lineage-wide rollup subtext). Expand modal `'badge-issuance'` schema with Output (header + Parties + References + Description + Revocation context) + JSON tabs.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.1 (architectural):** `getJsonRecordFor` dispatcher accepts an optional `context` parameter for cross-artifact reference resolution. Used by Badge Issuance to derive recipient + lineage fields at record-build time. Caller shape: `{ poes, badgeTemplates }`. Future artifacts that need cross-references can use the same hook without re-architecting. Computed fields are clearly marked in the JSON record with `_computed_` prefix so consumers don't mistake them for canonical references.
- **§17.X / §10 — Phase 14.0 (#169 part 1 of 2):** Badge Template artifact introduced. Versioned Library artifact paralleling Requirements Set shape — same `lineageId` / `version` / `supersededBy` chain semantics, same own/published model. Network-wide and inherently public; any Actor can reference a template when issuing a badge (issuance work lives in 14.1). New factory `makeBadgeTemplate` + helper `getLatestBadgeTemplateVersion` in `src/v2/v2_2Data.js`. New JSON record helper `getBadgeTemplateJsonRecord` and ExpandedArtifactModal schema `'badge-template'` with Output (header + description + referenced RS table) + JSON tabs. LibraryModal gains a 4th "Badges" tab; new `BadgesPanel` mirrors `RequirementsPanel` layout with sectioned list (own + per-Actor groups) + create / new-version form. Forward-looking `V22BadgeTemplatePanel` registered under `'BADGE TEMPLATE'` router case for Phase 14.1's canvas node work.
- **§17.X — Phase 14.0 (Detail Panel polish):** Claim Detail Panel — Referenced Assets rows are now clickable; click pans/zooms the canvas to the Asset (`onSelectAsset` reused). Referenced Standards section replaces the "PUBLIC" text badge with the canonical globe icon (matches LibraryModal / RequirementsPanel patterns) wrapped in a `<Tooltip content="Published Standard">`.
- **§17.1 / §10 — Phase 13.4 (#175):** Every artifact type's expand modal now follows the canonical `[Output] [JSON]` two-tab convention. Output content is rich for Eval Result (header + minibar + aggregate + per-Requirements-Set tables with `Requirement | Value | Status | Confidence` columns; N/A rows dropped per Phase 13.2) and Proof of Evaluation (Section 1 = wrapped Eval Result Output content; Section 2 = full Evaluation Provenance supersession chain, oldest-first, clickable). Asset / Claim / DA / EA / Parse Result first tab labeled "Output" (existing first-tab content unchanged; substantive Output content for DA / EA / Parse Result deferred to backlog #180). The JSON tab on every type renders a hand-crafted distributed-storage record from `src/v2/data/jsonRecords.js` — references are ID-only (a Claim's `referencedAssetIds: ["asset-...", ...]`, not embedded Asset objects). Selective Asset views still surface a disclosed-portion-only record so file metadata stays private. Tab headers carry a disabled "Download" button with tooltip "Export coming soon." (wires up under #58). Modal width 1280px (was 720px). Each Output body wraps in a `position: relative` container so #172's PDF annotation overlay can mount without restructuring.
- **§17.1 — Phase 13.4 (#175 / Detail Panels):** PanelHeader gained an `actions` slot. V22ClaimPanel, V22EvalResultPanel, V22PoEPanel, V22ParseResultPanel, and DisclosureAgreementDetailPanel all carry an Expand button in the header now (EA Detail Panel already had one since 11C.2). The shared `<ExpandButton>` from `src/components/DetailPanel/shared/ExpandButton.jsx` is the canonical affordance.
- **§4 / §10.4 — Phase 13.3 (bug fix, #177 follow-up):** Proof of Evaluation creation no longer leaves a parallel `Latest ER → Claim` bypass edge. `deriveAgreementEdges` extends the chain map family with `wrappingPoeByErId` (erId → poeId for any ER with a wrapping PoE on canvas). When the auto-disclosure DA targeting the chain endpoint would emit `ER → Claim` and the ER is wrapped by a PoE, the edge emission is suppressed entirely — the synth wrap pass produces `ER → PoE` and the PoE-targeting DA produces `PoE → Claim`. Single chain path, no bypass.
- **§4 / §10.6 — Phase 13.3 (#177a):** Eval Result chains span multiple columns. Each ER's `chainPosition` (0 = origin/oldest) maps to a column offset (`x = COL_OWN_EVAL_eff + chainPosition * ER_COL_SPACING`). PoEs sit one column right of the wrapped ER's chain position. Globally aligned: column N is "chain position N" everywhere on the canvas. Downstream columns (Pulled Claim, Pulled Asset, Public, Owned PoE) shift right by `chainColShift = (maxChainLengthOnCanvas - 1) * ER_COL_SPACING` to accommodate. On the Claim owner's view, ER positions mirror — `effectivePos = (chainLength - 1) - chainPosition` — so Latest sits closest to the Claim and origin sits closest to the pulled counterparty Asset. Pulled PoEs sit one column LEFT of the mirror-anchored Latest, between Claim and chain head.
- **§13 — Phase 13.3 (Re-Run gating):** Re-Run Evaluation requires at least one in-scope Asset that wasn't in the prior `evidenceUsed`. Helper: `hasNewAssetsForRerun(inScopeAssetIds, priorEvalResult)`. When false, the action-bar button hides; the Detail Panel footer keeps the button visible-but-disabled with tooltip "No new evidence to evaluate." The Run Evaluation modal's RS picker also locks to the prior Eval Result's exact `requirementsSets[]` in Re-Run mode — every other RS row is disabled.
- **§13 — Phase 13.3 (UX):** Run Evaluation Step 3 header reads "Evaluating [Claim label] by [Claim owner]". Re-Run mode initializes Step 1 with all Asset rows collapsed; rows whose Asset isn't in the prior `evidenceUsed` render a NEW indigo badge.
- **§13 — Phase 13.3 (UX):** SUGGESTED badge hides on disabled / locked / PoE-blocked rows. SUGGESTED auto-promotes to the latest version of an RS family via `lineageId` walk in the picker.
- **§3 / §10.X — Phase 13.3 (UX):** Superseded Eval Result cards keep the grayscale filter but drop the 0.45 outer-card opacity — backgrounds are opaque so the canvas grid pattern doesn't show through.
- **§10.X — Phase 13.3 (#168 polish):** PoE name format = "Proof of [Claim label] Evaluation"; createdDate suffix dropped. PoE Detail Panel body PIN row removed (header click-to-copy badge is the canonical surface).
- **§13 — Phase 13.3 (#178 / #179):** Run Evaluation RS picker is a two-section accordion — "Your Requirements Sets" expanded by default, "Published Standards" collapsed by default. Published rows surface globe icon + publishing actor inline. Library `RequirementsPanel` left-panel rows gain inline globe icons on published RSes; right-panel `ViewDetails` header promotes the publishing-actor attribution to a prominent globe + "Published by **X** · date" line.
- **§4.1 / §10.4 — Phase 13.2 (architectural correction):** Only 4 disclosure edge types exist on the netgraph: full, selective, proof-only, provisional. Proof-only is the auto-disclosure default for evaluations. Both parties see all evaluation results in Detail Panels regardless of disclosure type — proof-only is an edge style + the discriminated-union subject discriminator (`subject.kind: 'evalResult' | 'poe'`), NOT a content restriction. Reflects the real-world supply-chain pattern where evaluation outcomes are shared without exposing the source documents.
- **§4 / §10.6 — Phase 13.2 (#177):** Eval Result supersession chains render as linked sequences on the netgraph. Sequence on the evaluator's canvas: `[Asset] → [E1 (oldest)] → [E2] → ... → [Latest Eval Result] → [Claim]`. Each adjacent pair connected by a proof-only edge derived from each ancestor's auto-disclosure DA, rerouted to target the chain successor instead of the Claim. The latest Eval Result keeps the Claim target. The Asset edge anchors only on the chain origin. PoE creation extends the chain: `... → [Latest Eval Result] → [PoE] → [Claim]`. The Latest → PoE edge is a synthesized proof-only wrap edge; the PoE → Claim edge comes from the PoE-targeting DA. Reverses left-to-right on the Claim owner's canvas via mirror placement.
- **§13 — Phase 13.2 (Re-Run bug):** Re-Run RS picker initializes with all RSes from the prior Eval Result's `requirementsSets[]` pre-checked AND locked; user can only expand the selection (add new RSes), not contract it. Locked rows show a "Carried over from prior evaluation" tooltip. The legacy singular `lockedRequirementsSetId` prop kept as fallback.
- **§3 / §X.Y — Phase 13.2 (#176):** Minibars restored on Claim card + Detail Panel header (V2.1 carryover); added to Eval Result + PoE cards in place of the prior text aggregates. Minibar primitive (`HealthBar` in AssetNode.jsx, exported for shared use) renders SAT/UNSAT/MISSING segments in green/red/amber. N/A is excluded from minibar segments and from Detail Panel header aggregates. Per-row N/A entries in the Eval Result Detail Panel render dimmed (opacity 0.45) so structure stays visible. The N/A status remains in the data model unchanged.
- **§10.X — Phase 13.2 (#177):** PoE Detail Panel "Wrapped Eval Result" section renamed to "Evaluation Provenance" with the full supersession chain listed oldest-first. V2App resolves the chain by walking `priorEvalResultId` from the wrapped Eval Result back to the chain origin.
- **§10.6 — Phase 13.1 (#168a, model correction):** Multi-RS evaluation produces ONE Eval Result containing all selected RSes' results bundled — corrects Phase 12.2's wrong "N Eval Results sharing batchId" model. `makeEvaluationResult` factory takes `requirementsSets[]` (plural array of `{ id, name, version }`) plus a flat `results[]` where every row carries its own `requirementsSetId`. The `batchId` field is removed. The Sibling Evaluations Detail Panel section is removed with the concept. Eval Result Detail Panel renders Results grouped by RS with section headers + an aggregate row "X SAT · Y UNSAT · Z MISSING · W N/A across N Requirements Sets".
- **§10.4 — Phase 13.1 (bug fix):** Proof-only Disclosure Agreements use a discriminated-union shape — `subject.kind` disambiguates `evalResult` (auto-disclosure DA created at Eval Result save time) vs `poe` (PoE-creation DA, plus published proof-only Claim DAs). `scope.evaluationResultIds` is restored alongside `scope.poeIds`; both fields exist on the data shape, consumers branch on `subject.kind`. The `makeProofOfEvalDisclosureAgreement` factory accepts either `evaluationResultId` OR `poeId` (mutually exclusive). This fixes the Phase 13 Save crash where the grep-and-replace had over-migrated the field name.
- **§3.5 / §10.X — Phase 13.1 (#168a):** Proof of Evaluation simplified to a 1:1 wrap. PoE wraps exactly one Eval Result via `wrappedEvalResultId` (singular). Card body aggregate reads "X SAT · Y UNSAT · N RS" matching the wrapped Eval Result. PoE Detail Panel "Wrapped Eval Result" (singular header) shows a single clickable row.
- **§13 — Phase 13.1 (UX):** Re-Run Evaluation gating moves from submit-time to entry-point. Action-bar Re-Run button hides when an Eval Result is wrapped by an owned PoE. V22EvalResultPanel footer keeps Re-Run visible-but-disabled with a tooltip. V22RunEvaluationModal RS picker disables PoE-covered Requirements Sets with a `PoE` chip + tooltip "Already finalized as a Proof of Evaluation." Submit-time gate from Phase 13 is removed.
- **§13 — Phase 13.1 (UX):** Run Evaluation Step 3 modal header subtitle reads "Reviewing [Claim name]" (matching Step 1's wording style). Result-status button explicitly disables text selection so double-click doesn't select the label.
- **§3.5 / §10.X — Phase 13.1 (PoE creation):** Clicking Create Proof of Evaluation atomically (a) builds the new PoE; (b) marks the prior Eval-Result-targeting auto-disclosure DA `status: 'revoked'` (its edge unravel-animates); (c) creates a new PoE-targeting proof-only DA (its edge reveal-animates). The Claim owner sees the transition: the Eval-Result-anchored disclosure edge unravels, and the PoE node + its edge appear.
- **§10.X — Phase 13.1 (data hygiene):** Seed PoE / Eval Result / DA / EA + actor-name-leaking Asset IDs regenerated to a content-addressed-style `[type]-[8-char-base32]` format via a new `makeArtifactId(prefix, seedKey)` helper. No actor names appear in seed IDs.
- **§3.5 / §6.X / §10.X — Phase 13 (#168):** Proof of Evaluation (PoE) introduced as a new first-class node type. Wraps an active Eval Result batch (or solo Eval Result); created by deliberate Evaluator action via the Eval Result card / Detail Panel "Create Proof of Evaluation" entry; terminates the evaluation chain for the (Asset set, RS set, evaluator) combination. PoE has its own DOT (hash null, like Claims). The `subject.kind` enum gains `'poe'`. AssetNode renders `PROOF OF EVALUATION` type label + "Wraps N · X SAT · Y UNSAT" aggregate row.
- **§6.X / §10.X — Phase 13 (#168):** Proof-only Disclosure Agreements migrated to PoE-targeting. The `scope.evaluationResultIds` field renamed to `scope.poeIds` across the data model, factories, view derivation, edge derivation, and all consumers (no backwards-compat alias). Disclosing a PoE auto-discloses every wrapped Eval Result via share-PoE-shares-all semantics; the grantee canvas pulls in the PoE node alongside its wrapped Eval Results. Existing seed proof-only DAs migrated retroactively at seed construction. New `mergeProvisionals.proofsOfEvaluation` merge for in-progress Create-PoE flows.
- **§8.7 / §17.1 — Phase 12.7 (#171c):** Asset accordion left-panel structure converted from split-container (capped row list + dedicated body card with `fillHeight`) to a single overflow container holding rows with inline-expanded bodies. Scales correctly to arbitrary Asset counts. Inline-expanded body iframe sized at 480px (up from 360 in Phase 12.5) for a more substantial preview now that only one Asset shows at a time. Parse Evidence left/right panel height parity preserved via `flex: 1; minHeight: 0` on the outer overflow container — the column fills available height regardless of content extent. `<AssetEvidencePanel>` `fillHeight` prop retained for future callers but no longer applied by the modal accordions.
- **§8.7 / §17.1 — Phase 12.6 (#171b):** Asset accordion converted from multi-expand to single-expand; row list becomes a scroll container so many in-scope Assets remain navigable. Phase 12.5's auto-expand-on-set-growth behavior removed (no longer needed under single-expand). V22ParseEvidenceModal left/right panel heights now parity — `<AssetEvidencePanel>` grew a `fillHeight` prop that switches the iframe / fields list to `flex: 1; minHeight: 200`, replacing the fixed 360px cap that left a ~48px shortfall on the left side. ExpandedArtifactModal stays on the legacy fixed-height path (its tab body region is sized by the modal).
- **§8.7 / §17.1 — Phase 12.5 (#171a):** V22RunEvaluationModal and V22ParseEvidenceModal left panels converted to V2.1 accordion pattern (multiple Asset rows expandable inline, all expanded by default at modal open). V22RunEvaluationModal right panel unified into a single scroll surface with sticky RS group headers (replaces per-RS scroll boxes that didn't scroll). V22ParseEvidenceModal right-panel hierarchy adjustments — helper text below "Parse Template *" title, Parse Template list scrollable, "Fields to extract" panel stretches to viewer height. Filed #172 (PDF annotation overlay) as end-of-Phase-12 assessment item.
- **§8.7 / §17.1 — Phase 12.4 (#171):** Restored Asset evidence viewer to V22RunEvaluationModal and V22ParseEvidenceModal left panels per §17.1 split-panel parity. Modal widens to ~1280px with 1:1 column split. Disclosure-type-aware viewer (Full → AssetEvidenceViewer, Selective → parsed-fields table, owner → AssetEvidenceViewer; proof-only is unreachable since EAs aren't paired with proof-only DAs). Multi-Asset selector list at top of left panel. AssetEvidenceViewer + parsed-fields renderer lifted into shared `<AssetEvidencePanel>` component used by ExpandedArtifactModal too.
- **§10.6 — Phase 12.3:** Run Evaluation Requirements Set picker uses checkbox multi-select; "+ BATCH" mechanism removed. No primary RS concept at runtime — all checked RSes are equal batch members.
- **§10.6 — Phase 12.3:** Run Evaluation Review stage renders grouped requirement rows per selected RS, in check order. Submit-with-defaults still allowed.
- **§10.3 / §10.3b — Phase 12.3 (Bug B fix):** AmendClaim Remove now propagates correctly to all read sites of Claim → Asset references. `deriveAgreementEdges` intersects internal claim-ref DA scope.assetIds against the Claim's active `referencedAssetIds[]`; Run Evaluation `scopeAssetIds` for non-self path also intersects against the active set. New `getInScopeAssets(claim, allAssets)` helper as single source of truth. Filed #170 to consolidate the dual-shape `assetReferences[]` / `referencedAssetIds[]` model in a follow-up phase.
- **§10.6 — Phase 12.3 (Bug A fix):** RS picker source list deduped at composition time (owner-authored + public pools merged by id; owner-authored wins on duplicate).
- **§10.6 — Phase 12.3 (Bug C fix):** Superseded Eval Result Detail Panel Supersession list items clickable — same row-click pattern as the Sibling Evaluations section.
- **§10.3 / §10.3b — Phase 12.2 (#122):** Claim-internal Asset versioning via `assetReferences[]` supersession chain (parallel to the active `referencedAssetIds[]` primitive list). Asset nodes immutable. AmendClaim Option A drop semantics (Replace + Remove). New OUTDATED Eval Result status with amber-dashed visual treatment + persistence-until-rerun behavior. New `v22-eval-result-stale` notification type (single-grantee, informational, dismiss does NOT clear OUTDATED). New helpers `getLatestAssetVersion`, `isEvalResultStale`.
- **§10.6 — Phase 12.2 (#106):** Run Evaluation modal becomes review-rows-only at open; Asset picker dropped. `evidenceUsed` snapshots all in-scope Assets at evaluation time.
- **§10.6 — Phase 12.2 (#121):** Multi-RS evaluation support; N Eval Results share a `batchId`; backwards-compatible solo evaluations carry `batchId: null`. New `priorEvalResultId` + `evidenceDiff` audit fields.
- **§10.6 — Phase 12.2 (#117):** Re-run diff readout (banner in modal + section in Detail Panel); `evidenceDiff` stamped on new Eval Result; `priorEvalResultId` links chain. New helper `computeEvidenceDiff`.
- **§13 — Phase 12.2 (#105):** Run Evaluation modal empty-evidence copy split by role (owner vs. evaluator).
- **§10.3 / §10.3a — Phase 12.1:** Claims gain non-binding `referencedRequirementsSets[]` field; informational only, no evaluation coupling. Inline supersession-update affordance scoped to this section only. Adds + removes go through AmendClaim as cascade-skip amendments (no Eval Result staleness, no notifications).
- **§2.3 Proof-Only disclosure — Phase 6:** added the rule that Proof-Only disclosure requires at least one existing Eval Result on the Claim at response time; response modal lists existing Eval Results and blocks with an informational message when none exist.
- **§3 Node type labels + state badges — Phase 3 / Phase 6 / Phase 6.5+:** codified the multi-line layout (type label above name) and documented `PROVISIONAL` / `DECLINED` / `SUPERSEDED` as separate inline badges (not suffixes on `v22Type`), with `DECLINED` outranking `PROVISIONAL`.
- **§4.4 Selected-edge state — Phase 3 / Phase 4:** revised blend from 40% → 65% white and stroke from +0.5px → +1.5px; documented the rationale (visibility parity across all four disclosure styles against the dark canvas).
- **§6.4 Counterparty referenced-Asset filtering — Phase 6.5:** clarified that non-owner viewers of a Claim Detail Panel see only the referenced-Asset subset covered by an active DA in which they are grantee.
- **§6.5 Cross-canvas pull-in rules — Phase 3 / Phase 6 / Phase 6.5 (new subsection):** codified (1) accepted inter-party DA pull-in, (2) provisional DAs do not pull onto the grantor's canvas, (3) "Carol orphan fix" second-pass counterparty internal DA inclusion, (4) declined DA retention.
- **§7.1 Story 1 — Phase 4 / Phase 5 / Phase 6 / Phase 6.5:** step 1 anchored to the Asset Detail Panel footer (primary) with banner (secondary); step 2 provisional node is requester-canvas-only; step 3 introduced `v22-request` notification delivery to the grantor; step 4 response flow is 4 steps with Full Asset checklist, Proof-Only Eval Result picker, advisory EA terms; step 5 describes the two-canvas reveal (grantee anchor Asset gets `_isNew` on the grantor's canvas too); step 6 surfaced the duplicate-detection block with jump-to-existing.
- **§7.4 V2.2 Notification Types — Phase 4 / Phase 6 / Phase 6.5 (new subsection):** tabled `v22-request` / `v22-amendment` / `v22-evaluation` with grantor-vs-grantee delivery, click-through behaviour, and persistence semantics (request persists until terminal action; others dismiss on click).
- **§10.4 Disclosure Agreement subject — Phase 1:** replaced the V2.1 `claimId` field with `subject: { kind: 'asset' | 'claim' | 'evalResult' | 'parseResult', id }`; added the subject-assignment table.
- **§10.4 subject-assignment table — Phase 4 pre-fix:** added the Parse Result → source Asset row (one internal Full DA per Parse Result so `parse → asset` edges render on the owner's canvas).
- **§10.5 `authorizedRequirementsSetIds` — Phase 6:** downgraded from enforced gate to advisory / informational; the grantee may run any Requirements Set from their library against the Claim.
- **§11.3 Evaluation amendment — Phase 6.5+:** added the duplicate-detection rule (exact `(Req Set, evidence Set)` match blocks with a "View it on the canvas →" jump); clarified the three outcomes (block / supersede / new-result).
- **§11.4 Disclosure decline — Phase 6.5:** rewrote from "both artifacts deleted" to decline retention with `_declineMeta` annotation; declined DA derives its edge normally in DECLINED state; Dismiss strips annotations and collapses both artifacts + edge together.
- **§11.6 Self-evaluation — Phase 6 (new subsection):** documented the self-eval artifact shape (no EA; internal proof-of-evaluation DA + ownership DA with grantor == grantee); noted the `deriveAgreementEdges` internal-proof branch and that duplicate detection / supersede rules apply identically.
- **§12.6 Demo dataset composition — Phase 6.5 (new subsection):** enumerated actors + asset/claim/parse/eval/DA counts shipped in `buildV22SharedArtifacts`, including Bob's 3 Assets and Carol's 2 Assets added in Phase 5.
- **§1 Summary + document structure note — Phase 8:** reframed from "V2.2 migration spec" to "platform architecture spec" post-migration; added a note clarifying that §12 + §13 are preserved as historical record rather than operative guidance.
- **§7.4 Notification Types — Phase 8:** section title dropped the "V2.2" prefix.
- **§11.6 Self-evaluation — Phase 8:** section title dropped the "(Phase 6)" suffix.
- **§11.8 Parse flow — Phase 8 (new subsection):** documented the V2.2 Parse Evidence flow (`V22ParseEvidenceModal` + `makeParseRunArtifacts` factory + internal Full DA wiring the new Parse Result to its source Asset). Closes the one remaining gap in V2.2's process coverage — parsing was seeded in demo data but had no user-facing creation flow pre-Phase-8.
- **§1 annotation convention + §18 Production Handoff Map — Spec annotation pass (2026-04-23):** introduced a systematic Prototype-vs-Production annotation convention at the top of the document (four-part callout: Topic / Prototype behavior / Production behavior / Authority); added a client-canon terminology-mapping note on first-use of "Disclosure Agreement" (SDA), "Asset" (Data Object), and the endorsement UI step. Inline flags added in §2.3 (process authority), §2.5 (QS + endorsement, reformatted from prior partial flag), §2.6 (DOT issuance + COP lineage), §6 (view-building + access control + Permission Consumption), §8.5 (Directory Layer, reformatted), §9 (AI Shopper, reformatted), §10 opening (schema fidelity, reformatted), §10.1 / §10.3 / §10.4 / §10.5 / §10.6 (per-schema field-authority flags), §11.1 through §11.8 (every state transition, including new §11.5a for Revocation Phase 9D). New §18 Production Handoff Map consolidates every flag into a master reference for production-build planning, organized by concern area (registration, storage, agreement lifecycle, evaluation execution, access control, notifications, cryptographic primitives, out-of-scope). Protocol names used: DPP (Data Property Protocol), SDP (Selective Disclosure Protocol), REP (RICE Evaluation Protocol), PEP (Parse and Extract Protocol), COP (Change of Ownership Protocol), with "Platform" as umbrella when attribution spans multiple protocols. Addresses client-report issue C1 (Prototype-vs-Production annotation applied inconsistently).
- **§11.5a Agreement revocation — Phase 9D (new subsection):** documented the four revocation cases (A: grantor revokes DA, B: grantee revokes DA, C: grantor revokes EA, D: grantee revokes EA), inline-vs-Claim-level Detail Panel patterns, the cascade rules (DA → paired EA, but NOT to Eval Results), the dismiss mechanic (`_dismissedRevoked`), and the unravel animation choreography (Phase 9D.2.x). Inline `Prototype note` callouts added to §11 covering SDP lifecycle authority. Eval Result persistence: Eval Results are independent artifacts that survive their backing DA's revocation; orphaned Eval Results gain a Dismiss action in their Detail Panel footer. Phase 9D.1.6 added the internal-DA carve-out to the POE cascade so an Eval Result keeps its ownership edge to its evaluator's Asset on the evaluator's canvas after revocation.
- **§3.7 Layout — Phase 10.2.1 (new subsection):** codified grid-alignment rules (all positions multiples of 100), `symmetricRowY(i)` distribution (alternate-outward from y=0), per-column Y offset table, elastic columns based on Asset hierarchy depth.
- **§10.1 Asset hierarchy — Phase 10.2:** added `parentAssetId` field with the Prototype note covering DPP authority. Hierarchy constraints (single-party, no cycles, parent must precede child) documented inline. §6.4 updated to clarify counterparty visibility — parent-child structure is owner-only.
- **§8.6 Library — Phase 10.3 (new subsection):** documented the unified three-tab Library (Parsing Templates / Requirement Sets / Published Requirements) with Prototype note covering Platform-side registry authority. §17.1 Future Direction updated to reflect that the Library is shipped, not future.
- **Register Asset modal copy — Phase 10.1:** plain-language rewrite of step 1 / step 2 / hashing-progress strings to remove model-vocabulary leakage. No structural change; copy strings only.
- **§11.6a EA-only request lifecycle — Phase 11C (new subsection):** documented the warm-path EA-only request flow. New `EARequestModal` (single-step) for the requester; `CombinedResponseModal` extended with `eaOnlyMode` for the grantor's response (lands at step 3, hides DA scope/type steps). New factories `makeProvisionalEvaluationAgreement` and `finalizeProvisionalEvaluationAgreement`. Three new notification types added to §7.4 (`v22-request-ea-only`, `v22-ea-accepted`, `v22-ea-declined`).
- **§10.5 EA terms — Phase 11C (#115):** added `terms.resultConfidentiality` and `terms.attribution` acknowledgment booleans (default false; surfaced as checkboxes in the request flow, read-only in the response flow). Production enforcement out of scope; demo records the values for audit.
- **§10.5 EA expiry — Phase 11C:** documented the demo-only `evaluationDeadline` check at Run Evaluation time (modal refuses to open when the deadline is past). Production replaces with platform-level policy.
- **§6.3a Dave's view — Phase 11C (new subsection):** Dave (ChipCo) added to ROLES so the warm-path response flow is testable end-to-end. Mechanically identical to Alice (supplier-side view).
- **CombinedRequestModal two-step — Phase 11C (#113):** cold-path request flow split into Step 1 (Disclosure: PIN + Req Sets + message) and Step 2 (EA: expiry + acknowledgments). Submission produces a provisional DA + EA pair carrying the new EA terms.
- **Phase 11C.1 architectural correction — terms vs. acknowledgments:** Phase 11C drafted requester-authored `terms.resultConfidentiality` + `terms.attribution` booleans on the EA. The actual model is that terms are responder-authored — the Claim owner sets agreement terms when responding. The requester's role is to acknowledge pre-set commitments the Claim owner authored on the Claim itself. Cleanup: §10.3 Claim schema gained an `acknowledgments[]` array (owner-authored at Claim creation). §10.5 EA schema dropped `terms.resultConfidentiality` + `terms.attribution`; added a top-level `acknowledgmentsAccepted` array (ids referencing the Claim's `acknowledgments[]`).
