CombinedRequestModal Step 2 + EARequestModal both render the Claim's `acknowledgments[]` as required checkboxes; Submit gates on all-checked. CombinedResponseModal step 3 shows a read-only "Requester accepted these acknowledgments" panel alongside the responder-authored expiry input. §11.6a updated to reflect the new flow.
- **Phase 11C.2 — reveal animation + acknowledgments display + EA Expand modal:** Phase 11C.1 QA exposed five follow-ups, all shipped. The `_wasProvisional` flag the V2.1-era reveal-animation guard reads in V2App's notification-click handler was dead infrastructure since the V2.2 retreat; `v22DataWithReveal` now stamps it on Claim reveals (only the recently-accepted Claim id, not pulled-in Asset reveals) so the flip-from-provisional → active animation finally fires on cold + warm path acceptance. §10.3 Claim acknowledgments are now surfaced on the Claim Detail Panel (visible to all viewers — owners see what they authored; counterparties see what they agreed to). The Phase 11B `ExpandedArtifactModal` schema gained an `'evaluation-agreement'` schema (JSON-only — Output tab hidden since EAs have no file or structured rows that map cleanly); `EvaluationAgreementDetailPanel` exposes the Expand affordance via a new `onExpand` prop. CombinedResponseModal `eaOnlyMode` titles spell out "Evaluation Agreement Request" rather than the abbreviated "EA Request"; the read-only `ReadonlyAck` checkbox switched from indigo to grey to signal "locked, can't change."
- **Phase 11C.3 — reveal animation timing fix + Expand icon consistency:** Phase 11C.2's `_wasProvisional` wiring set the guard correctly but the flip animation still played from active → active rather than provisional → active. Diagnosis: at notification-click time the artifact has already finalized (responder's accept fired finalize → `_provisional` cleared in `v22Provisionals`), so AssetNode's `isProvisional` predicate returns false and the dashed/dimmed render never appears. Fix: `v22DataWithReveal` now also stamps `_showAsProvisional: true` on the recently-accepted Claim, forcing AssetNode to render provisional state during the reveal window. The stamp clears at reveal phase 'done' via `setV22RecentlyAcceptedClaimId(null)` — next render shows clean active state. The reveal animation also migrated out of inline V2App.jsx into `src/v2/animations/reveal.js` for code-organization parity with the unravel primitive; `startReveal` is now a thin wrapper. Plus W4: Expand icon button extracted to `src/components/DetailPanel/shared/ExpandButton.jsx` and standardized to the EA panel's two-opposing-corner-arrows icon (replaced V22NodeDetailPanel's diagonal-arrow icon).
- **Phase 11C.4 — edge reveal animation + warm-path notification reveal trigger:** Phase 11C.3 fixed the Claim card's flip but two issues remained. (1) The disclosure edge connecting the Claim to the requester's Asset rendered as active during the reveal window — V2Canvas already reads `edge._showAsProvisional` and renders dashed for provisional, but the stamp wasn't being applied during reveal. Fix: `v22DataWithReveal` now also maps `v22Data.edges` and stamps `_showAsProvisional: true` on edges incident to the recently-accepted Claim id; the stamp clears at reveal phase 'done' alongside the node stamp via the same `v22RecentlyAcceptedClaimId` clear. (2) The `v22-ea-accepted` notification handler wasn't checking `_isNew && _wasProvisional` so warm-path acceptances skipped the reveal entirely. Fix: extended the cold-path reveal-trigger pattern (V2App:3221) to the warm-path notification handler — `v22-ea-accepted` clicks now fire `startReveal(targetNode.id)` when both flags are set; `v22-ea-declined` keeps the simple animated pan since declined Claims stay in their declined visual state without a flip-to-active transition.
- **Phase 11C.5 — NEW badge persistence + reveal animation cleanup:** Phase 11C.3's `onDone` callback at reveal phase 'done' cleared `v22RecentlyAcceptedClaimId`, which over-cleared. It correctly dropped the `_showAsProvisional` stamp (which should clear) but also dropped `_isNew + _wasProvisional` (which should persist until the user deselects, per Phase 7 carry-over #1). The reveal window's two stamp lifecycles are now decoupled: a new state var `v22RevealActiveClaimId` (set at `startReveal` start, cleared at phase 'done') gates `_showAsProvisional` on the Claim node + incident edges; `v22RecentlyAcceptedClaimId` (cleared by the deselect-aware effect at V2App:2141) continues to gate `_isNew + _wasProvisional`. The two stamps now have independent lifecycles — the orange tint + NEW badge persist until deselect, while the dashed/dimmed provisional render clears at reveal completion.
- **§10.4 grantee view derivation per disclosure type — Phase 11D.2 (new subsection):** documented how the Claim Detail Panel's Referenced Assets section + Expand modal branch by the active DA's `type` for non-owner viewers. Selective grantees see a per-Asset disclosed-field count beside each Asset row (intersection of `scope.fieldIds` with Parse Results derived from the Asset). The Expand modal's Output tab renders a parsed-fields table rather than the file viewer; the JSON tab renders a disclosed-portion-only view (`{ assetId, name, owner, disclosureType, disclosedFields }`) — the underlying `file.{filename, hash, mimeType, size, uri, localPath}` is suppressed since it isn't covered by the Selective DA. Full + owner views unchanged. Proof-only deferred to Phase 11D.3.
- **§10.4 Proof-only Eval Result placement + AssetNode tooltip z-index — Phase 11D.4.1:** Phase 11D.4's fixed-column placement (`COL_PULLED_EVAL = 1700`) pushed the pulled-in Eval Result into the actor's own evaluation column on the grantee canvas, and the connecting edge crossed unrelated nodes on Dave's canvas (e.g. ChipCo own Claims/Assets traffic at y=0–300). Switched to source-Claim-derived placement: `(sourceClaim.x + 200, sourceClaim.y + 300)` — Eval Result hangs off-and-below the source Claim with stacking by `COL_Y_OFFSET` when multiple ERs disclose under one DA. Edge stays short, traffic lanes stay clean. Independently, AssetNode mini + dot LOD tooltip portals (`createPortal` to `document.body`, `position: fixed`) had `zIndex: 5000` — above the Detail Panel's 200. Selected-node-while-canvas-drag rendered the portal'd tooltip ON TOP of the open Detail Panel as the tooltip's screen position drifted into the panel's footprint. Capped both portal tooltip z-indices to 150 — below the panel (200), still above the canvas itself.
- **§10.4 grantee view derivation — Phase 11D.4 fixes:** (1) Layout — `COL_PULLED_EVAL` moved 2300 → 1700 (now sharing the column with `COL_OWN_EVAL`; y separation preserves non-overlap since own evals carry `COL_Y_OFFSET = 100` while proof-only-pulled evals match the source Claim's row, typically y=0). Phase 11D.3 placed it at 2300 which produced visible card overlap with the pulled Claim at 2100. (2) Selective grantee Referenced Assets count — clarified that the section header reflects the disclosed-Asset count (`referencedAssetNames.length`), not the Claim's full `referencedAssetIds.length`. Pre-fix the count read the raw Claim size, so a Selective grantee with 1 of 3 disclosed saw "(3)" but only 1 row.
- **§10.4 grantee view derivation: Proof-only branch — Phase 11D.3:** wired the proof-only path. New seed `da-alice-dave-prm-proof` (Alice → Dave, subject = PRM Claim, type = 'proofonly', scope.evaluationResultIds = [Bob's MIL-PRF-55681 result]). `buildViewForActor` now pulls the source Claim AND the chosen Eval Results onto the grantee's canvas (`pulledInClaimIds` extended; new `proofOnlyPulledEvalIds` set surfaced on the view). Edge derivation gained a proof-only Claim DA branch producing two edge classes: Claim ↔ granteeAssetId (anchor) and one edge per disclosed Eval Result → Claim. New layout column `COL_PULLED_EVAL = 2300` places pulled-in Eval Results between the pulled Claim and the grantee's anchor Asset. V22ClaimPanel: Referenced Assets section renders `(0)` + "No Assets disclosed under this agreement." for proof-only-only viewers. Evaluation Results rows are now clickable (any viewer) — pan + open Eval Result Detail Panel. Eval Result panel: section renamed Evaluator → Owner; new "Claim" row resolves the linked Claim name. ExpandedArtifactModal: defensive proof-only Asset branch (empty-state Output + bare-identity JSON) for the unlikely case the row is Expanded.
- **§7.4 Notification table + §11.2 prototype notes — Phase 11E.2 (#102):** the existing `v22-amendment` (DA-amendment) notification type was renamed to `v22-da-amendment` so it parallels `v22-ea-amendment`; click handler updated to deep-link directly to the DA Detail Panel via `setOpenAgreement({ kind: 'disclosure', disclosureAgreementId })`, mirroring the EA amendment routing instead of the prior pan-only behavior. Notification body copy now includes the Claim name + optional `(Note: …)` suffix — pre-fix the body fell through to a bare `req.asset?.name`. (Phase 11E.2 also briefly shipped a parallel `v22-claim-amendment` fan-out, rolled back in Phase 11E.4 after the conceptual review concluded that grantees only learn of new Claim content via DA amendment, never via the Claim amendment itself. The DA-amendment side of Phase 11E.2 is preserved.)
- **No-spec-impact sub-phases through Phase 11.6.1:** the following code-only sub-phases shipped without changes to spec semantics — see `CLAUDE-phase-log.md` for per-sub-phase detail. **Phase 11E.1.1 → 11E.1.7** (Amend EA polish bursts, May 1-2 2026): submit-on-open bug fix, deadline-field read audits across `EdgeHoverMenu` + `EvaluationAgreementRow`, ExpiryPicker preset-card label fixes, modal subtitle/footer copy upgrades, REVOKED badge relocation to the Row 0 type-label header, "Never expires" copy unification across DA + EA + Modal surfaces, DA expiration UX added to Response Step 2 + Amend DA modal, AMEND inline-row gating on revoked rows, Step 4 abbreviation expansion, fixed-720px modal height for the response flow, Detail Panel close on Directory Layer entry, AmendDisclosureModal zero-Asset gating, backlog #162 (EA revocation copy + Directory post-revocation visualization) + #163 (anchor Asset picker for warm-path Directory requests) filed. **Phase 11E.5** (May 3): edge draw-in geometry stub fix attempt + notification deep-link edge selection + #164 filed. **Phase 11E.6** (May 3): one-line `_fullInstanceCount = Infinity` root-cause fix — `THREE.LineGeometry.setPositions` doesn't update `instanceCount`; deriving segment count from `curvePoints.length - 1` instead. **Phase 11E.8** (May 3): `anyDecoration` early-return guard extended with reveal-window state + draw-in duration 500ms → 1200ms. **Phase 11E.9** (May 3): reverse draw-in animation direction (anchor → Claim) + `applyEdgeStylingRef` honors the `showAsProvisional` userData flag through restyle passes. **Phase 11.6.1** (May 3): notification dismissal on amendment accept/reject, EA Detail Panel acknowledgments source-of-truth (now reads from the live Claim), Run Evaluation gating preserved during pending-acceptance, Revoke override preserved during pending-acceptance, current-terms display added above proposed amendments in both `AmendEvaluationAgreementModal` and `AmendmentResponseModal`.
- **§11.2a major revision + new §11.2b + §7.4 update — Phase 11.6 (#164):** EA amendments converted from unilateral grantor mutations (Phase 11E.1 Option B) to bilateral proposals requiring grantee acceptance. Closes the post-acceptance acknowledgment-injection exploit vector. New EA `status` enum value `'pending-acceptance'` (between `'active'` and a possible return to `'active'` after the grantee responds). New `amendments[]` item shape: `id`, `status: 'pending'|'accepted'|'rejected'|'superseded'`, `proposalMessage`, `responseMessage`, `responseDate`, `termsBefore`, `proposed: { evaluationDeadline, acknowledgments }`, `acknowledgmentChanges`. Three new factory helpers in `v2_2Data.js`: `proposeEvaluationAgreementAmendment` (replaces `makeAmendedEvaluationAgreement`), `acceptEvaluationAgreementAmendment`, `rejectEvaluationAgreementAmendment`. Three new V2App handlers: `handleV22ProposeEvaluationAmendment`, `handleV22AmendmentAccept`, `handleV22AmendmentReject`. New `AmendmentResponseModal.jsx` for the grantee's accept/reject UX with diff display + per-row acknowledgment ticking + expiration confirm + free-text response. Updated `AmendEvaluationAgreementModal.jsx`: subtitle copy reflects the proposal model + pending-evaluations pause; submit button label "Submit Amendment Proposal" (was "Amend Evaluation Agreement"); "Amendment note" field renamed "Proposal message". Pending-acceptance lock enforced UI-side: `V22RunEvaluationModal` blocks submit + shows footer copy directing grantee to respond to the proposal; `EvaluationAgreementDetailPanel` + `EvaluationAgreementRow` AMEND buttons disabled with tooltip pointing to the pending grantee response. Notifications updated: old `v22-ea-amendment` informational type sunset; three new types `v22-ea-amendment-proposal` / `v22-ea-amendment-accepted` / `v22-ea-amendment-rejected` with distinct badge labels (`AMENDMENT PROPOSAL` indigo, `AMENDMENT ACCEPTED` green, `AMENDMENT REJECTED` red) — distinct from the unified `AMENDMENT` badge used for DA amendments since these carry actionable consequences. Click handler routes proposal → `AmendmentResponseModal` (persists until terminal action), accepted/rejected → pan + EA Detail Panel + edge selection (auto-dismiss). Architecture spec §11.2a major revision documents the new lifecycle + record shape; new §11.2b documents the pending-acceptance lock semantics including the revoke override; §7.4 notification table replaces the old single row with three new rows. Same V2App reveal-stamp pattern applies to proposals as to acceptances per §11.5b — pre-click pending-reveal stamp ensures the EA edge renders provisional grey on the grantee's view from proposal until notification click.
- **§11.5b Acceptance reveal stamp lifecycle — Phase 11E.7 (new subsection):** documented the three-phase lifecycle of `_showAsProvisional` on the grantee's view of an accepted Claim — pre-click window (acceptance until notification click), reveal animation window, post-reveal. Pre-fix the stamp gated only on `v22RevealActiveClaimId` (the active-reveal-only gate from Phase 11C.5), so the grantee's view rendered the new Claim + typed edge in active state immediately on acceptance; clicking the notification then visually regressed the Claim to provisional before re-animating to active, reading as "acceptance, then re-acceptance" rather than a true first-time materialization. New gate composition: `_showAsProvisional` applies if (a) the claim is currently mid-reveal animation OR (b) the active viewer has a pending acceptance notification for this Claim. New per-role state slot `v22PendingRevealsByRole` populated by the acceptance handlers; drained by the notification click handler immediately before `startReveal` fires so React batches both state updates into the same render — gate (b) clears in the same frame gate (a) sets. The grantor (responder) does not carry the stamp; their view transitions to active immediately on acceptance. Edge stamping in `v22DataWithReveal` extended to compute the union of active-reveal + pending-reveal claim ids and stamp incident edges accordingly.
- **§7.4 Notification table + §11.2 prototype note — Phase 11E.4 (#102 rollback + #139 fix):** rolled back the `v22-claim-amendment` notification type added in Phase 11E.2 — counterparties don't see Claim amendments directly; they learn of new content via DA amendment. §7.4 row removed; §11.2 prototype-note Claim-amendment entry trimmed to remove the notification reference. Phase 11E.2 changelog entry corrected. Independently, `v22-da-amendment` and `v22-ea-amendment` badge labels unified to `AMENDMENT` (was `DA AMENDED` / `EA AMENDED`) — the badge is a category tag, body copy already specifies which artifact was amended. Phase 11E.3's edge draw-in animation also reworked here under the two-edge architecture Andrew's spec called for: V2Canvas now exposes a separate `revealOverlayGroup` containing typed-style overlay edges that survive `buildEdges`'s `clearGroup` calls, plus four atomic methods (`addRevealOverlayEdge` / `playEdgeDrawInById` / `fadeEdgeOpacityById` / `removeRevealOverlayEdge`). The reveal animation orchestrator (`src/v2/animations/edgeDrawIn.js` `playRevealEdgeAnimation`) coordinates: at draw-in start a typed overlay edge is added with stub geometry at the anchor; geometry grows over 500ms; provisional canonical edge fades out concurrent with the Claim card flip; overlay is removed after reveal phase 'done' has had a chance to re-render the canonical typed edge.
- **§11.2a Evaluation Agreement amendment — Phase 11E.1 (#108) (new subsection):** wired the EA amendment flow end-to-end. New modal `AmendEvaluationAgreementModal.jsx` (expiration via shared `ExpiryPicker` + acknowledgment editor list). New factory `makeAmendedEvaluationAgreement` mirrors `makeAmendedDisclosureAgreement` shape; new pure helper `diffAcknowledgments(before, after)` computes `{ added, removed, edited }`. `makeEvaluationAgreement` extended with `amendments[]` parameter. Each amendment record carries `date`, `note`, `termsBefore.evaluationDeadline`, and `acknowledgmentChanges`. New notification type `v22-ea-amendment` (single-grantee, informational; click pans to Claim and opens EA Detail Panel). EA Detail Panel: Amendments section between Status + paired-DA navigation; Amend footer button gated to grantor + active + non-revoked. **Option B (prototype): acknowledgments live on the Claim** — editing via Amend EA mutates the underlying `claim.acknowledgments[]` directly while the EA's `acknowledgmentsAccepted` audit trail is preserved. Multi-EA implicit propagation + lineage chaining limitations documented; Option C target (per-EA snapshot, multi-grantee fan-out) deferred to production (backlog #160).
- **Phase 11D — Polish punch-list:** seven small accumulated polish items in a single commit. **#134:** `CombinedRequestModal` Step 1 PIN resolution gained an `already-disclosed` state — when the requester enters a PIN that resolves to a Claim already on their canvas via an active DA, Submit is disabled with copy steering the user toward the Detail Panel instead of firing a duplicate request. **#135:** counterparty-pulled Asset Detail Panel hides the FILE metadata + REGISTRATION sections — disclosure is directional, so non-owners see only the Asset's name, description, owner, and an "Open Evidence Viewer" button (which still grants viewer access via the existing Expand modal pattern). **#136:** provisional Claim cards expose a `✕ Cancel Request` action-bar button for the requester (non-owner of the provisional). The handler is async — it plays the unravel animation, drops the provisional artifacts (cold-path DA+EA pair OR warm-path EA only), and dismisses the responder's pending request notification. **#137:** cross-role notification indicators in the user menu — yellow dots on the chrome trigger button when any other role has un-dismissed notifications, plus per-role dots in the dropdown list. **#118:** `v22DataWithReveal` now skips `_isNew` stamping for Asset reveals where the active party owns the Asset — fixes the stale NEW badge that appeared on the requester's anchor Asset after the responder's session set the asset reveal id. **#119:** narrow user-facing terminology audit — "Evidence in scope" → "Assets in scope" in `V22RunEvaluationModal`; related copy aligned. **#138:** NEW badge persistence audit across all node-creation paths — confirmed all use `setV22RecentlyAcceptedClaimId` / `setV22RecentlyAcceptedAssetId` and rely on the V2App:2207 deselect-aware effect for clearing. No setTimeout-based premature clears found. No regressions introduced.

## Phase 11 (April 2026)

Major architectural work delivered across twelve sub-phases, summarised here at high level. Per-sub-phase details live in the Changelog above and in `CLAUDE-phase-log.md`.

- **11A:** ChipCo + Dave actor seed; Public Directory cluster rebrand from mock `ElectroGrid Ltd` to real `ChipCo` cluster with per-role visibility filter; corner ACTOR card matching parent-layer styling.
- **11B:** Cluster click → Claim materialization on directory layer (synthetic node via `buildClaimNodeForDirectoryMaterialization`); Detail Panel "Expand" modal restoration (`ExpandedArtifactModal.jsx`); iframe-based Asset evidence viewer; placeholder PDFs for ChipCo + Alice Assets in `/public/`.
- **11C:** DA/EA flow separation. Cold-path `CombinedRequestModal` is now two-step (Disclosure + Evaluation); warm-path single-step `EARequestModal` for grantees who already hold an active DA. New factories `makeProvisionalEvaluationAgreement` + `finalizeProvisionalEvaluationAgreement`. `CombinedResponseModal` extended with `eaOnlyMode`. Three new notification types in §7.4. Dave promoted to switchable role.
- **11C.1:** Acknowledgments architecture correction. Earlier 11C drafts placed `resultConfidentiality` + `attribution` as requester-authored EA terms — wrong shape. Fix: terms are responder-authored; the requester acknowledges pre-set commitments the Claim owner authored on the Claim's `acknowledgments[]` field (§10.3). EA carries `acknowledgmentsAccepted: [id, ...]` as audit trail.
- **11C.2 / 11C.3 / 11C.4 / 11C.5:** Reveal animation diagnosis and fixes. Wired the `_wasProvisional` flag (dead infrastructure since the V2.2 retreat); migrated reveal animation to `src/v2/animations/reveal.js`; extended edge `_showAsProvisional` stamping to incident edges during the reveal window; warm-path notification handler now triggers reveal via the cold-path predicate; decoupled `v22RecentlyAcceptedClaimId` (drives `_isNew + _wasProvisional` until deselect) from `v22RevealActiveClaimId` (drives `_showAsProvisional` for the reveal window only).
- **11D / 11D.1:** Polish punch-list — PIN-existing-Claim validation in cold path, counterparty Asset Detail Panel hiding file metadata, Cancel Request action-bar button, cross-role notification dots, NEW badge audit, Evidence → Assets terminology audit. Copy fixes follow-on in 11D.1.
- **11D.2:** Selective Disclosure grantee view restoration. Disclosed parsed-fields table in Expand modal Output tab; field counts in Claim Detail Panel referenced-Asset rows; disclosed-portion-only JSON tab. §10.4 grantee-view subsection added.
- **11D.3:** Proof-only Disclosure: Eval Result materialization on grantee canvases. New `proofOnlyPulledEvalIds` view-builder set; proof-only edge derivation branch; layout column for pulled-in Eval Results; clickable Evaluation Results rows in V22ClaimPanel; Eval Result Detail Panel adjustments (Owner section + linked Claim row).
- **11D.4 / 11D.4.1:** Layout placement refinement. `COL_PULLED_EVAL` moved 2300 → 1700 (Phase 11D.4) → derived placement `(sourceClaim.x + 200, sourceClaim.y + 300)` with `COL_Y_OFFSET` stacking (Phase 11D.4.1). Z-index hierarchy fix: AssetNode mini + dot tooltip portals dropped from `zIndex: 5000` → `150` so they sit below the Detail Panel (200). Referenced Assets count corrected to use the filtered array length, not the Claim's raw `referencedAssetIds.length`.

The three disclosure types (Full, Selective, Proof-only) are now properly enforced end-to-end across canvas, edges, panels, and modals, with documented grantee-view derivation rules in §10.4. Dave (ChipCo) joins Alice / Bob / Carol as a fourth switchable demo role enabling the warm-path EA-only response flow to be exercised end-to-end.

---

## Sections requiring expansion (Phase 11.5 audit)

The 2026-04-30 hygiene-pass audit walked every section. Substantial expansions deferred for future writing passes:

- **§14 Implementation Guidelines** — written during V2.2 migration; references stale (`V2_2_ENABLED` flag, V2.1 retention, `v2Data.js`). Inline note added at top of section pointing readers at `CLAUDE.md` for current conventions; full rewrite or removal deferred.
- **§12 V2.1 → V2.2 Migration Map** — historical document of the migration plan; preserved as-is for archive value but not reflective of current architecture (V2.1 deleted in Phase 8). No action needed unless referenced material becomes confusing.
- **§13 Phased Implementation Plan** — likewise archive-value. Phase 1–8 details preserved; Phase 9–11 spec coverage lives inline in the section bodies (§3.7, §6.5, §10.4, §11.5a, §11.6a, etc.) and in the Changelog above.
- **§15+ open questions** — not re-audited this pass. Future hygiene pass could reconcile against shipped reality.
- **Acknowledgments architecture cross-reference** — §10.3 + §10.5 + §11.6a + Changelog all cover this comprehensively. No expansion stub needed.
- **Reveal animation primitive** — Phase 11C.3 migrated the primitive to `src/v2/animations/reveal.js` parallel to `unravel.js`. Both are summarised in spec §3 and §11.5a; the per-stage timing details + state-var split (`v22RecentlyAcceptedClaimId` vs `v22RevealActiveClaimId`) live in `CLAUDE-phase-log.md`'s phase notes, not the spec, by design — the spec describes the architecture, not the React state machine.
